Motivation: 经典与量子Stein引理是假设检验与信息论的核心结果，但既有量子版本多限于特定代数或态族。本文旨在将其推广到任意von Neumann代数及更一般的凸、张量稳定态族，以扩大适用范围并刻画最坏情况错误指数。

Method: 证明结合三种技术：相对熵的hockey-stick散度积分表示、模检验界（modular testing bound），以及凸minimax论证。

Result: 在存在有限相对熵备择态的条件下，对任意type-I错误容限，最坏情况type-II错误指数由正则化相对熵达到，并满足强逆定理。

Conclusion: 该工作将量子Stein引理推广至任意von Neumann代数上的凸、张量稳定态族，给出了正则化相对熵作为最坏情况错误指数的精确刻画及强逆性质。

Abstract: We prove a generalized quantum Stein lemma for i.i.d. normal states against convex, tensor-stable families on arbitrary von Neumann algebras. Assuming the existence of an alternative state with finite relative entropy from the null state, we show that, at every type-I error tolerance $\varepsilon\in (0,1)$, the worst case type-II error exponent is achieved with the regularized relative entropy with a strong converse. The proof combines the integral representation of relative entropy by hockey-stick divergences, a modular testing bound, and convex minimax argument.

</details>


### [169] [Random Quantum Circuits Beyond Moment Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02135)
*Shih-Han Hung*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为强ε-近似酉k-设计在单个输出概率分布上的精度提供了最优Kolmogorov距离界，并证明局部不变性可带来指数级改进，同时应用于输出分布Shannon熵的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机量子电路旨在高效复现理想随机量子演化的统计性质，常用手段是构造近似酉设计（匹配Haar随机酉的矩）。然而，此类设计在多大程度上能复现单个输出概率的完整分布，此前缺乏定量保证。

Method: 将强ε-近似酉k-设计的输出概率分布与有限维Porter-Thomas分布（参数为1和2^n-1的beta分布）在Kolmogorov距离下进行比较分析；进一步引入在log k+O(1)量子比特上的酉平移不变性（局部不变性），并在总变差距离下给出更强保证。

Result: 对n量子比特上任意强ε-近似酉k-设计，每个输出概率分布与Porter-Thomas分布的Kolmogorov距离为O(√(2^n/(k(k+2^n)))+ε)，该界在常数因子意义下最优；局部不变设计将k依赖改进为指数级，误差为2^{-Ω(k)}+O(ε)（Kolmogorov）与2^{-Ω(k)}+O(εlog(2/ε))（总变差）。应用方面，若边缘分布与Haar对应量的Kolmogorov距离为η，则输出分布期望Shannon熵与Haar值之差为O(√η)。

Conclusion: 矩匹配所能保证的分布精度存在本质极限，而局部不变性是显著提升精度的额外结构；该框架还可用于界定输出分布Shannon熵的偏差。

Abstract: Random quantum circuits aim to efficiently reproduce the statistical properties of ideal random quantum evolution. One approach is to construct approximate unitary designs, which match the moments of Haar-random unitaries up to a prescribed order with controlled error. In this work, we establish quantitative guarantees for how accurately these designs reproduce the full distributions of individual output probabilities. We show that, for every strong $\varepsilon$-approximate unitary $k$-design on $n$ qubits, the distribution of each individual output probability is within $O\left(\sqrt{\frac{2^n}{k(k+2^n)}}+\varepsilon\right)$ in Kolmogorov distance of the finite-dimensional Porter-Thomas distribution, a beta distribution with parameters $1$ and $2^n-1$. This bound is optimal up to constant factors, implying that a substantially better uniform bound requires additional structure. We further show that local invariance yields an exponential improvement in the dependence on $k$, with guarantees also in the stronger metric of total variation: every strong $\varepsilon$-approximate unitary $k$-design invariant under unitary translations acting on $\log k+O(1)$ qubits achieves error $2^{-Ω(k)}+O(\varepsilon)$ in Kolmogorov distance and $2^{-Ω(k)}+O(\varepsilon\log(2/\varepsilon))$ in total variation distance. These results identify both the distributional accuracy guaranteed by moment matching and the additional structure that substantially improves it. As an application, we show that if the marginal distribution of each output probability of a strong approximate design is within Kolmogorov distance $η$ of its Haar counterpart, then the expected Shannon entropy of the output distribution differs from its Haar value by $O(\sqrtη)$.

</details>


### [170] [A provable quantum advantage for approximate optimization via decoded quantum interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02145)
*Maximilian J. Kramer,Elies Gil-Fuster,Benjamin D. M. Jones,Jens Eisert,Franz J. Schreiber*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在oracle框架下证明了DQI相较于所有多项式时间经典算法具有严格逼近优势，并提出改进算法进一步扩大该量子分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DQI（解码量子干涉）是一种求解近似优化问题的新范式，具有强性能保证并利用优化与编码理论之间的对偶性。核心问题是：DQI能否可证明地超越所有多项式时间经典算法。本文旨在建立这种优势。

Method: 构造名为folded OPI（折叠最优多项式交集）的优化任务，其接受集随机选取并通过成员oracle访问；基于Jordan等人的DQI框架，并将Yamakawa与Zhandry精确搜索oracle分离所依赖的经典下界方法扩展到近似情形；结合Sun-Wootters、Horinaga-Yamakawa与Jo的近期工作，构造改进版DQI算法。

Result: 在任意多项式时间经典算法可达逼近比与DQI算法可达逼近比之间建立了严格gap；改进版DQI在folded OPI上取得更大gap。具体地，码率0.3时DQI与改进算法期望得分约为0.85和0.95，而经典算法以常数概率超越0.65阈值则需超多项式多次成员查询。

Conclusion: DQI在oracle设定下可证明地优于所有多项式时间经典算法，且改进算法带来更强量子分离，为量子近似优化的优势提供了新的理论证据。

Abstract: Decoded quantum interferometry (DQI) is a novel paradigm for tackling approximate optimization problems on quantum computers. This framework comes with strong performance guarantees and exploits a well-established duality between optimization and coding theory. A central question, however, is whether DQI can actually provably outperform all polynomial-time classical algorithms. In this work, we establish such an advantage in an oracle setting: we consider an optimization task called folded optimal polynomial intersection (folded OPI), where the acceptance sets are chosen randomly and accessed through membership oracles. We establish a strict gap between the approximation ratio achievable by any polynomial-time classical algorithm and the approximation ratio achieved by the DQI algorithm. Our proof builds on Jordan et al.'s DQI framework for approximate optimization and extends the classical lower-bound method underlying Yamakawa and Zhandry's exact-search oracle separation to approximation. Building on recent developments by Sun and Wootters, Horinaga and Yamakawa, and Jo, we further show that a modified version of the DQI algorithm achieves a strictly larger gap on the folded OPI problem, yielding an even stronger quantum separation. As a concrete example, for code rate $0.3$, DQI and the modified algorithm achieve expected scores of approximately $0.85$ and $0.95$, respectively. In contrast, exceeding the classical threshold of $0.65$ by any fixed amount with constant probability on sampled instances requires super-polynomially many classical membership queries.

</details>


### [171] [Polynomial-time additive-error estimation of output probabilities for shallow quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02146)
*Matthew Coudron,Michael J. Gullans,Jon Nelson,Joel Rajakumar,Shi Jie Samuel Tan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个确定性经典算法，可在多项式时间内以加性误差估计常数深度量子电路在特定输出态上的概率幅平方值，显著改进了此前最优算法的时间复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 估计常数深度量子电路输出概率幅是量子计算与经典模拟交叉领域的重要问题。此前最优经典算法在一般电路下需要准多项式甚至更高时间，作者希望降低这一复杂度，逼近多项式时间。

Method: 采用确定性经典算法，针对由有界扇入门构成的常数深度量子电路（支持任意连通性），直接估计目标输出串 x 对应概率幅的模平方值。

Result: 算法可在 poly(n, 1/ε) 时间内以加性误差 ε 估计 |⟨x|U|0^n⟩|^2。相比此前一般电路 n^{O(log n)}、几何局部电路 n^{O(log log n)} 以及二维几何局部电路 n^{O(1)} 的复杂度，本文方法在一般常数深度电路上达到多项式时间。

Conclusion: 该工作表明常数深度、任意连通性的量子电路输出概率幅可通过确定性经典算法在多项式时间内高效估计，统一并改进了现有各类局部性假设下的结果。

Abstract: We give a deterministic classical algorithm that estimates $|\langle x|U|0^n\rangle|^2$ to additive error $\varepsilon$ in $\mathrm{poly}(n, 1/\varepsilon)$ time, where $U$ is a constant-depth quantum circuit comprised of gates with bounded fan-in and arbitrary connectivity, and $x$ is an arbitrary $n$-bit output string. This improves over prior state-of-the-art algorithms that takes $n^{O(log(n))}$ time for the same task, $n^{O(log(log(n))}$ when $U$ is geometrically local, and $n^{O(1)}$ for 2D geometrically-local circuits.

</details>


### [172] [The Robustness of QAC0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02154)
*Daniel Grier,Jackson Morris,Kewen Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究 $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ 的稳健性，证明其固有误差可被完全消除，且其计算能力对单比特门集的限制具有稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究 $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ 电路计算布尔函数时通常允许的非零误差是否必要，以及其计算能力在多大程度上依赖于任意单比特门的使用。

Method: 通过在多样本背景下新颖地应用精确幅度放大，消除并行 $W$-测试中的固有误差；并构造由广义 Toffoli、$S$ 和 Hadamard 门组成的近似电路。

Result: $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ 可精确模拟 $\mathsf{TC}^0$（使用多项式多个经典输入副本）；对每个固定素数 $p$，精确 $\mathsf{QAC}^0$（即 $\mathsf{EQAC}^0$）可计算 $\mathsf{AC}^0[p]$ 之外的全布尔函数；每个 $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ 电路可由仅含广义 Toffoli、$S$、Hadamard 门的 $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ 电路近似实现，且该近似电路可高效构造。

Conclusion: $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ 对误差容忍和门集限制均具有稳健性：其误差可被完全消除，且其计算能力不依赖于任意单比特门的使用。

Abstract: In this work we study the robustness of $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ with respect to error tolerance and modifications to its gate-set. First, we investigate whether the non-zero error typically allowed for $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ circuits computing Boolean functions is truly necessary. We show that the error inherent in the parallel $W$-test of \cite{grier_morris_wu} can be eliminated entirely via a novel application of exact amplitude amplification in the many-copies context. Consequently, we find that $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ can \textit{exactly} simulate $\mathsf{TC}^0$ with polynomially many copies of the classical input and that for every fixed prime $p$ exact $\mathsf{QAC}^0$, $\mathsf{EQAC}^0$, can compute total Boolean functions outside of $\mathsf{AC}^0[p]$.
  Second, we ask to what extent the computational power of $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ follows from the fact that arbitrary single-qubit gates may be used at any point in the circuit. We find that $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ is in fact robust to restrictions on which single-qubit gates are permitted: every $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ circuit can be approximately implemented by a $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ circuit consisting of just generalized Toffoli, $S$, and Hadamard gates. Moreover, this approximating circuit can be constructed efficiently from a classical description of the original circuit.

</details>


### [173] [Learning Many-Body Hamiltonians Using a Local Probe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02157)
*Suying Liu,Zitai Xu,Alexey V. Gorshkov,Xiaodi Wu,Yu-Xin Wang,Zhi-Yuan Wei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出仅用单个可测量子比特，在连接参考骨干与快速单比特控制下，以Heisenberg极限精度学习N量子比特有界度两体哈密顿量的全部O(N)个独立参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有哈密顿量学习协议大多假设能够直接测量整个系统。论文旨在突破这一测量接口限制，探索在只有局部（单比特）测量能力时，是否仍能高效、高精度地学习多体哈密顿量的全部参数。

Method: 利用量子信号处理合成的鲁棒SWAP门，将远端哈密顿量参数作用下演化的量子态相干传输到唯一可测量子比特，无需对中间链路的哈密顿量参数做预校准；并设计并行学习架构，在N量子比特链上实现总查询时间Õ(N)。

Result: 在N量子比特链上，协议以Heisenberg极限精度学习全部O(N)个独立参数，总查询时间为Õ(N)；该标度在链上达到基本精度与信息传播下界的对数因子内最优，并可推广到任意有界度相互作用图。

Conclusion: 结果表明，仅通过局部测量接口即可学习大量的多体哈密顿量参数，建立了可扩展的哈密顿量学习路径，为受限测量条件下重建未知量子动力学提供了新框架。

Abstract: Hamiltonian learning provides a systematic framework for reconstructing unknown quantum dynamics. However, existing protocols typically assume direct measurement access to the entire system. With fast single-qubit control and a connected reference backbone, we show that a single measurable qubit suffices to learn all $O(N)$ independent parameters of a bounded-degree two-body Hamiltonian on $N$ qubits at the Heisenberg limit. Crucially, our protocol uses robust SWAP gates synthesized by quantum signal processing, enabling coherent transfer of states evolving under distant Hamiltonian parameters to the measurable qubit. This transfer requires no prior calibration of the Hamiltonian parameters of the intermediate links. A parallel learning architecture achieves total query time $\widetilde{O}(N)$ for an $N$-qubit chain, while retaining Heisenberg-limited precision scaling. On the chain, these scalings match the fundamental precision and information-propagation lower bounds up to logarithmic factors. The framework further extends to arbitrary bounded-degree interaction graphs. Our results establish a scalable route to learning an extensive number of many-body Hamiltonian parameters through only a local measurement interface.

</details>


### [174] [Robust exponential lower bounds for fermionic and bosonic Gaussian ranks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02172)
*Fuchuan Wei,Kong-Wing Wu,Zhengwei Liu,Zi-Wen Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了玻色子和费米子系统中的高斯版本分解秩问题，对高斯秩建立了稳健的指数下界，证明非高斯性普遍意味着指数级高斯分解复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典模拟的能力与局限是理解量子计算优势的核心。基于向经典可处理自由态作相干分解的模拟范式，其分解秩决定模拟复杂度，而对这一数量证明强下界是极为困难且数学上丰富的问题（如量子比特稳定子秩问题）。作者希望在高斯情形下研究该问题。

Method: 结合两种技术：对费米子，将问题归约到四模并结合马约拉纳谱界；对玻色子，利用高斯后选择配合基于熵的秩界。同时在玻色和费米系统中研究高斯秩。

Result: 证明了对于有限模上的任意纯非高斯态（费米情形需具确定宇称），其张量幂的近似边界高斯秩在任意低于1的固定范数误差下至少指数增长。作为具体例子，得到四模费米GHZ态和玻色单光子态的显式指数下界；玻色结果无需对目标态能量作任何假设。

Conclusion: 非高斯性普遍导致指数级高斯分解复杂度，为基于分解的玻色子和费米子系统经典模拟设定了根本性限制。

Abstract: The power and limitations of classical simulation are central to understanding quantum computational advantages. A leading simulation paradigm is based on coherent decomposition into classically tractable free states where the decomposition rank determines the simulation complexity. Proving strong lower bounds on this number is a notoriously difficult and mathematically rich problem, as exemplified by the qubit stabilizer rank problem. Here, we study the Gaussian version of this problem in both bosonic and fermionic systems and establish robust exponential lower bounds on Gaussian rank. In particular, we prove that for every pure non-Gaussian state on finitely many modes, with definite parity in the fermionic case, the approximate border Gaussian rank of its tensor powers grows at least exponentially at any fixed norm error below one. Our proofs combine reduction to four modes with Majorana spectral bounds for fermions, and Gaussian postselection with an entropy-based rank bound for bosons. As concrete examples, we derive explicit exponential lower bounds for the four-mode fermionic GHZ state and the bosonic single-photon state. The bosonic results require no assumption on the energy of the target state. Our results show that non-Gaussianity universally entails exponential Gaussian decomposition complexity, setting fundamental limitations on decomposition-based classical simulation of bosonic and fermionic systems.

</details>


### [175] [From Permutation Symmetry to Communication Bounds and Additivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02176)
*Zahra Baghali Khanian,Debbie Leung,Graeme Smith*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明全置换不变性将量子信道跨次相关带来的优势限制为有限块长效应：优化相干信息每用次收敛到单次最大值，并由此导出若干通信与熵的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨信道使用的相关性可提升量子通信速率，因此量子容量的确定需要对任意大块长进行优化，这是核心困难。本文旨在刻画置换不变性如何限制这种优势。

Method: 利用全置换不变性约束、De Finetti约化与置换协变性，在统一变分框架下分析相干信息、完全有界范数、夹心Rényi信道量以及输出熵。

Result: 优化后的每用次相干信息收敛到单次最大值并在有限块长处取到上确界；有限块超可加性仍可能存在，而渐近优化归约为单次信道使用。对置换不变输入的纯源纠缠生成给出强逆定理：速率高于单次相干信息最大值时保真度趋于零。同时给出完全有界范数与夹心Rényi信道量（判别替代者、纠缠辅助通信）的共同结构及其乘性与可加性结果。置换不变输入下每用次输出熵不能渐近低于单次最小值。

Conclusion: 置换不变性将跨次相关的作用限制在有限块长，渐近通信与熵优化均由单次使用决定；对称性在通信界与输出熵控制之间建立了统一联系。

Abstract: Correlations across channel uses can improve quantum communication rates, making optimization over arbitrarily large blocks a central difficulty in determining quantum capacity. We show how full permutation invariance limits this advantage for every finite-dimensional memoryless channel. The optimized coherent information per use converges to the single-use maximum and consequently attains its supremum at a finite block length. Thus finite-block superadditivity remains possible, while the asymptotic optimization reduces to a single channel use. We also establish a strong converse for pure-source entanglement generation with permutation-invariant inputs: at any fixed rate above the single-use coherent-information maximum, the fidelity tends to zero. Beyond these communication bounds, we identify a common structure underlying completely bounded norms and the sandwiched Rényi channel quantities governing discrimination against a replacer and entanglement-assisted communication. De Finetti reduction and permutation covariance yield systematic alternative proofs of their known multiplicativity and additivity results within a shared variational framework. We further show that, for permutation-invariant inputs, correlations cannot sustain an asymptotic reduction in output entropy per use below the single-use minimum. This entropy limit holds for Rényi orders greater than one and for the von Neumann entropy, extending the role of symmetry from communication bounds to the control of output entropy.

</details>


### [176] [Learning SYK Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02178)
*Anurag Anshu,Srinivasan Arunachalam,Sitan Chen,Yeongwoo Hwang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究从Gibbs态副本中学习稠密SYK哈密顿量的问题，突破传统方法对几何局域性或有限相互作用度的依赖，实现了多项式样本学习与拟多项式时间学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有哈密顿量学习算法通常依赖几何局域性或有限的相互作用度，而SYK模型中每个四次相互作用与Θ(n^3)个其他项重叠，导致这些方法失效。因此需要利用SYK随机平均场结构克服这一障碍。

Method: 利用SYK模型的随机平均场结构，从Gibbs态副本中学习哈密顿量。在任意常数温度下，证明随SYK耦合的高概率下，可用多项式数量样本将整个哈密顿量学到逆多项式精度。此外，当逆温度限制为足够小的常数时，构造拟多项式时间学习算法。

Result: 证明在任意常数温度下，以高概率关于SYK耦合，可用多项式数量样本将整个哈密顿量学习到逆多项式精度；当逆温度为足够小常数时，得到拟多项式时间学习算法，与样本高效算法在性质上不同。

Conclusion: SYK稠密哈密顿量可从其Gibbs态副本中有效学习，随机平均场结构可克服Θ(n^3)重叠导致的障碍，样本复杂度为多项式级别，且在低温限制下存在拟多项式时间算法。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning the dense Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev (SYK) Hamiltonian from copies of its Gibbs state. Existing algorithms for Hamiltonian learning typically rely on geometric locality or bounded interaction degree and therefore do not apply to SYK, where each quartic interaction overlaps with $Θ(n^3)$ others. We show that this obstruction can be overcome by exploiting the random mean-field structure of the model. At any constant temperature, we prove that with high probability over the SYK couplings, the entire Hamiltonian can be learned to inverse-polynomial accuracy using polynomially many samples. Furthermore, when the inverse temperature is restricted to be a sufficiently small constant, we construct a quasipolynomial-time learning algorithm which is qualitatively different from the sample-efficient algorithm.

</details>


### [177] [Single-Particle Spectral Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02183)
*Adrian Chapman,Charles Derby,Steven T. Flammia,Yeongwoo Hwang,Joel Klassen,Calum McCartney*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在被全局酉变换隐藏物理结构的设定下的哈密顿量学习问题，证明其DQC1-困难，并提出SPICES算法高效恢复谱分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料建模中的经典方法（如平均场理论、格林函数、密度泛函理论）依赖于无相互作用结构的哈密顿量，但真实系统中该结构常被未知的全局酉变换所隐藏，因此需要在不重建全局酉变换U的前提下恢复单粒子能量权重。

Method: 定义了受全局酉变换U隐藏的无相互作用模式集合I与旁观项H_O构成的哈密顿量学习任务，证明该问题DQC1-困难；随后提出SPICES（single-particle inference via contour estimators），将Hadamard测试与新颖的经典后处理相结合，利用单粒子能量与复逆温度下配分函数零点所对应的傅里叶频率之间的对应关系来恢复谱分布。

Result: 该学习问题被证明为DQC1-困难，尽管存在表面上的无相互作用简化；SPICES在自然假设下能高效地在Wasserstein距离意义下恢复谱分布。

Conclusion: 由于准粒子谱在后续材料计算中普遍存在，且SPICES简单实用，作者预期其是通向材料模拟中量子应用的一条有前景的路径。

Abstract: We study Hamiltonian learning in a novel setting, where we are promised a physical structure that is obfuscated by a global unitary. In particular, we consider the task of learning the weights $E_i$ of a Hamiltonian $H=U^{\dagger}(\sum_{i \in I} E_i n_i + H_O)U$ comprised of a set of non-interacting modes $I$ and an arbitrary ``spectator'' $H_O$ acting on a disjoint set of modes $O$. The non-interacting structure is hidden by an unknown global unitary $U$, and our goal is to recover the $E_i$ without reconstructing $U$. Such Hamiltonians are well-motivated in the setting of materials modeling, where they find application for classical methods such as mean-field theory, Green's functions, and density functional theory. We show that this problem is DQC1-hard, despite the apparent simplification given by the non-interacting structure. We then introduce SPICES (single-particle inference via contour estimators), which combines a Hadamard test with a novel classical postprocessing procedure to efficiently recover the spectral distribution in Wasserstein distance under certain natural assumptions. The method exploits the correspondence between single-particle energies of the individual quasiparticles and the Fourier frequencies governing zeros of the partition function at complex inverse temperature. As quasiparticle spectra feature ubiquitously in downstream materials calculations, and with SPICES being simple and practical to execute, we expect that SPICES is a promising route to quantum applications in materials simulation.

</details>


<div id='math.QA'></div>

# math.QA [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [Rectangular Cyclotomic Expansions of Colored HOMFLY-PT Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00303)
*Honghuai Fang,Tian Zhou*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 该论文证明了Kameyama-Nawata-Tao-Zhang提出的矩形分圆展开猜想，并给出了任意零标架纽结的约化着色HOMFLY-PT多项式的显式双扭转公式，同时构造了独立于矩形颜色宽高的双牛顿展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对约化着色HOMFLY-PT多项式，验证Kameyama、Nawata、Tao与Zhang提出的矩形分圆展开猜想及其显式双扭转公式，从而推广相关分圆展开结果至任意零标架纽结与任意矩形颜色。

Method: 利用有限秩表示对称性与整性插值构造双牛顿展开；通过两个四点Casimir算子的同时外幂比较，并结合已知的单行公式推导双扭转公式；用有限转移公式将双扭转纽结的双牛顿系数表示为给定扭转系数。

Result: 证明了矩形分圆展开猜想及双扭转公式（适用于每个矩形颜色与所有非零整数扭转参数对）；对每个纽结构造出系数属于$\mathbb Z[A^{\pm1},q^{\pm1}]$的双牛顿展开，且系数唯一确定，不依赖于矩形颜色的宽与高。

Conclusion: 矩形分圆展开猜想与显式双扭转公式成立，双牛顿系数具有良好的唯一性与颜色无关性，为着色HOMFLY-PT多项式的分圆展开结构提供了统一的代数框架。

Abstract: We prove the rectangular cyclotomic expansion conjecture of Kameyama, Nawata, Tao, and Zhang for reduced colored HOMFLY-PT polynomials of arbitrary zero-framed knots. We also prove their explicit double-twist formula for every rectangular color and all pairs of nonzero integer twist parameters. For each knot, we construct a double Newton expansion with coefficients in $\mathbb Z[A^{\pm1},q^{\pm1}]$. These coefficients are uniquely determined and independent of the width and height of the rectangular color. The construction uses finite-rank representation symmetries and integral interpolation. The double-twist formula follows from a simultaneous exterior-power comparison of the two four-point Casimir operators and the known one-row formula. Finite transition formulas express the double Newton coefficients of double-twist knots in terms of the prescribed twist coefficients.

</details>


### [179] [Ordinary modules for affine vertex operator superalgebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01774)
*Huaimin Li,Qing Wang*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文研究基本经典李超代数对应的仿射李超代数，在边界可容许水平 $k$ 下证明了 Cartan 子代数在 $L_{\widehat{\mathfrak{g}}}(k,0)$ 的 ordinary 模上半单作用，进而得出 ordinary 模范畴 $\mathcal{O}_{k}^{ord}(\mathfrak{g})$ 有限、半单，并等于有限长广义模范畴 $KL_k(\mathfrak{g})$，最终证明其为 ribbon fusion 超范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究仿射顶点算子超代数在边界可容许水平下 ordinary 模范畴的结构与张量性质，是该领域的核心问题。作者希望刻画该范畴的半单性、有限性及其与已知范畴 $KL_k(\mathfrak{g})$ 的关系，并确定其是否具备 ribbon fusion 结构。

Method: 设 $\mathfrak{g}$ 为基本经典李超代数，$\widehat{\mathfrak{g}}$ 为对应仿射李超代数。首先证明 Cartan 子代数在简单仿射顶点算子超代数 $L_{\widehat{\mathfrak{g}}}(k,0)$ 的 ordinary 模上作用半单（$k$ 为边界可容许水平）；随后证明 ordinary 模范畴 $\mathcal{O}_{k}^{ord}(\mathfrak{g})$ 有限、半单，并等于有限长广义模范畴 $KL_k(\mathfrak{g})$；在此基础上进一步讨论其张量结构与刚性。

Result: $\mathcal{O}_{k}^{ord}(\mathfrak{g})$ 是有限、半单范畴，且恰等于 $KL_k(\mathfrak{g})$，因而是 braided tensor 超范畴；进一步证明其刚性，故为 ribbon 超范畴；最终得到 $\mathcal{O}_{k}^{ord}(\mathfrak{g})$ 是 ribbon fusion 超范畴。

Conclusion: 在边界可容许水平下，ordinary 模范畴 $\mathcal{O}_{k}^{ord}(\mathfrak{g})$ 具有有限、半单、braided、ribbon 及 fusion 等良好性质，统一并深化了对仿射顶点算子超代数表示范畴的理解。

Abstract: Let $\mathfrak{g}$ be a basic classical Lie superalgebra and let $\widehat{\mathfrak{g}}$ be the corresponding affine Lie superalgebra. In this paper, we first prove that a Cartan subalgebra acts semisimply on ordinary modules for the simple affine vertex operator superalgebra $L_{\widehat{\mathfrak{g}}}(k,0)$ at boundary admissible level $k$. Then we prove that the category $\mathcal{O}_{k}^{ord}(\mathfrak{g})$ of ordinary $L_{\widehat{\mathfrak{g}}}(k,0)$-modules is finite, semisimple and $\mathcal{O}_{k}^{ord}(\mathfrak{g})$ is exactly the category $KL_k(\mathfrak{g})$ of finite-length generalized modules for the affine vertex operator superalgebra $L_{\widehat{\mathfrak{g}}}(k,0)$.Thus $\mathcal{O}_{k}^{ord}(\mathfrak{g})$ is a braided tensor supercategory. Furthermore, we obtain the rigidity of the supercategory $\mathcal{O}_{k}^{ord}(\mathfrak{g})$ and thus it is a ribbon supercategory. Finally, we conclude that $\mathcal{O}_{k}^{ord}(\mathfrak{g})$ is a ribbon fusion supercategory.

</details>


### [180] [A Non Commutative Grauert Theorem and Fourier Mukai Duality for Generalized Complex Tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01945)
*Patrick Antweiler,Jonathan Block*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文证明了一类弯曲微分分次非交换Fréchet代数上Grauert高阶相干定理的推广，并借此将Fourier-Mukai演算拓展到新的导出范畴语境，最终用于证明对偶广义复环面导出范畴的等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fourier-Mukai演算和对偶性理论此前主要局限于特定几何语境（如复环面、辛环面）。作者希望将其推广至广义复几何，包括非交换与B-field形变的框架，从而为广义复环面上的brane范畴奠定基础。

Method: 首先证明Grauert高阶相干定理在一类弯曲微分分次非交换Fréchet代数上的推广；随后构造非交换版本的Poincaré线丛，并应用上述推广结果，在广义复环面上实施Fourier-Mukai演算。

Result: 成功证明了（对偶）广义复环面导出范畴之间的等价性，并展示了该框架对复环面、辛环面及其非交换与B-field形变的适用性。

Conclusion: 该工作将Fourier-Mukai演算拓展至广义复几何的新语境，为定义广义复环面上的广义复brane范畴提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We prove a generalization of Grauert's higher coherence theorem for a class of curved differential graded (non-commutative) Fréchet algebras. This allows us to extend the Fourier-Mukai calculus to derived categories arising in many new contexts. We then apply it and prove equivalence of derived categories of dual generalized complex tori using a non-commutative version of the Poincaré line bundle. This lays the foundation for categories of generalized complex branes on generalized complex tori. Examples include complex tori, symplectic tori as well as their non-commutative and B-field deformations.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [Direct Group Laws on Classical Genus-One Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00029)
*Hongfeng Wu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文利用“残差相交”在多种椭圆曲线模型上构造群律，并给出统一且显式的结合性证明方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为短Weierstrass三次曲线、Edwards曲线等多种椭圆曲线模型给出统一、几何化的群律构造与结合性证明，并解决特征2、3下的显式公式问题。

Method: 采用残差相交构造群律：平面三次曲线用直线定义运算，PP^3中两个二次曲面光滑交用平面定义运算；结合性通过比较三个直线/平面方程的两个乘积证明，消去公共零点后，由亏格一的平滑射影曲线不存在恰有一个单极点的非常值有理函数这一基本事实推出剩余零点相等。对 (u^2+u)(v^2+v)=d 给出任意特征均有效的公式并在特征2、3显式写出，其余模型在特征不等于2、3时处理。

Result: 在所列各曲线模型上成功构造出群律并证明结合性；得到 (u^2+u)(v^2+v)=d 在任意特征下有效、并在特征2和3显式表达的公式；其余模型在特征≠2,3下得到处理。

Conclusion: 残差相交为多种椭圆曲线模型提供了统一的群律构造与结合性证明框架，且能覆盖特征2、3等特殊情形。

Abstract: This exposition constructs group laws by residual intersection on short Weierstrass cubics, the curves $(u^2+u)(v^2+v)=d$, Edwards curves, Hessian curves, Jacobi quartics curves, Jacobi intersections curves, Huff curves, and a symmetric QRT biquadratic. Lines give the operation on plane cubics, while planes give the operation on smooth intersections of two quadrics in $\PP^3$. Associativity is proved by comparing two products of three line or plane equations. After the common zeros are cancelled, equality of the remaining zeros follows from the elementary fact that a smooth projective curve of genus one admits no nonconstant rational function with one simple pole. The formulas for $(u^2+u)(v^2+v)=d$ are valid in every characteristic and are written explicitly in characteristics $2$ and $3$; the other models are treated in characteristic different from $2$ and $3$.

</details>


### [182] [Categorical Torelli theorem for (weighted) hypersurfaces via singularity category](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00130)
*Xun Lin,Jieheng Zeng,Shizhuo Zhang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 利用完备仿射锥的奇异范畴，对普通与加权超曲面证明了范畴Torelli定理；Fano情形用Kuznetsov分量，一般型情形用分次矩阵分解全范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一处理Fano与一般型超曲面的范畴Torelli问题，并覆盖加权情形，弥补以往结果在数值假设与一般型超曲面上缺乏统一证明的不足。

Method: 取完备仿射锥的奇异范畴。Fano超曲面使用Kuznetsov分量；一般型超曲面使用分次矩阵分解全范畴。当次数与权重和互素时，将内部分次平移用Serre函子与上同调平移表达，从而其dg轨道是内蕴的，取perfect hull后与完备锥的dg奇异范畴一致。再通过范畴Mather–Yau定理恢复形式奇异点。

Result: 对普通齐次方程，所得形式等价诱导射影线性等价；在加权情形，对偶环面作用决定Tjurina代数的分次，进而决定加权齐次方程。由此在给定数值假设下给出Fano超曲面的统一证明，并获得一般型超曲面的新范畴Torelli定理。

Conclusion: 奇异范畴与分次矩阵分解提供了统一框架，使Fano与一般型超曲面的范畴Torelli定理（含加权情形）得以成立并能重构几何数据。

Abstract: We prove categorical Torelli theorems for ordinary and weighted hypersurfaces using the singularity category of the completed affine cone. For Fano hypersurfaces we use the Kuznetsov component, whereas for hypersurfaces of general type we use the full category of graded matrix factorizations. When the degree is coprime to the sum of the weights, the internal grading shift can be expressed in terms of the Serre functor and the cohomological shift. Its dg orbit is therefore intrinsic and, after taking the perfect hull, agrees with the dg singularity category of the completed cone. The formal singularity is then recovered by the categorical Mather--Yau theorem. For ordinary homogeneous equations, the resulting formal equivalence induces a projective linear equivalence; in the weighted setting the dual torus action determines the grading of the Tjurina algebra and hence the weighted homogeneous equation. The argument gives a uniform proof for Fano hypersurfaces under the stated numerical assumptions and proves new categorical Torelli theorems for hypersurfaces of general type.

</details>


### [183] [Completed cycle transfer and the conifold gap for (P^2, E)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00179)
*Xiaobin Li*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文证明了连通椭圆完备循环迹的正则性定理，并借此建立射影平面在光滑三次曲线边界与最高Hodge类插入下的Nekrasov-Shatashvili锥流形间隙（conifold gap），进而以三角归纳将已知的局部射影平面间隙转移到相对理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锥流形边界条件虽约束高亏格拓扑弦自由能，但其次主导极点的抵消并不由主导奇异性行为所蕴含，因此需要额外的正则性机制来保证极点逐层相消。该文旨在为"局部/相对对应"下的相对Gromov-Witten理论提供严格的极点抵消与间隙证明。

Method: 1) 证明连通椭圆完备循环迹的正则性定理；2) 利用高亏格局部/相对对应，将普通势用相对势与低亏格微分贡献表示，并把其锥流形极化修正识别为椭圆迹；3) 通过中心差分恒等式，将每个非真空配分权重新求和为"内容乘积×正则指数"；4) 对任意正则输入势部分与具有单零点的nome，证明归一化非真空连通贡献可被锥流形坐标与耦合平方整除；5) 由三角归纳把已知的局部射影平面间隙传递到相对理论。

Result: 证明了Nekrasov-Shatashvili锥流形间隙：归一化非真空连通贡献可被锥流形坐标与耦合平方整除，从而整体极部分与有限部分完全由真空常数决定；给出有限部分恒等式、正则Taylor系数的有限公式，并重构全纯歧义；每一固定亏格下给出全纯余项。

Conclusion: 该论证在耦合上是形式的（formal），可在每个固定亏格给出全纯余项，从而将局部射影平面的间隙结果推广至带光滑三次边界与最高Hodge类插入的相对Gromov-Witten理论。

Abstract: Conifold boundary conditions constrain higher genus topological string free energies, but the cancellation of subleading poles is not implied by their leading singular behavior. We prove a regularity theorem for connected elliptic completed cycle traces and use it to establish the Nekrasov-Shatashvili conifold gap for the maximal contact relative Gromov-Witten theory of the projective plane with a smooth cubic boundary and a top Hodge class insertion. The higher genus local/relative correspondence expresses the ordinary potential in terms of the relative potential and differentiated lower genus contributions. We identify its conifold polarized correction with an elliptic trace. A central difference identity resums each non-vacuum partition weight into a content product multiplied by a regular exponential. For arbitrary regular parts of the input potentials and any nome with a simple zero, the normalized non-vacuum connected contribution is divisible by the conifold coordinate and by the square of the coupling. The entire polar part and finite part are consequently determined by the vacuum constants. A triangular induction transfers the known ordinary local projective plane gap to the relative theory. We also obtain finite part identities, finite formulas for regular Taylor coefficients, and reconstruction of the holomorphic ambiguities. The argument is formal in the coupling and gives holomorphic remainders at every fixed genus.

</details>


### [184] [A characterization of the Ree curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00189)
*Saeed Tafazolian*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明达到特定有理点数与亏格上界的 $\mathbb{F}_q$ 上光滑射影几何不可约曲线必与 Ree 曲线 $\mathbb{F}_q$-同构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ree 曲线在有限域上以极高有理点数和特定亏格著称，是函数域与编码理论中的极值对象。本文旨在刻画该极值曲线的唯一性：若一条曲线同时达到 $q^3+1$ 个有理点和相应的亏格，则它是否必然是 Ree 曲线。

Method: 取 $q=3q_0^2$，$q_0=3^s$，$s\ge3$。设定曲线的有理点数为 $q^3+1$，亏格为 $\frac{3}{2}q_0(q-1)(q+q_0+1)$，通过有理点数与亏格约束及曲线同构理论进行论证。

Result: 满足上述有理点数与亏格的 $\mathbb{F}_q$ 上光滑射影几何不可约曲线，被证明与 Ree 曲线 $\mathbb{F}_q$-同构。

Conclusion: 该结果给出 Ree 曲线在给定有理点数与亏格参数下的唯一性刻画（即在相应参数条件下，极值曲线只能同构于 Ree 曲线）。

Abstract: Let $q=3q_0^2$, where $q_0=3^s$ and $s\ge3$. We prove that a smooth, projective, geometrically irreducible curve over $\F_q$ with $q^3+1$ rational points and genus \[
  \frac32q_0(q-1)(q+q_0+1) \] is $\F_q$-isomorphic to the Ree curve.

</details>


### [185] [All Genus Gopakumar-Vafa Reconstruction from Central Difference Forcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00217)
*Xiaobin Li*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文引入一个中心差分算子，将曲线计数生成函数中"多重覆盖（曲线度数）"与"拓扑弦耦合（BPS亏格）"两类信息以可逆方式分离，并给出 Chebyshev–Fejér ghost 变换及其算术与几何实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 曲线计数生成函数混合了两类不同信息：多重覆盖贡献混合曲线度数，拓扑弦耦合贡献混合 BPS 亏格。本文旨在找到一种精确且可逆的方式来分离这两种效应。

Method: 在 Calabi-Yau 三维有效射线上，对全亏格 Gopakumar-Vafa 展开引入单个中心差分算子；令 y=(2sin(λ/2))^2，将 Bryan-Pandharipande 覆盖多项式与相应 Fejér 因子结合，构造"Chebyshev-Fejér ghost 变换"用于归一化差分 forcing；该变换具有显式的算子值 Möbius 逆；并分析其积分像与 y-adic 过滤。

Result: 变换的 y-adic 过滤恰为 BPS 亏格过滤：forcing 模 y^{G+1} 等价于亏格不超过 G 的全部 BPS 不变量；亏格零重构（经典特化的第一分次片）是全亏格层级的首个关联分次片。积分 BPS forcing 等价于两阶段条件：ghost 坐标的 Witt-Dwork 型 Frobenius 同余 + 中心差分算子施加的额外 Fejér 可除性条件；在素数幂次数下得到含完整 Fejér 因子的强化 Dwork 同余。局部曲线情形：超刚性椭圆曲线给出 forcing 的精确 Dedekind η 表达式；平衡高亏格局部曲线理论中，上二次 BPS 不变量实现为对称积上平方根自然栈的 top Chern 数。

Conclusion: 本文建立了中心差分方程与 BPS 重构、算术 ghost 结构以及局部曲线几何之间的联系，表明全亏格 BPS 层级可由中心差分算子的分次结构统一刻画。

Abstract: Generating functions in curve counting theory mix two different kinds of information: multiple covers mix curve degrees, while the topological string coupling mixes BPS genera. We show that a single central difference operator separates these two effects in a precise and invertible way for the all genus Gopakumar-Vafa expansion along an effective ray of a Calabi-Yau threefold.
  Writing \[ y=\left(2\sin\fracλ{2}\right)^2, \] we combine the Bryan-Pandharipande cover polynomials with the corresponding Fejér factors and obtain a Chebyshev-Fejér ghost transform for the normalized difference forcing. The transform admits an explicit operator-valued Möbius inverse. Its $y$-adic filtration is exactly the BPS genus filtration: the forcing modulo $y^{G+1}$ is equivalent to the complete collection of BPS invariants of genera at most $G$. Thus the genus zero reconstruction from the classical specialization is the first associated graded piece of an all genus hierarchy.
  We also characterize the integral image of the transform. Integral BPS forcing is equivalent to a two stage condition: Witt-Dwork type Frobenius congruences for the underlying ghost coordinates, together with an additional Fejér divisibility condition imposed by the central difference operator. In prime power degree this gives a strengthened Dwork congruence containing the full Fejér factor.
  For local curves, the formalism has concrete geometric realizations. A super-rigid elliptic curve gives an exact Dedekind $η$ expression for the forcing. In the balanced higher genus local curve theory, the upper degree two BPS invariants are realized as top Chern numbers on natural stacks of square roots over symmetric products. These results connect central difference equations with BPS reconstruction, arithmetic ghost structures, and the geometry of local curves.

</details>


### [186] [Cohomology of the fine compactified universal Jacobians $\overline{J}_2$ and $\overline{J}_{2,1}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01829)
*Marco Fava*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 计算了亏格2、0和1个标记点稳定曲线模空间上精细紧化万有Jacobi簇的有理上同调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精细紧化万有Jacobi簇的上同调是代数几何与模空间理论中的核心研究对象，但此前对低亏格情形缺乏显式计算，尤其在$g=2$且标记点数为0、1时其有理上同调结构尚不清楚。

Method: 以稳定曲线模空间的模空间$\overline{\mathcal{M}}_{2,0}$与$\overline{\mathcal{M}}_{2,1}$为基础，构造其上的精细紧化万有Jacobi簇，并运用代数几何与上同调计算技术确定其有理上同调环。

Result: 得到了亏格$2$、标记点数为$0$和$1$时精细紧化万有Jacobi簇的有理上同调的具体结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为低亏格精细紧化万有Jacobi簇的上同调提供了显式计算，可作为进一步研究一般亏格及标记点数情形的基础与验证案例。

Abstract: We compute the rational cohomology of the fine compactified universal Jacobians over the moduli spaces of stable curves of genus $2$ with $0$ and $1$ marked points.

</details>


### [187] [Pentagram relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00225)
*Nikita Markarian*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究五点有理曲线模空间上迭代Kummer覆盖的上同调，通过二维收缩构造比较五个遗忘投影的Betti表示，得到五角星关系（Drinfeld associator系数的二次关系），并证明其在群似性、归一化和反转下等价于五边形方程，进而蕴含shuffle与正则化双重shuffle关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续关于五点有理曲线模空间迭代Kummer扩张上同调的研究工作，旨在揭示不同遗忘投影所给Betti表示之间的一致性，并厘清Drinfeld associator系数之间关系的代数结构与来源。

Method: 采用二维收缩构造（shrinking construction），将来自五个遗忘投影的Betti表示加以比较；利用对应的Hodge缺陷导出五角星关系（即associator系数的二次关系），并与五边形方程进行对比分析；结合群似性、归一化与反转操作进行等价性论证，并通过卷积特殊化恢复其Hodge实现。

Result: 五角星关系在群似性、归一化和反转条件下等价于五边形方程；在反转及纯幂系数消失的条件下，这些关系进一步蕴含shuffle关系与正则化双重shuffle关系，其Hodge实现可由卷积特殊化恢复。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了模空间上同调中Hodge缺陷所生关系与Drinfeld associator方程之间的精确对应，表明五角星关系是多重zeta值shuffle与双重shuffle结构的来源之一，为理解associator的Hodge实现提供了新的几何视角。

Abstract: We study the cohomology of iterated Kummer extensions on the moduli space of five-pointed rational curves, continuing [arXiv:2609.19341, arXiv:2609.21356]. A two-dimensional shrinking construction compares the Betti presentations obtained from its five forgetful projections. The corresponding Hodge defects give pentagram relations, quadratic relations between coefficients of the Drinfeld associator, which are equivalent to the pentagon equation under group-likeness, normalization, and reversal. Under reversal and vanishing of pure-power coefficients, the relations imply shuffle and regularized double shuffle, whose Hodge realization is recovered by a convolution specialization.

</details>


### [188] [GW/PT-correspondence for CY 4-folds-I: Canonical Orientations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02061)
*Ivan Karpov*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文构建了非紧复解析Calabi-Yau四维流形模叠上朝向的凝聚拓扑理论，推广了Joyce-Upmeier与Bojko的工作，并得到Pandharipande-Thomas对的模空间典范族朝向。证明使用了表示KU-和KO-理论的映射空间的代数拓扑，以及Lie群E_8与HSpin(16)的性质。这是与Felix Thimm合作项目的一部分，旨在通过实现Pardon纲领于四维情形来证明CY四维流形的GW/PT对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为证明Calabi-Yau四维流形的GW/PT对应，需要为相关模空间（尤其是Pandharipande-Thomas对的模空间）建立典范的族朝向，而现有朝向理论（Joyce-Upmeier、Bojko）尚不足以覆盖此情形。

Method: 构建非紧复解析Calabi-Yau四维流形模叠上朝向的凝聚拓扑理论，推广并扩展Joyce-Upmeier与Bojko的结果；证明中运用了表示KU-与KO-理论的映射空间的代数拓扑，以及Lie群E_8和HSpin(16)的性质。

Result: 建立了非紧复解析Calabi-Yau四维流形模叠朝向的凝聚拓扑理论，并特别获得了Pandharipande-Thomas对的所有模空间的典范族朝向。

Conclusion: 该工作为CY四维流形的GW/PT对应证明提供了关键的朝向理论基础，是旨在实现Pardon纲领于四维情形、证明GW/PT对应的联合项目的一部分。

Abstract: We build a cohesive topological theory of orientations on moduli stacks for non-compact complex-analytic Calabi-Yau $4$-folds by extending and generalising the work of Joyce and Upmeier, and the work of Bojko. As a result, we get, in particular, canonical family orientations for all moduli spaces of Pandharipande-Thomas pairs. Our proofs use the algebraic topology of mapping spaces representing $KU$- and $KO$- theory, as well as some properties of the Lie groups $E_8$ and, surprisingly, $\operatorname{HSpin}(16)$.
  This is part of a joint project with Felix Thimm dedicated to the proof of the $GW/PT$-correspondence for Calabi-Yau $4$-folds by implementing Pardon's programme in dimension $4$.

</details>


### [189] [Derivative and multiplicity loci of elementary symmetric polynomials in positive characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00259)
*Ying Xie*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文在正特征代数闭域上确定了初等对称多项式的所有导数轨迹与高阶重数轨迹的维数，并刻画其几何性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究正特征下初始对称多项式的导数零点轨迹与重数轨迹的维数问题，解决 Orzel 猜想中的维数问题，并区分维数跳变与无穷小加厚现象。

Method: 对 n 元 d 次多项式，令 Q = p^{v_p(n-d+1)}；通过分析 q 阶导数公共零点轨迹与重数至少 s 的零点轨迹，分类达到较大维数的分支并证明其一般光滑，同时计算坐标分支上的局部环与一般重数，并确定平移稳定子概形与 Frobenius 标准形。

Result: q 阶导数公共零点轨迹维数为 d - q - 1 + 1_{Q>d-q}，重数至少 s 的零点轨迹维数为 d - s + 1_{Q>d}；二阶情形解决 Orzel 猜想的维数问题（当 d 为 p 的幂时需必要修正）；分类了达到较大维数的分支并证明其一般光滑；在较小维数情形的坐标分支上确定局部环且一般重数恰为 Q；识别出完整平移稳定子概形并导出 Frobenius 标准形。

Conclusion: 结果区分了维数跳变与无穷小加厚，并给出它们对特征依赖的统一描述。

Abstract: We determine the dimensions of all derivative loci and all higher multiplicity loci of an elementary symmetric polynomial over an algebraically closed field of positive characteristic. For the degree-d polynomial in n variables, set Q = p^{v_p(n-d+1)}. The common zero locus of its order-q derivatives has dimension d - q - 1 + 1_{Q>d-q}, whereas the locus of zeros of multiplicity at least s has dimension d - s + 1_{Q>d}. The order-two case settles the dimension question in a conjecture of Orzel, with a necessary correction when d is a power of p. We classify all components attaining the larger dimension and prove that they are generically smooth. Along the coordinate components in the smaller-dimension cases, we determine the local rings and obtain generic multiplicity exactly Q. Finally, we identify the full translation stabilizer scheme and derive a Frobenius normal form. These results distinguish dimension jumps from infinitesimal thickening and give a uniform description of their dependence on the characteristic.

</details>


### [190] [Contraction Algebras, Cyclotomic Forms, and Reflection Rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00263)
*Xiaobin Li*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文为有限分块数据构造正定分圆形式与Householder反射表示，证明一个算术刚性定理：反射群有限当且仅当保满秩格，等价于支撑集为{1,N}且n₁=N²n_N，此时群为W(A_N)≅S_{N+1}；并将其应用于三维flop收缩代数，用Toda宽度公式给出有限性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望将分块重数数据、分圆形式和Householder反射统一起来，用以刻画分裂半单代数的同构类型，并借此理解反射群的有限性/刚性，进而联系三维flop收缩代数的Toda宽度公式与BPS/Riemann-Hilbert描述。

Method: 对有限块尺寸数据n=(n_d)构造正定分圆形式B_n(g,h)=1/wt·Σ_{(g^{-1}h)^d=1}d²n_d（N=lcm{d:n_d≠0}，wt=Σd²n_d）及Householder反射表示；用带标号Fourier谱与Möbius反演重构重数序列；把n_d视为分裂半单代数S=∏M_d(k)^{n_d}的Wedderburn重数，将整除宽度解释为Wedderburn块特征和的维数；进而分析反射群的有限性与满秩格保持性。

Result: 主要定理：N≥2时反射群有限⇔保满秩格⇔supp(n)={1,N}且n₁=N²n_N，此时B_n=½(I+J)，群为W(A_N)≅S_{N+1}；代数上等价于S≅k^{N²m}×M_N(k)^m（m≥1）。N=2时每个非有限情形为正交群中D_∞的稠密拷贝。三维flop收缩代数中，Toda宽度公式把总尺度与原始Fourier模分别识别为dim A_con与dim A_con^{ab}，故长度≥3的不可约flop均无限，长度2时Γ_f有限⇔dim A_con=2dim A_con^{ab}；末段给出同一反射矩阵的BPS/Riemann-Hilbert实现。

Conclusion: 分块重数数据可由分圆形式的Fourier谱经Möbius反演完全恢复，并决定分裂半单代数同构类型；反射群有限性对应严格算术约束（仅支撑{1,N}且n₁=N²n_N），给出W(A_N)型有限反射群。该刚性结果通过Toda宽度公式落到三维flop收缩代数的有限性判据，并具有BPS/Riemann-Hilbert的互补实现。

Abstract: We associate to finite block size data \[ \bm n=(n_d)_{d\ge1},\qquad n_1>0, \] a positive definite cyclotomic form and a Householder reflection representation. Writing \[ N=\operatorname{lcm}\{d:n_d\ne0\},\qquad \wt=\sum_d d^2n_d, \] the form on the cyclic group $C_N$ is \[ B_{\bm n}(g,h)=\frac1{\wt}\sum_{(g^{-1}h)^d=1}d^2n_d. \] Its labelled Fourier spectrum reconstructs the complete multiplicity sequence by Möbius inversion. If the $n_d$ are the Wedderburn multiplicities of a split semisimple algebra $S=\prod_d M_d(k)^{n_d}$, the divisibility widths are dimensions of characteristic sums of Wedderburn blocks, and the form together with $\dim_kS$ recovers the isomorphism type of $S$.
  The main theorem is an arithmetic rigidity result. For $N\ge2$, the reflection group is finite if and only if it preserves a full rank lattice, equivalently \[ \supp(\bm n)=\{1,N\},\qquad n_1=N^2n_N. \] In this case $B_{\bm n}=\frac12(I+J)$ and the group is $W(A_N)\cong S_{N+1}$. In semisimple algebra terms, finiteness is equivalent to \[ S\cong k^{N^2m}\times M_N(k)^m \] for some $m\ge1$. For $N=2$, every nonfinite case is a dense copy of $D_\infty$ in the corresponding orthogonal group.
  For contraction algebras of threefold flops, Toda's width formulas identify the total scale and primitive Fourier mode with $\dim A_{\con}$ and $\dim A_{\con}^{\ab}$. This yields infinitude for all irreducible flops of length at least three and, in length two, \[ Γ_f\text{ finite}\quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad \dim A_{\con}=2\dim A_{\con}^{\ab}. \] The final part gives a complementary BPS/Riemann--Hilbert realization of the same reflection matrices.

</details>


### [191] [Motivic Vanishing Cycles at Infinity and Toric Landau-Ginzburg Mirror Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00289)
*Haoxu Wang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文比较了motivic nearby cycles at infinity与在有理紧化上直接计算的类，证明由$1/f$定义的开对类与Raibaut的紧化无关motivic nearby cycle at infinity一致；关键步骤是横截零-极点相交爆破下的局部消去。对无穷远处非退化的Laurent多项式，该比较给出Raibaut essential-face公式的环面证明；等变Hodge实现与加权Ehrhart理论给出保持上同调次数和极限权滤过的新顿公式，并通过极限-非正规比较确定非正规Hodge数。忘权特化计算同一径向支撑的环面stacky扇的零势stringy不变量，从而给出相应Fano-Landau-Ginzburg镜像恒等式的motivic-Hodge推导；在全维弱Fano情形，精化公式将单值特征值与Jordan块大小同惯性年龄及反规范Lefschetz链识别，给出$\mathbb C$上的非规范比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有motivic nearby cycles at infinity（Raibaut）定义为紧化无关，但其与在具体有理紧化上直接计算类之间的关系需要澄清；同时希望借此为Raibaut essential-face公式、非正规Hodge数、stringy不变量及Fano-Landau-Ginzburg镜像等提供统一的motivic-Hodge框架和更直接的（如环面）证明。

Method: 设$f$为特征零域上光滑簇的正则函数，$X$为光滑proper紧化，假设包含边界与$\operatorname{div}_X(f)$支撑的约化除子在极轨迹附近为simple normal crossings；比较$1/f$定义的开对类与Raibaut紧化无关motivic nearby cycle at infinity，核心是横截零-极点相交爆破下的局部消去。随后结合等变Hodge实现、加权Ehrhart理论、极限-非正规比较、忘权特化及Lefschetz链/惯性年龄分析等工具进行推导。

Result: 证明$1/f$的开对类与Raibaut紧化无关motivic nearby cycle at infinity一致；对无穷远处非退化Laurent多项式给出Raibaut essential-face公式的直接环面证明；新顿公式保持上同调次数与极限权滤过并确定非正规Hodge数；忘权特化计算同径向支撑环面stacky扇的零势stringy不变量，给出Fano-Landau-Ginzburg镜像恒等式的motivic-Hodge推导；全维弱Fano情形将单值特征值及Jordan块大小与惯性年龄和反规范Lefschetz链对应。

Conclusion: 该比较建立了motivic nearby cycles at infinity与有理紧化直接计算之间的联系，统一了essential-face公式、非正规Hodge数、stringy不变量及镜像对称中的若干结果，并在全维弱Fano情形给出$\mathbb C$上单值结构与反规范Lefschetz链的非规范比较。

Abstract: We compare motivic nearby cycles at infinity with classes computed directly on rational compactifications. Let $f$ be a regular function on a smooth variety over a field of characteristic zero, and let $X$ be a smooth proper compactification. Suppose that a reduced divisor containing the boundary and the support of $\operatorname{div}_X(f)$ has simple normal crossings near the polar locus. We prove that the open-pair class defined by $1/f$ agrees with Raibaut's compactification-independent motivic nearby cycle at infinity. The key step is a local cancellation under blow-ups of transverse zero-pole intersections.
  For Laurent polynomials nondegenerate at infinity, this comparison yields a direct toric proof of Raibaut's essential-face formula. Equivariant Hodge realization and weighted Ehrhart theory give Newton formulas retaining cohomological degree and the limit-weight filtration, and determine irregular Hodge numbers through the limiting-irregular comparison. The weight-forgetting specialization computes the zero-potential stringy invariant of toric stacky fans with the same radial support, yielding a motivic-Hodge derivation of the corresponding Fano-Landau-Ginzburg mirror identity. In the full-dimensional weak Fano case, the refined formulas identify monodromy eigenvalues and Jordan-block sizes with inertia ages and anticanonical Lefschetz strings, giving a noncanonical comparison over $\mathbb C$.

</details>


### [192] [Transcending Algebraic Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00335)
*Carlos Simpson*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文述评了在算术与代数几何中发展o-minimal可定义性理论的若干核心思想,并追溯其如何导向Jacob Tsimerman的突破性工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: o-minimality原属模型论与实解析几何领域,将其引入算术与代数几何需要概念上的衔接与铺垫,本文旨在梳理这些前置思想,帮助读者理解Tsimerman突破性工作的来龙去脉。

Method: 采用综述与"说明性阐述"(exposition)的方式,系统整理通向o-minimal可定义性理论的关键概念与思想脉络。

Result: 呈现出从早期相关思想到Tsimerman在算术与代数几何中建立o-minimal可定义性理论的逻辑演进过程。

Conclusion: Tsimerman的工作被视为该方向的里程碑式突破,其理论根植于一系列先前积累的思想,本文为理解这一发展提供导引。

Abstract: We give an exposition of some of the ideas leading up to Jacob Tsimerman's groundbreaking work developing the theory of o-minimal definability in arithmetic and algebraic geometry.

</details>


### [193] [A Pluripotential-Theoretic Approach to Finiteness of Polarized Calabi--Yau Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00361)
*Truong Dinh Dat*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文提出一套利用多势论（pluripotential theory）研究极化 Calabi–Yau 流形有限性问题的框架，以全局 \(m\)-Hessian 容量作为复势论与全局几何有界性之间的定量中介，并将分析估计与代数有界性论证相连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极化 Calabi–Yau 流形的有限性问题通常假设分析估计与代数有界性之间的蕴含关系自动成立，缺乏结构化的定量桥梁。本文旨在提供统一框架，明确隔离并刻画这一进路中的主要分析与代数瓶颈。

Method: 以全局 \(m\)-Hessian 容量为核心，建立从容量到体积—容量不等式、容量—周长估计、Sobolev 界、Ricci 平坦 Kähler 度量非塌缩的一系列一致估计；在适当正则性假设下导出直径与曲率的一致控制及度量紧性。代数部分利用有界次数的统一射影嵌入，经 Macaulay–Gotzmann 理论得到有限多个 Hilbert 多项式与有限个 Hilbert 概形，再用 Ehresmann 纤维化定理将光滑代数族的有界性转化为微分同胚型与拓扑型的有限性。

Result: 建立了一条连接 \(m\)-Hessian 容量、体积—容量不等式、容量—周长估计、Sobolev 界与非塌缩的估计链，进而在正则性假设下给出度量紧性；代数侧得到有限 Hilbert 多项式与有限 Hilbert 概形，从而导出微分同胚型与拓扑型的有限性。

Conclusion: 该框架将复 Hessian 势论与几何、代数有界性联系起来，而非假设这些蕴含关系自动成立。其中，容量—周长估计以及从度量估计导出统一代数嵌入数据是仍需进一步发展的关键步骤。

Abstract: We propose a pluripotential-theoretic framework for studying finiteness questions for polarized Calabi--Yau manifolds. The central idea is to use global \(m\)-Hessian capacities as a quantitative intermediate between complex potential theory and global geometric boundedness. We formulate a chain of uniform estimates connecting \(m\)-Hessian capacity, volume--capacity inequalities, capacity--perimeter estimates, Sobolev bounds, and non-collapsing of the associated Ricci-flat Kähler metrics. Under suitable regularity assumptions, these estimates lead to uniform diameter and curvature control and hence to metric compactness. We then describe the algebraic part of the argument. Uniform projective embeddings with bounded degree lead, through the Macaulay--Gotzmann theory, to only finitely many possible Hilbert polynomials and hence to a finite collection of Hilbert schemes. Ehresmann's fibration theorem then converts boundedness of the smooth algebraic families into finiteness of diffeomorphism and topological types. The resulting framework isolates the main analytic and algebraic bottlenecks in a potential-theoretic approach to Calabi--Yau finiteness. In particular, the capacity--perimeter estimate and the derivation of uniform algebraic embedding data from the preceding metric estimates constitute the essential steps requiring further development. Thus the paper provides a structured program linking complex Hessian potential theory with geometric and algebraic boundedness, rather than assuming that these implications are automatic.

</details>


### [194] [Stratification of Joint Moduli Spaces of Higgs Bundles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00378)
*Shiyu Cao*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究Teichmüller空间上零次稳定SL_r(C)-Higgs丛的带标记解析模空间，证明由稳定子定义的轨道型轨迹连通分支构成复Whitney分层，每层带相对全纯辛形式，并给出垂直全纯Poisson括号与Hitchin-Kobayashi对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Higgs丛模空间在Teichmüller空间上的分解结构，尤其是轨道型轨迹的几何性质及 Hitchin-Kobayashi 对应在分层上的表现。

Method: 考察由全行列式为1稳定子定义的轨道型轨迹的连通分支，分析其复Whitney分层结构、到Teichmüller空间的投影，并构造相对全纯辛形式与垂直全纯Poisson括号。

Result: 轨道型轨迹连通分支构成复Whitney分层，投影在每层为全纯淹没，每层带规范相对全纯辛形式；这些形式诱导垂直全纯Poisson括号，其辛叶为纤维中连通轨道型分层；每纤维上Hitchin-Kobayashi对应在每层双全纯并识别超Kähler结构；调和代表与垂直超Kähler张量沿全空间分层光滑变化。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了Higgs丛标记模空间的复Whitney分层与相对辛/Poisson结构，并将Hitchin-Kobayashi对应提升到分层层面，给出超Kähler结构的层间光滑依赖性。

Abstract: We study the marked analytic moduli space of polystable degree-zero $\mathrm{SL}_r(\C)$-Higgs bundles over Teichmüller space. The connected components of the orbit-type loci defined by the full determinant-one stabilizers form a complex Whitney stratification. The projection to Teichmüller space is a holomorphic submersion on every stratum. Each stratum carries a canonical relative holomorphic symplectic form. These forms determine a vertical holomorphic Poisson bracket on the reduced structure sheaf, whose symplectic leaves are the connected orbit-type strata in the fibers. On each fiber, the Hitchin--Kobayashi correspondence is biholomorphic on every stratum and identifies its hyperkähler structure. The harmonic representatives and vertical hyperkähler tensors vary smoothly along the strata of the total space.

</details>


### [195] [Quasi-modularity of $q$-traces and integrals over Hilbert schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02049)
*Killian Hong-Minh,Sergey Mozgovoy*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究玻色Fock空间上归一化$q$-迹的准模性,证明由自由玻色场及其后代算子得到的准模性,并给出多个应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨玻色Fock空间上归一化$q$-迹的准模性,并推广已有结果(如Bloch--Okounkov定理、Qin猜想)。

Method: 针对有限维二次空间及超空间上的玻色Fock空间,对由自由玻色场及其后代得到的一类算子,证明其归一化$q$-迹的准模性,且权有界。

Result: 证明了该类算子归一化$q$-迹的准模性,权被插入算子权之和所界;并证明Qin关于数值平凡典范类曲面Hilbert概型上tautological积分的准模性猜想,得到Heisenberg顶点算子代数中任意零模关联函数的准模性,以及Bloch--Okounkov准模性定理的新证明。

Conclusion: 该工作系统建立了玻色Fock空间上归一化$q$-迹的准模性框架,统一并推广了若干已有结果。

Abstract: We study quasi-modularity of normalized $q$-traces on bosonic Fock spaces associated with finite-dimensional quadratic spaces and superspaces. For a natural class of operators obtained from free-boson fields and their descendants, we prove that their normalized $q$-traces are quasi-modular, with weight bounded by the sum of the weights of the insertions. As applications, we prove Qin's quasi-modularity conjecture for tautological integrals on Hilbert schemes of points of a surface with numerically trivial canonical class, obtain quasi-modularity of arbitrary zero-mode correlation functions in the Heisenberg vertex operator algebra, and give a new proof of the Bloch--Okounkov quasi-modularity theorem as a rank-one specialization of our general result.

</details>


### [196] [Supermoduli spaces are non-projected in every genus $g\geq 3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00490)
*Mauricio Corrêa,Ron Donagi,Simone Noja*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明了在所有亏格 g≥3 时，光滑无 punctured 超黎曼曲面模超叠的每个宇称分支的主障碍在代数与全纯意义下均非零，因而两个分支均非投影且非分裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究超黎曼曲面模空间（模超叠）的几何结构，特别是其宇称分支相对约化自旋曲线模叠的可投影性与分裂性，这是超几何/超弦理论中模空间结构的基础问题。

Method: 计算并分析光滑无 punctured 超黎曼曲面模超叠各宇称分支的主障碍（primary obstruction），分别在代数范畴与全纯范畴下进行验证。

Result: 对任意亏格 g≥3，两个宇称分支的主障碍均非零（代数与全纯两种情形皆然）。

Conclusion: 两个宇称分支均非投影（non-projected），特别地也非分裂（non-split）：任一分支都不存在到其约化自旋曲线模叠的代数或全纯收缩（retraction）。

Abstract: We prove that, for every genus $g\geq 3$, the primary obstruction of each parity component of the moduli superstack of smooth unpunctured super Riemann surfaces is nonzero, both algebraically and holomorphically. Consequently, both components are non-projected and, in particular, non-split: neither admits an algebraic or holomorphic retraction onto its reduced moduli stack of spin curves.

</details>


### [197] [Automorphisms of hypersurfaces in polarized varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00709)
*Ana Quedo*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文给出了一个统一判据，用截面环 R(F,H) 的分次 Betti 数刻画充分条件，使得一般除子 X∈|H^{⊗d}| 的每个自同构都能延拓为 F 的自同构，将 Matsumura-Monsky 关于射影空间超曲面的经典结果推广到更广的极化簇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Matsumura-Monsky 曾研究射影空间超曲面的自同构延拓问题，但其结论局限于射影空间中的超曲面。作者希望将这一问题推广到更一般的极化簇 (F,H)，从而涵盖有理齐性簇、Fano 三维簇、hyperkähler 与 Calabi-Yau 簇等广泛对象，因此需要一个统一且可计算的判据。

Method: 考虑射影簇 F 上正规生成（normally generated）的线丛 H，以及一般除子 X∈|H^{⊗d}|。以截面环 R(F,H) 的分次 Betti 数为工具，建立判据，给出保证 Aut(X) 中每个自同构均可延拓为 Aut(F) 中自同构的充分条件。

Result: 建立了一个用 R(F,H) 的分次 Betti 数表述的统一充分条件，用于判定一般除子 X 的自同构能否延拓到 F。该判据适用范围包括 Picard 数为 1 的有理齐性簇、反典范极化的光滑 Fano 三维簇，以及若干 hyperkähler 与 Calabi-Yau 簇的实例，从而推广了 Matsumura-Monsky 的经典结论。

Conclusion: 一般除子的自同构延拓问题可由截面环的分次 Betti 数统一控制；该框架将射影空间超曲面的经典延拓定理拓展到一大类极化簇，显示出 Betti 数判据在自同构延拓研究中的通用性与有效性。

Abstract: Let $F$ be a projective variety, let $H$ be a normally generated line bundle on $F$, and let $X\in |H^{\otimes d}|$ be a general projective variety. We establish a uniform criterion, formulated in terms of the graded Betti numbers of the section ring $R(F,H)$, that provides sufficient conditions for every automorphism of $X$ to extend to an automorphism of $F$. This extends the classical results of Matsumura-Monsky on automorphisms of hypersurfaces in projective spaces to a broader class of polarized varieties $(F,H)$, including rational homogeneous varieties of Picard number one, smooth Fano threefolds with their anticanonical polarization, and examples of hyperkähler and Calabi-Yau varieties.

</details>


### [198] [Verdier-Type Category, Geometric Serre's conditions and perversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01146)
*Shang Xu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 将Serre关于凝聚层的条件推广到带t-结构与对偶的三角范畴（Verdier型范畴），给出实例，聚焦可构层上的几何Serre条件，研究其与反常性（perversity）的关系，并证明Auslander-Reiten猜想的一个几何版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Serre条件刻画了凝聚层正合列中局部自由层与凝聚层的关系（如S1、S2），在交换代数与代数几何中有重要地位。作者希望把这套条件从阿贝尔范畴（凝聚层）提升到带t-结构与对偶的三角范畴框架下，获得更一般的同调代数工具，并将其应用于几何场景中的可构层。核心问题是：这些条件与反常性（perversity）之间有何联系，以及能否据此推出Auslander-Reiten猜想的几何版本。

Method: 定义Verdier型范畴，即配备t-结构与对偶（Verdier对偶）的三角范畴，并在此框架下推广Serre条件；构造此类范畴的实例并给出对应的Serre条件；将理论专门化到可构层情形，得到几何Serre条件；分析几何Serre条件与反常性（perversity）之间的关系，并通过一系列例子说明该理论；最后利用该理论证明Auslander-Reiten猜想的几何版本。

Result: 建立了Verdier型范畴中Serre条件的推广理论，获得了一系列实例；在可构层上给出几何Serre条件并阐明其与反常性的关系；通过多个例子展示了理论的适用性；成功证明了Auslander-Reiten猜想的几何版本。

Conclusion: Serre条件可以在带t-结构与对偶的三角范畴中自然推广，并在可构层几何场景下与反常性产生深刻联系；该框架不仅统一了若干已知现象，还足以证明Auslander-Reiten猜想的几何形式，表明Verdier型范畴是研究几何同调代数中凝聚性/反射性问题的有效工具。

Abstract: We generalize Serre's conditions for coherent sheaves to triangulated categories equipped with a $t$-structure and duality, which we call Verdier-type categories. We present examples of such categories and their associated Serre conditions, then focus on constructible sheaves, where we refer to these conditions as geometric Serre conditions. We investigate their relationship with perversity and illustrate the theory through a range of examples. Finally, we prove a geometric version of the Auslander-Reiten conjecture.

</details>


### [199] [An elliptic Calabi-Yau threefold of Mordell-Weil rank twelve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01276)
*Dominik Burek*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 构造了一个光滑射影 Calabi–Yau 三维簇，具有平展椭圆纤维化、Mordell–Weil 秩为十二，Hodge 数为 (25,1)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造具有高 Mordell–Weil 秩的椭圆纤维化 Calabi–Yau 三维簇，以丰富对椭圆纤维化与镜像对称等相关结构的研究样本。

Method: 从固定椭圆曲线与四阶模族之积对应的 Kummer 曲面出发进行构造；该三维簇双有理等价于 P(O_{P^1}(3)^{⊕2}⊕O_{P^1}(4)⊕O_{P^1}) 中的反规范四次超曲面；给出十二个独立截面，并构造相应 Weierstrass 模型的射影 crepant 消解。

Result: 得到光滑射影 Calabi–Yau 三维簇，具有平展椭圆纤维化，Mordell–Weil 秩为十二，Hodge 数 (h^{1,1}, h^{2,1})=(25,1)。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了一类高 Mordell–Weil 秩的椭圆纤维化 Calabi–Yau 三维簇的显式构造。

Abstract: We construct a smooth projective Calabi--Yau threefold with a flat elliptic fibration of Mordell--Weil rank twelve and Hodge numbers $(h^{1,1},h^{2,1})=(25,1)$. The construction starts from the Kummer surfaces associated with the products of a fixed elliptic curve and the level-four modular family. The threefold is birational to an anticanonical quartic in $\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^1}(3)^{\oplus2}\oplus\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^1}(4)\oplus\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^1})$. We give twelve independent sections and construct a projective crepant resolution of the associated Weierstrass model.

</details>


### [200] [Abeloid varieties over higher rank valued fields: Raynaud uniformization and Néron models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01354)
*Simon Kaib,Annette Werner*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文在任意秩赋值环上的adic空间背景下研究abeloid簇，推广了Raynaud一致化，并证明了半阿贝尔簇的代数Néron模型及abeloid簇的形式带标记Néron模型的存在性（在有限可分基变换后），并探讨了高阶Raynaud扩张与形式Néron模型的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Raynaud一致化及Néron模型理论主要针对秩一赋值环或非阿基米德域。作者希望将这些理论推广到任意秩赋值环（即高阶赋值）的情形，以建立更一般的abeloid簇与半阿贝尔簇的模型理论。

Method: 在$\operatorname{Spa}(K, K^+)$（$K$为完备非阿基米德域，$K^+$为任意秩赋值环）上研究abeloid簇，推广Raynaud一致化；通过代数与形式两种情形构造合适的Néron模型，并利用有限可分基变换技术。

Result: 证明了在代数封闭分式域的赋值环上，半阿贝尔簇存在代数(lft-)Néron模型；在剩余域代数封闭的完备秩一赋值环上，经有限可分基变换后同样存在。基于此，进一步证明了$\operatorname{Spa}(K, K^+)$上abeloid簇经有限可分基变换后存在形式带标记Néron模型。

Conclusion: 该工作将Raynaud一致化与Néron模型理论成功推广到任意秩赋值环的adic空间框架，建立了高阶Raynaud扩张与形式Néron模型之间的联系，为高阶非阿基米德几何中的模型理论提供了基础。

Abstract: We study abeloid varieties over the adic space $\operatorname{Spa}(K, K^+)$, where $K$ is a complete non-Archimedean field and $K^+$ is a valuation ring of arbitrary rank. We extend the well-known Raynaud uniformization to this setting. We also show the existence of suitable Néron models in algebraic and formal situations over higher rank valuation rings: We prove the existence of algebraic (lft-)Néron models for semi-abelian varieties over valuation rings with algebraically closed fraction fields, as well as, up to finite separable base change, over complete rank one valuation rings with algebraically closed residue field. Building on this result, we show the existence of formal marked Néron models for abeloid varieties over $\operatorname{Spa}(K, K^+)$ after finite separable base change. We also investigate the connection between the higher rank Raynaud extension and the formal Néron model.

</details>


### [201] [Stability of representation cohomology sheaves on moduli spaces of bundles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01481)
*Jianping Wang,Xueqing Wen*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文证明光滑复射影曲线模空间 $\SU_C(n,L)$ 的切丛是斜率稳定的，并将结论推广到连通约化群主丛模空间上由表示诱导的相干层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模空间 $\SU_C(n,L)$ 与主丛模空间可能是奇异的，其切丛/相关层的稳定性是代数几何与模理论中的重要问题，已有结论多局限于特殊情况或光滑情形，需要一般性的稳定性定理。

Method: 对 $g\geq2$、$n\geq2$ 及任意行列式 $L$，直接证明 $\SU_C(n,L)$ 切丛的斜率稳定性；对 $g\geq3$，利用表示对应的上同调层，证明连通约化群主丛模空间上的一般稳定性定理，并通过伴随表示推出几乎单群情形的切丛稳定性。证明确保对任意丰沛 Cartier 极化均成立，并允许模空间奇异。

Result: 证明了 $\SU_C(n,L)$ 的切丛对所有 $g\geq2$、$n\geq2$、任意 $L$ 均斜率稳定；对 $g\geq3$，建立了连通约化群主丛模空间上基于表示上同调层的更一般稳定性定理，伴随表示给出几乎单群切丛稳定性；结论对奇异模空间与任意丰沛 Cartier 极化均成立。

Conclusion: 该文将模空间切丛/相关层的稳定性从特殊情况推广到一般曲线亏格、一般秩与一般群，并适用于奇异模空间和任意极化，具有较强的一般性。

Abstract: We prove that the tangent sheaf of $\SU_C(n,L)$ is slope stable for every smooth complex projective curve $C$ of genus $g\geq2$, every $n\geq2$, and every determinant $L$. For $g\geq3$, we prove a more general stability theorem on moduli spaces of principal bundles for connected reductive groups, using cohomology sheaves associated with representations in place of tangent sheaves. The adjoint representation gives tangent sheaf stability for the case of almost simple groups. The moduli spaces may be singular, and stability holds with respect to every ample Cartier polarization.

</details>


### [202] [Conifold factorization in topological recursion and Gromov--Witten theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01557)
*Juping Chen,Linji Chen,Bohan Fang,Zhengyu Zong*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明了谱曲线在正规节点退化附近的拓扑递归分解定理，允许对数谱坐标，将亏格 $g\ge2$ 的配分函数分解为规范化配分函数、普适高斯真空与连通图求和的指数，并应用于局部 $\mathbb P^2$、$\mathbb F_0$、$\mathbb F_1$ 的 Gromov–Witten 分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究谱曲线在节点退化（conifold 型极限）附近拓扑递归的结构，理解闭亏格配分函数如何分解，并揭示与 conifold gap、规范化自由能及相对 Gromov–Witten 不变量的联系。

Method: 建立允许对数谱坐标的谱曲线正规节点退化附近的拓扑递归分解定理。将闭亏格 $g\ge2$ 的配分函数分解为三部分：规范化谱曲线的配分函数、普适高斯真空、连通图求和的指数。该图和在消失周期中具有严格正阶数，由此给出正幂次系数的有限图公式；高斯颈张量用参数化 $\mathbb P^1$ 的相对 Gromov–Witten 不变量表示。并通过 remodeling 方法构造具体例子。

Result: 得到分解定理：图和在消失周期中严格正阶，从而恢复 conifold gap；零阶项等于规范化的自由能；正幂次系数有有限图公式。固定亏格时，正则级数在周期与节点轨迹参数上联合收敛。高斯颈张量可由参数化 $\mathbb P^1$ 的相对 Gromov–Witten 不变量表达。作为例子，通过 remodeling 得到局部 $\mathbb P^2$ 与局部 $\mathbb F_0$ 在 conifold 框架下的 Gromov–Witten 分解，背景分别为 $\mathbb C^3$ 与解消 conifold；局部 $\mathbb F_1$ 的相应分解背景为 $\mathbb P^1$ 上的 $\mathcal O(1)\oplus\mathcal O(-3)$。

Conclusion: 该分解定理统一刻画了节点退化附近拓扑递归的结构，将闭亏格配分函数与规范化自由能、高斯真空及相对 Gromov–Witten 不变量联系起来，并为局部曲面在 conifold 框架下的 Gromov–Witten 理论提供显式分解公式。

Abstract: We prove a factorization theorem for ordinary topological recursion near a regular nodal degeneration of a spectral curve, allowing logarithmic spectral coordinates. The partition function for closed genera $g\ge2$ factors into the partition function of the normalization, a universal Gaussian vacuum, and the exponential of a connected graph sum. This graph sum has strictly positive order in the vanishing period, and thus the factorization recovers the conifold gap, identifies the term of degree zero in the vanishing period with the free energy of the normalization, and gives finite graph formulas for the coefficients of positive powers of the period. At fixed genus, the regular series converges jointly in the period and the parameters along the nodal locus. The Gaussian neck tensors can be expressed in terms of relative Gromov--Witten invariants of a parametrized $\mathbb P^1$. As examples, we use remodeling to obtain Gromov--Witten factorizations for local $\mathbb P^2$ and local $\mathbb F_0$ in the conifold frame, with backgrounds $\mathbb C^3$ and the resolved conifold, respectively. The analogous local $\mathbb F_1$ factorization has background $\mathcal O(1)\oplus\mathcal O(-3)$ over $\mathbb P^1$.

</details>


### [203] [An explicit form of the standard conjecture of Hodge type for Hermitian varieties, with $p$-adic applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01731)
*Shushi Harashita*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文给出了Hermitian簇中间上同调由极大全迷向子空间类张成、且本原部分交形式正定的初等证明，并给出了Λ的p进控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dummigan、Dummigan--Tiep与Shimada的工作基于有限酉群的表示论证明了相关Hodge型标准猜想。作者希望寻找一个不依赖有限酉群表示论、仅用对偶极图（dual polar graph）的初等证明，并能进一步给出p进信息。

Method: 设X为有限域上偶数维非奇异Hermitian簇，Λ为由其基域上极大全迷向线性子空间类张成的格。作者利用与Hermitian形式相伴的对偶极图，避免使用有限酉群的表示论，重新证明这些类张成中间上同调且本原部分交形式正定。证明显示本原部分交形式是某个Euclidean内积的显式正倍数。对定义在F_{q^2}上、维数为2m的X，进一步分析Λ的p进性质。

Result: 这些子空间交矩阵的每个p进初等因子整除q^m；因此q^m乘以余维m的任意代数圈都数值等价于这些子空间的整系数线性组合。作为副产品，Hodge型标准猜想对可被Hermitian簇有限覆盖的簇成立，且p进界沿度数与p互素的有限态射下降。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了Hodge型标准猜想在Hermitian簇情形的一个不依赖表示论的初等证明，并额外给出了p进控制与若干推论。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a nonsingular Hermitian variety of even dimension over a finite field, and let $Λ$ be the lattice spanned by the classes of its maximal totally isotropic linear subspaces defined over the base field. By work of Dummigan, Dummigan--Tiep and Shimada, these classes span the middle cohomology, and the intersection form on the primitive part is definite, as predicted by the standard conjecture of Hodge type. We give an elementary proof, which avoids the representation theory of finite unitary groups and uses only the dual polar graph attached to the Hermitian form. It shows that the intersection form on the primitive part is an explicit positive multiple of a Euclidean inner product.
  The advantage of the new proof is that it also controls $Λ$ $p$-adically. If $X$ is defined over $\F_{q^2}$ and has dimension $2m$, every $p$-adic elementary divisor of the intersection matrix of these subspaces divides $q^m$. Consequently $q^m$ times every algebraic cycle of codimension $m$ is numerically equivalent to an integral combination of them. As a by-product, the standard conjecture of Hodge type holds for varieties finitely covered by Hermitian varieties, and the $p$-adic bound descends along finite morphisms of degree prime to $p$.

</details>


### [204] [Dihedral reflections and an infinite series of irrational Seshadri constants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01783)
*Grzegorz Malara,Łukasz Merta,Justyna Szpond,Marcin Zieliński*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文将 Laface–Ugaglia 构造无理一点 Seshadri 常数的机制统一推广到所有奇数 n≥5，给出了在 P¹×P¹ 及 P² 爆破上的显式丰沛线丛与 Seshadri 常数的精确值，并抽象出平衡反射原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Laface 与 Ugaglia 曾在 P² 于九个甚一般点爆破上构造出一个无理的一点 Seshadri 常数，其依赖二面体轨道、de Jonquières 变换与沿 (-4)-曲线的反射。作者希望说明该机制并非偶然，而是可统一推广到更一般情形，并寻找其背后的普遍原理。

Method: 对奇数 n=2k+1，结合 P¹×P¹ 上的二面体轨道、de Jonquières 变换序列以及沿具有平衡法丛的 (-4)-曲线的反射论证；并抽象出“平衡反射原理”：当特殊纤维上的有理曲线具有法丛 O(-a)^{⊕2} 时，可将特殊纤维上的 nef 类沿该曲线（平方为 -2a）反射，并证明反射后的类在甚一般纤维上仍 nef。

Result: 对 n=2k+1 证明 ε(O_{P¹×P¹}(n-4,1); p_1,…,p_{2n})=√((n-4)/n)，在 2n 个甚一般点处成立，且在固定 2n 阶二面体群的甚一般自由轨道上即已成立。对每个奇数 n≥7，在 P² 于 k+7=(n+13)/2 个甚一般点爆破上给出显式丰沛线丛，其甚一般点处一点 Seshadri 常数为 2√(n(n-4))；经一次二次变换后得到 L_n=(3n-4)H-nE_1-(n-2)(E_2+…+E_5)-4(E_6+…+E_{k+5})-2(E_{k+6}+E_{k+7})，满足 L_n²=4n(n-4) 且 ε(L_n;x)=√(L_n²)。

Conclusion: 作者成功将 Laface–Ugaglia 的无理 Seshadri 常数构造统一推广至所有奇数 n≥5，得到一族显式丰沛线丛并精确计算其一点 Seshadri 常数；同时提炼出平衡反射原理，为在特殊纤维与甚一般纤维之间传递 nef 性提供了通用工具。

Abstract: Laface and Ugaglia recently constructed an irrational one-point Seshadri constant on the blow-up of $\mathbb{P}^2$ at nine very general points by combining a dihedral orbit on $\mathbb{P}^1\times\mathbb{P}^1$, a sequence of de Jonquières transformations, and a reflection argument along a $(-4)$-curve with balanced normal bundle. We show that the same mechanism extends uniformly to every odd integer $n\geq 5$. For $n=2k+1$ we prove $$ \varepsilon\bigl(\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^1\times\mathbb{P}^1}(n-4,1);p_1,\ldots,p_{2n}\bigr)=\sqrt{\frac{n-4}{n}} $$ at $2n$ very general points, and already at a very general free orbit of a fixed dihedral group of order $2n$. For every odd $n\geq 7$ this produces an explicit ample line bundle on the blow-up of $\mathbb{P}^2$ at $k+7=(n+13)/2$ very general points whose one-point Seshadri constant at a very general point equals $$ 2\sqrt{n(n-4)}. $$ More precisely, after one quadratic transformation we obtain the ample divisor $$ L_n=(3n-4)H-nE_1-(n-2)(E_2+\cdots+E_5)-4(E_6+\cdots+E_{k+5})-2(E_{k+6}+E_{k+7}), $$ with $L_n^2=4n(n-4)$ and $\varepsilon(L_n;x)=\sqrt{L_n^2}$. We also isolate an abstract balanced-reflection principle underlying the construction: a nef class on a special fiber can be reflected across a rational curve of square $-2a$ whenever the curve has normal bundle $\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^1}(-a)^{\oplus 2}$ in the total space, and the reflected class is nef on very general fibers.

</details>


### [205] [Euclidean Distance Optimization Within the Grassmannian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02018)
*Hannah Friedman,Serkan Hoşten,Andrea Rosana*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究Grassmannian子簇上的欧氏距离优化问题，给出ED判别式的刻画，并计算若干几何子簇的ED与GD次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Grassmannian子簇上寻找距给定数据点欧氏距离最近的点，其复数临界点个数即欧氏距离（ED）次数，是度量优化问题代数复杂度的核心指标；同时需要刻画临界点数异常的数据点集合，并为重要子簇给出显式次数公式。

Method: 将数据视为投影矩阵，证明整个Grassmannian的ED判别式等于该投影矩阵特征多项式的判别式；引入Grassmann距离（GD）次数，即数据点本身位于Grassmannian时的临界点数；对乘积型小Grassmannian、拟阵实现簇与Schubert簇推导ED与GD次数公式。

Result: ED判别式被刻画为数据投影矩阵特征多项式的判别式；给出了乘积Grassmannian、拟阵实现簇和Schubert簇等几何意义明确的子簇的ED与GD次数显式公式。

Conclusion: 该工作将Grassmannian上的距离优化复杂度与投影矩阵的特征多项式判别式联系起来，并通过ED、GD次数为多类重要子簇提供了可计算的代数复杂度度量。

Abstract: Given a subvariety of the Grassmannian and a data point, we seek to find a point on the subvariety minimizing the Euclidean distance to the data point. The number of complex critical points of this optimization problem is the Euclidean distance (ED) degree. We show that the ED discriminant of the whole Grassmannian, that is, the set of data points with a number of critical points different than the ED degree, is the discriminant of the characteristic polynomial of the data as a projection matrix. Another closely connected algebraic complexity measure for a subvariety in the Grassmannian is the Grassmann distance (GD) degree, which is the number of complex critical points of the distance optimization problem when the data point itself is in the Grassmannian. We give formulae for ED and GD degrees of geometrically meaningful subvarieties of the Grassmannian, namely, products of smaller Grassmannians, matroid realization varieties, and Schubert varieties.

</details>


### [206] [The Nash manifold of four-point configurations modulo similarity subgroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02087)
*Bruce Olberding,Elaine A. Walker*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文在实数平面上构造并研究四点构型在相似变换的Nash子群作用下的Nash模空间，通过四边形（保留退化中的极限线数据）替代四点构型，证明四边形空间𝒬是8维Nash流形，且其轨道空间𝒬/G为Nash流形；引入取值于[-1,1]的"纵横比"几何不变量，并证明在(-1,0)与(0,1)上𝒬/G的相应部分Nash微分同胚于区间与固定纤维的乘积，从而将非例外模问题约化为两个模型纤维的分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究实数平面四点构型在相似变换子群作用下的模空间，利用更精细的四边形概念保留退化过程中的极限线信息，以刻画轨道空间结构并简化模问题。

Method: 采用Nash流形与Nash子群的理论框架，以四边形替代四点构型以避免退化信息丢失；定义取值于[-1,1]的"纵横比"几何不变量，并借助局部平凡化（乘积分解）方法分析轨道空间。

Result: 证明四边形空间𝒬是8维Nash流形；对相似群的任意Nash子群G，轨道空间𝒬/G是Nash流形；在区间(-1,0)和(0,1)上，𝒬/G的相应部分Nash微分同胚于该区间与固定纤维的乘积。

Conclusion: 非例外部分的模问题被约化为对两个模型纤维的分析，每个纤维都可由四边形本身给出自然的几何解释。

Abstract: We construct and study the Nash manifold of four-point configurations in the real plane modulo the action of a Nash subgroup of the group of similarity transformations. We do so by using the finer notion of a quadrangle in place of that of a four-point configuration, since this retains limiting line data in degenerations. We prove that the space ${\mathcal Q}$ of quadrangles is an 8-dimensional Nash manifold and that, for every Nash subgroup $G$ of the similarity group, the orbit space ${\mathcal Q}/G$ is a Nash manifold. We define a geometric invariant, called aspect, with values in $[-1,1]$, and prove that, over each of the intervals $(-1,0)$ and $(0,1)$, the corresponding part of ${\mathcal Q}/G$ is Nash diffeomorphic to the product of the interval with a fixed fiber. Thus the nonexceptional part of the moduli problem reduces to the analysis of two model fibers, each having a natural geometric interpretation in terms of the quadrangles themselves.

</details>


### [207] [Higher-Page Jacobian and Albanese Tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02109)
*Dan Popovici,Luis Ugarte*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文通过Hodge理论方法，对page-(r-1)-∂∂̄流形与E_r-sGG流形构造了E_r-Jacobian环面、E_r-Albanese环面及E_r-Albanese映射，并给出结构定理与代数维数恒等式，应用于S^6的复结构相关研究，证明后一种情形下S^6不存在强Gauduchon度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期由两位作者与J. Stelzig及第一作者分别引入了page-(r-1)-∂∂̄与E_r-sGG两类流形，需要为其建立相应Hodge理论并推广Albanese理论，以便研究其几何与上同调性质，并用于S^6复结构等问题。

Method: 采用Hodge理论方法，对page-(r-1)-∂∂̄或E_r-sGG紧复流形构造E_r-Jacobian环面、E_r-Albanese环面与E_r-Albanese映射，并为E_r-sGG类流形发展Hodge理论。

Result: 建立了E_r-Albanese理论，给出结构定理与以E_r-Albanese映射和环面表达的代数维数恒等式；应用于S^6时得到上同调与度量定理，特别证明在文献中声称存在的复结构情形下S^6上不存在强Gauduchon度量。

Conclusion: E_r-Albanese理论为更广泛的非Kähler复流形提供了统一框架，并对S^6复结构问题给出了重要限制性结论。

Abstract: We construct, through Hodge-theoretical methods, what we call the $E_r$-Jacobian torus, the $E_r$-Albanese torus and the $E_r$-Albanese map of any compact complex manifold that is either {\it page-(r-1)-$\partial\bar\partial$} or {\it $E_r$-sGG}. These two classes of manifolds, the former of which is contained in the latter, have been introduced recently by both authors jointly with J. Stelzig, respectively by the first-named author. A Hodge theory is also developed for the latter class of manifolds. We then apply our results to give structure theorems and an identity of algebraic dimensions in terms of the $E_r$-Albanese map and torus. Other applications result in cohomological and metrical theorems for the $6$-dimensional sphere when it is equipped either with a hypothetical complex structure or with the complex structures very recently claimed to exist in the literature. For example, we show that in the latter case no {\it strongly Gauduchon} metric exists on $S^6$.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [208] [Sectional category versus module sectional category for retractive maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00089)
*Paul-Eugene Parent*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个满足同伦收缩的单连通有理有限型空间之间的映射，其 msecat 为2而 secat 为3，从而否证了 Carrasquel–Vera 关于收缩映射的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究映射的截面范畴 secat 与其有理性类比 msecat（mod 截面范畴）之间的关系。Carrasquel 和 Vera 曾猜想对于满足同伦收缩的映射，二者可能相等或存在某种约束，本文旨在检验该猜想。

Method: 在单连通有理有限型空间范畴中构造具体的映射 p，使其承认同伦收缩（homotopy retraction），并精确计算其 msecat(p) 与 secat(p)。目标空间取为一个含11个胞腔的十二维单连通 CW 复形的有理性，且该空间为有理双曲型。

Result: 所构造的映射 p 满足 msecat(p)=2 而 secat(p)=3，二者不相等，且目标空间为有理双曲型。

Conclusion: 该反例表明 Carrasquel–Vera 关于收缩映射的猜想不成立，说明即使映射满足同伦收缩，msecat 与 secat 仍可以不同。

Abstract: We construct a map between simply connected rational spaces of finite rational type which admits a homotopy retraction and satisfies $msecat(p)=2<secat(p)=3$. The target is the rationalization of a simply connected twelve-dimensional CW complex with eleven cells, and is rationally hyperbolic. This disproves Carrasquel--Vera's conjecture for retractive maps.

</details>


### [209] [A homotopical enhancement of Neisendorfer's algebraic models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01350)
*Bruno Stonek*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文为Neisendorfer关于有理单纯集、交换微分分次代数（cdga）、余交换微分分次余代数（cdgc）与微分分次李代数（dg Lie代数）同伦范畴之间等价关系的结论，构造了$\infty$-范畴层面的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Neisendorfer已给出这些模型范畴同伦范畴之间的等价，但该等价仅在经典同伦范畴层面成立，缺乏更高范畴结构的描述。本文旨在给出$\infty$-范畴的强化版本，从而在更精细的结构层面精确刻画这些等价。

Method: 通过证明线性对偶是弱有限型连通cdgc与cdga之间的部分Quillen等价；并证明Quillen的Chevalley--Eilenberg伴随是cdgc与dg Lie代数之间的部分交叉可导出等价（partial crossed derivable equivalence），其中"部分交叉可导出等价"是本文引入的新概念，用以公理化该伴随的行为。

Result: 在满足连通性、幂零性、有限型等相应假设下，线性对偶给出部分Quillen等价；Chevalley--Eilenberg伴随满足所定义的部分交叉可导出等价性质，从而在$\infty$-范畴层面实现了四类对象同伦范畴之间的等价提升。

Conclusion: 本文成功给出Neisendorfer等价在$\infty$-范畴层面的增强，并通过引入"部分交叉可导出等价"这一概念，为Chevalley--Eilenberg伴随的精细行为提供了公理化刻画。

Abstract: We give an $\infty$-categorical enhancement of Neisendorfer's equivalences between the homotopy categories of rational simplicial sets, commutative dg algebras (cdgas), cocommutative dg coalgebras (cdgcs) and dg Lie algebras (dgls), where these objects have respective hypotheses of connectedness, nilpotency and finite type. We do so by proving that linear duality is a partial Quillen equivalence between weak finite type connected cdgcs and cdgas, and that Quillen's Chevalley--Eilenberg adjunction is a partial crossed derivable equivalence between cdgcs and dgls, a concept we introduce in this paper to axiomatize precisely the behavior of this adjunction.

</details>


### [210] [Mumford--Morita--Miller classes in generalised cohomology theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01888)
*Oscar Randal-Williams*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文将 Mumford–Morita–Miller 类从普通上同调推广到任意复定向上同调理论，并对其展开初步研究，重点揭示由此产生的新现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mumford–Morita–Miller 类传统上定义于黎曼曲面模空间的普通上同调中，其性质已被广泛研究。作者希望将其置于更一般的复定向上同调理论框架下考察，以探究推广后出现的新现象与结构。

Method: 利用复定向上同调理论（complex-oriented cohomology theory）的框架，将 Mumford–Morita–Miller 类的定义从普通上同调推广到任意此类理论中，并对其基本性质展开研究。

Result: 摘要仅说明开始了这一推广类的研究，并强调推广后出现的新现象，未给出具体结论性结果。

Conclusion: Mumford–Morita–Miller 类可在任意复定向上同调理论中定义，对其研究构成一个值得深入的新方向，并将带来不同于经典情形的新现象。

Abstract: The Mumford--Morita--Miller classes on the moduli space of Riemann surfaces may be defined not only in ordinary cohomology but in any complex-oriented cohomology theory. We begin their investigation, emphasising the new phenomena that arise.

</details>


### [211] [Stable Complex Structures On Real Vector Bundles Over Connected Sums Of Quaternionic Projective Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02147)
*Souvik Mandal*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 通过分析复化映射 $c:\widetilde{KO}(M^{4n}_{\ell,m})\to\widetilde{K}(M^{4n}_{\ell,m})$ 的像，刻画了 $M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$ 上可稳定复化的实向量丛，并证明其稳定几乎复结构存在的充要条件为 $n=2$ 且 $\ell-m$ 为偶数，从而对所有 $n\ge 3$ 排除几乎复结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sato 与 Suzuki 于 1974 年断言 $M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$ 在 $3\le n\le 10$ 上不存在几乎复结构（可能除 $n=3,\ \ell=3m+1$ 外），但未给出对所有 $n\ge 3$ 的一般结论。本文旨在彻底解决该问题，并强化为稳定几乎复结构的不存在性。

Method: 研究四元数射影空间连通和 $M^{4n}_{\ell,m}=\ell\,\mathbb{HP}^{n}\,\#\,m\,\overline{\mathbb{HP}^{n}}$ 上的复化映射 $c:\widetilde{KO}(M^{4n}_{\ell,m})\to\widetilde{K}(M^{4n}_{\ell,m})$，分析其像，并借助 Pontryagin 类刻画可稳定复化的实向量丛。

Result: 以 Pontryagin 类给出了 $M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$ 上实向量丛可稳定复化的判别条件；证明 $M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$ 稳定几乎复化当且仅当 $n=2$ 且 $\ell-m$ 为偶数；由此推出所有 $n\ge 3$ 的 $M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$ 均不具有几乎复结构。

Conclusion: 本文对所有 $n\ge 3$ 建立了 $M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$ 不存在几乎复结构的结论，并加强为不存在稳定几乎复结构，完整推广并解决了 Sato–Suzuki 于 1974 年的部分结果。

Abstract: For integers $n\ge 2$ and integers $\ell,m\ge 0$, not both zero, let $M^{4n}_{\ell,m}=\ell\,\mathbb{HP}^{n}\,\#\,m\,\overline{\mathbb{HP}^{n}}$ denote the connected sum of $\ell$ copies of the quaternionic projective space $\mathbb{HP}^{n}$ and $m$ copies of $\mathbb{HP}^{n}$ endowed with the opposite orientation. By analysing the image of the complexification map $c\,\colon\,\widetilde{KO}(M^{4n}_{\ell,m})\to\widetilde{K}(M^{4n}_{\ell,m})$, we characterise, in terms of Pontryagin classes, the real vector bundles over $M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$ admitting a stable complex structure, and deduce that $M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$ is stably almost complex if and only if $n=2$ and $\ell-m$ is even. Consequently no $M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$ with $n\ge 3$ admits an almost complex structure. Sato and Suzuki asserted the non-existence of almost complex structures on $M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$ in 1974 for $3\le n\le 10$, except possibly when $n=3$ and $\ell=3m+1$; we establish it for all $n\ge 3$, and in the stronger form of stable almost complex structures.

</details>
