Only four recover the formula across all kinematic chambers (i.e., regions in which a single polynomial form applies), while most unsuccessful runs find correct chamber polynomials but fail to combine them or test their full domain. Conventional and LLM-assisted symbolic regression and standard machine-learning regressors likewise fail to recover the global formula in our experiments. Guided by these failure modes, we test a PI$+$two-student workflow in which a coordinating lead agent assigns complementary analytic and numerical tasks to two research agents and independently evaluates their results. The PI$+$two-student team successfully rediscovers the complete hydrotope formula, while the same workflow applied to the harder three negative wavenumber problem discovers a new independent verified analytic expression for the six-point amplitude $A_6$.

</details>


### [261] [No-signaling-in-time condition for three-flavor neutrino oscillations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00439)
*Massimo Blasone,Fabrizio Illuminati,Luciano Petruzziello,Kyrylo Simonov,Luca Smaldone*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 将无信号-时间(NSIT)条件从双味推广到三味中微子振荡，并纳入PMNS矩阵的CP破坏相位，以获得强宏观实在论更完整的刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宏观实在论是量子基础的核心概念，Leggett-Garg不等式只给出弱宏观实在论的充要条件。为更完整地刻画中微子振荡语境下的宏观实在论，需要研究强宏观实在论的充要条件——无信号-时间(NSIT)与时间之箭条件，并将已有NSIT研究推广到三味情形。

Method: 结合强宏观实在论的充要条件(无信号-时间条件、时间之箭条件)，在三味中微子振荡框架下扩展先前对NSIT条件的分析，并在Pontecorvo-Maki-Nakagawa-Sakata(PMNS)矩阵中显式引入CP破坏相位。

Result: 给出三味中微子振荡中含CP破坏相位情形下NSIT条件的扩展形式，从而对强宏观实在论的违反给出更完整的刻画。

Conclusion: 对NSIT条件的推广将弱宏观实在论(LGI)的研究扩展至强宏观实在论，并为三味中微子振荡、含CP相位情形下宏观实在论的检验提供了更完整的理论框架。

Abstract: Macrorealism is a fundamental concept in quantum foundations, capturing basic features of our everyday experience. Among the main tools introduced to test violations of macrorealism are the Leggett-Garg inequalities. These inequalities provide necessary and sufficient conditions for a certain data set including the correlators measured in a number of different experimental runs (weak version of macrorealism). Leggett-Garg inequalities have proved useful for detecting violations of macrorealism in neutrino oscillation experiments. However, it is interesting to investigate a set of necessary and sufficient conditions for strong macrorealism - no-signaling-in-time and arrow-of-time conditions - in order to obtain a more complete characterization of macrorealism in the context of neutrino oscillations. In particular, here we extend previous study of the no-signaling-in-time condition to the case of three-flavor neutrino oscillations, including the CP-violating phase of the Pontecorvo-Maki-Nakagawa-Sakata matrix.

</details>


### [262] [Constructive Neural Network Field Theory: $φ_2^4$ in Finite Volume](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00453)
*Samuel Frank*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文引入构造性神经网络场论，通过对动量壳逐层积分的归纳法，将有限体积标量φ⁴₂实现为网络测度的极限，并证明紫外与无限宽极限可交换且收敛到标准φ⁴₂测度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络场论将相关函数表示为对网络参数的积分，但此前缺乏构造性框架来严格实现具体量子场论。作者希望建立构造性神经网络场论，把有限体积标量φ⁴₂严格表示为网络测度的极限，从而为用网络架构定义场论提供数学基础。

Method: 按自由传播子的二进动量壳，为每个壳分配独立神经元集，系数取高斯分布、相位均匀、频率取自该壳谱。固定频率与相位后，逐壳对系数做有限维高斯积分，并用Wick序化四次相互作用重加权；对每个壳进行尺度归纳分析，估计其对对数配分函数的贡献及相邻截断边缘分布的全变差距离。

Result: 在二、三维任意耦合下，每个足够高的壳的重加权密度一致对数凹，壳场涨落保持在自由场尺度。二维情形下，对几乎所有频率和相位抽样，壳j对对数配分函数贡献为O(2^{-2j}j^3)，相邻截断边缘分布的全变差距离同阶；这些贡献可求和，故对任意增长足够快的宽度序列可移除紫外截断。几乎必然地，紫外极限与无限宽极限可交换，两种顺序下有限多个涂抹场的联合分布均收敛到标准有限体积φ⁴₂测度。

Conclusion: 该工作构造性地实现了φ⁴₂的网络表示并证明了极限的良定义性与交换性。由于以网络组织动量壳并逐壳积分的方法不依赖φ⁴₂的具体形式，预期可推广至其他场论。

Abstract: Neural network field theory specifies a Euclidean field theory by a network architecture and a density on its parameters, so that correlation functions are integrals over those parameters. We introduce constructive neural network field theory and use it to realize finite-volume scalar $φ^4_2$ as a limit of network measures. For each dyadic momentum shell of the free propagator, the field is given a separate set of neurons with Gaussian coefficients, uniform phases, and frequencies drawn from the shell's spectrum. Once the frequencies and phases are fixed, integrating out one shell's coefficients is a finite-dimensional integral against a Gaussian density reweighted by the Wick-ordered quartic interaction. At any coupling in two and three dimensions, the reweighted density is uniformly log-concave for every shell above a threshold, and the fluctuations of those shell fields are held to the free-field scale. In two dimensions, for almost every draw of the frequencies and phases, an induction over scales shows that shell $j$ contributes $O(2^{-2j}j^3)$ to the log partition function, and the total-variation distance between marginals at successive cutoffs is of the same order. Because these contributions are summable, the ultraviolet cutoff can be removed for any fixed sequence of widths that grows fast enough with the shell index. Almost surely, the ultraviolet and infinite-width limits commute, and in either order the joint laws of finitely many smeared fields converge to those of the standard finite-volume $φ^4_2$ measure. The method of parametrizing a field via a network organized into momentum shells and integrating out one shell at a time is not specific to $φ^4_2$, so we expect it to extend to other theories.

</details>


### [263] [${\mathcal{N}}=3$ supersymmetric AdS$_4$ solutions in $d=11$ supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00468)
*Andrea Conti*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了11维超引力中AdS₄解保持N≤3超对称的充要条件，证明了N=3情形唯一对应Tri-Sasaki类，从而证明所有N>2的超对称AdS₄解必为Tri-Sasaki型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对11维超引力中超对称AdS₄解进行完整分类，特别是解决N=3情形的唯一性问题，以完善对高维超引力紧化解的认识。

Method: 通过推导11维超引力AdS₄解保持N≤3超对称的充要条件，先施加N=2条件以验证方程的自洽性（重现已知解类），再针对N=3情形推导Tri-Sasaki类并证明其唯一性。

Result: N=2情形重现了先前已知的解类，验证了方程的非平凡自洽性；N=3情形唯一对应Tri-Sasaki类。

Conclusion: 所有保持N>2超对称的AdS₄解必为Tri-Sasaki型，完成了该类解的分类。

Abstract: We derive the necessary and sufficient conditions for AdS$_4$ solutions of $d=11$ supergravity to preserve ${\mathcal{N}} \leq 3$ supersymmetry. We use our setup to first impose ${\mathcal{N}}=2$ supersymmetry, recovering a previously known class of solutions, providing a non-trivial consistency of our equations. We then focus on the ${\mathcal{N}} = 3 $ case, first deriving the Tri-Sasaki class and then proving its uniqueness as the only ${\mathcal{N}} = 3$ AdS$_4$ class in eleven dimensional supergravity. Our work proves that all supersymmetric AdS$_4$ solutions preserving ${\mathcal{N}} > 2 $ supersymmetry are necessarily of Tri-Sasaki type.

</details>


### [264] [No Shortcuts to Infinity: Weil-Petersson Distance in Calabi-Yau Complex Structure Moduli Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00470)
*Jeroen Monnee*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文为Calabi-Yau复结构模空间中任意余维边界层建立了一个无穷Weil-Petersson距离的充要判据，并由此证明无穷距离边界除子的交仍为无穷距离，同时证明了Wang猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Swampland纲领中无穷距离极限至关重要，但边界除子交点处的行为难以刻画。因为需要同时控制多个模量的联合极限，而现有方法难以处理这类任意余维边界层的距离判定问题。

Method: 在IIB弦紧化框架下，利用Weil-Petersson轨迹长度与关联辅助荷矢量的BPS质量之间的不等式。借助渐近Hodge理论构造满足条件的辅助荷矢量，并直接从退化对应的极限混合Hodge结构导出判据；方法适用于Calabi-Yau型极化Hodge结构的变分。

Result: 建立了边界层位于无穷Weil-Petersson距离的充要判据；证明了无穷距离边界除子的交必然保持无穷距离；该结论对完整Weil-Petersson度量和任意轨迹成立，允许saxion与axion缠绕比无限制的层级；并证明了高维基上退化情形的Wang猜想。

Conclusion: 任意余维边界层的无穷距离判定可由极限混合Hodge结构直接刻画，且无穷距离性质在边界除子相交时稳定保持。该框架为Swampland无穷距离猜想提供了更一般的几何基础。

Abstract: Infinite-distance limits play a central role in the Swampland program, but understanding their behaviour at intersections of boundary divisors is subtle, as it requires control over simultaneous limits of several moduli. To address this, we establish a necessary and sufficient criterion for a boundary stratum of arbitrary codimension in the complex-structure moduli space of Calabi-Yau manifolds to lie at infinite Weil-Petersson distance. In particular, intersections of infinite-distance boundary divisors necessarily remain at infinite distance. The result holds for the full Weil-Petersson metric and arbitrary trajectories, including those with unrestricted hierarchies in the saxions and axion winding. In compactifications of Type IIB string theory on Calabi-Yau threefolds, our proof is based on an inequality relating the Weil-Petersson length of a trajectory to the BPS mass associated with an auxiliary charge vector. If this mass tends to zero while the charge-to-mass ratio remains uniformly bounded, every trajectory approaching the limit has infinite length. Using asymptotic Hodge theory, we construct an auxiliary charge vector satisfying these conditions and derive the criterion directly from the limiting mixed Hodge structure associated with the degeneration. More generally, our result applies to polarized variations of Hodge structure of Calabi-Yau type. In particular, we prove Wang's conjecture on finite Weil-Petersson distance for degenerations over higher-dimensional bases.

</details>


### [265] [Supersymmetric quantum mechanics on a Kähler space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00476)
*Olaf Lechtenfeld,Fin Müller*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 证明了超对称自由非相对论粒子在Kähler流形上运动的Nicolai映射经典部分对Kähler度规线性且关于速度局部；给出单圈修正的精确表达式并提出通往更高圈的途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nicolai映射的精确形式在文献中极为罕见，本文旨在为这类稀有案例提供新样例，并首次给出全阶单圈结果。

Method: 对超对称自由非相对论粒子在Kähler流形上的Nicolai映射进行分析：推导经典部分对Kähler度规的线性性与速度局部性，利用Kähler势给出单圈修正的精确表达，并指明推广到更高圈阶的方法；通过验证零圈（free-action）与单圈（determinant-matching）的Nicolai映射条件进行检验，并给出图形表示。

Result: 得到单圈修正的精确表达式；Nicolai映射条件在零圈与单圈阶均获验证；提供了图形表示。

Conclusion: 该工作为稀有的精确Nicolai映射提供了又一实例，并给出首个全阶单圈结果，同时为进一步推广至更高圈阶奠定了基础。

Abstract: We prove that the classical part of the Nicolai map for a supersymmetric free nonrelativistic particle moving on a Kähler manifold is linear in the Kähler metric and local for its velocity. Moreover, we give an exact expression for the one-loop correction of the map in terms of the Kähler potential and point out a direct way to higher loop order. This provides another example for the rare cases of exact Nicolai maps, and the first all-order one-loop result. As a check we have verified the Nicolai-map conditions to zero and one-loop order (free-action and determinant-matching). A graphical representation is given as well.

</details>


### [266] [Algebraic structure in holographic tensor networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00478)
*Abhisek Sahu,Jeremy van der Heijden,Mark Van Raamsdonk,Rana Zibakhsh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文用张量网络玩具模型检验广义纠缠楔对应子代数的假设，验证其熵的单调性与强次可加性，并构造精确的type II₁ von Neumann代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前作2511.21852提出：在半经典极限下，广义纠缠楔（Bousso-Penington）对应可观测量子代数，且其熵满足与广义引力熵相同的单调性和强次可加性。本文旨在用全息张量网络玩具模型检验这些代数与几何对应思想。

Method: 在张量网络中，广义纠缠楔的类比是"不存在包含它的区域具有更少边界腿"的区域。在大键维极限（类比半经典极限）下，论证每个此类区域有规范关联的边界Hilbert空间可观测代数子代数，以及一族酉相关的von Neumann因子子代数；详细研究子代数的交、并与交换子等代数运算与张量网络区域几何运算的对应；检验广义熵的单调性和强次可加性在多大程度上来自代数熵性质；引入与子代数A相关的替代熵量S(ω||ω∘E_{A'})；并说明如何基于键维递增的无穷随机张量网络族定义type II₁ von Neumann代数，使广义纠缠楔区域对应其精确子代数。

Result: 每个广义纠缠楔类比区域在大键维极限下有规范关联的边界子代数及一族酉相关von Neumann因子子代数；子代数的交、并、交换子等运算与区域几何运算存在对应关系；广义纠缠楔广义熵的单调性和强次可加性部分可由代数熵性质推出；替代熵量S(ω||ω∘E_{A'})在适当条件下也满足单调性和强次可加性；构造出使广义纠缠楔区域对应精确子代数的type II₁ von Neumann代数。

Conclusion: 张量网络玩具模型支持广义纠缠楔与子代数对应的假设，代数运算与几何运算对应，广义熵性质可由代数熵部分解释，并可通过随机张量网络极限构造精确的type II₁代数框架。

Abstract: In a previous work 2511.21852, we explored the hypothesis that in general theories of quantum gravity, spacetime regions known as generalized entanglement wedges (introduced by Bousso and Penington) have an associated subalgebra of observables in a semiclassical limit. We defined an entropy associated with these subalgebras that obeys the same monotonicity and strong subadditivity properties as the generalized gravitational entropy of generalized entanglement wedges. Here, we test aspects of these ideas making use of tensor network toy models for holography. In this context, the analog of a generalized entanglement wedge is a region for which no region containing it has a smaller number of boundary legs. In the limit of large bond dimension (analogous to the semiclassical limit), we argue that each such region has a canonically associated subalgebra of the full algebra of observables on the boundary Hilbert space, as well as a family of unitarily related von Neumann factor subalgebras. We investigate in detail how various algebraic operations on these subalgebras (e.g. their intersections, joins, and commutants) are related to corresponding geometrical operations on the tensor network regions. We study to what extent the monotonicity and strong subadditivity properties of generalized entropy for generalized entanglement wedges follow from properties of our algebraic entropy. We also introduce an alternative entropic quantity $S(ω|| ω\circ E_{\mathcal{A}'})$ associated with a subalgebra $\mathcal{A}$ that also obeys monotonicity and strong subadditivity properties with suitable conditions. Finally, we explain how to define a type $II_1$ von Neumann algebra based on an infinite family of random tensor networks with increasing bond dimension such that the regions corresponding to generalized entanglement wedges can be associated with exact subalgebras of this full algebra.

</details>


### [267] [Twisted BPS decoupling limits and AdS/CFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00482)
*Troels Harmark*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究"twisted BPS decoupling limits"，即BPS态沿基空间上几何扭曲的圆纤维携带动量的极限，其弦涨落由涌现非洛伦兹靶空间上的非相对论sigma模型描述，并与Spin Matrix theory极限对偶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BPS解耦极限（如弦论与M理论中）已深刻推动了对引力与非微扰量子理论的理解，最典型的是AdS/CFT对应。作者希望将这一框架推广到更一般的情形，即引入几何扭曲的圆纤维，从而研究twisted BPS解耦极限，以拓展BPS极限与非洛伦兹几何之间的联系。

Method: 作者在type IIB弦论（特别是AdS_5×S^5背景）中构造twisted BPS解耦极限，涉及整体对称代数PSU(2,2|4)的BPS态；分析弦涨落在该极限下由非相对论sigma模型描述，其靶空间为具有自然辛结构的涌现非洛伦兹空间；并引入D-brane的类似极限，使其探测与弦相同的辛结构。

Result: twisted BPS解耦极限下的弦涨落由非洛伦兹靶空间上的非相对论sigma模型刻画，该靶空间带有自然辛结构；D-brane的类似极限探测到相同的辛结构；对于AdS_5×S^5，twisted BPS解耦极限与N=4超对称Yang-Mills理论的Spin Matrix theory极限对偶。

Conclusion: 该工作支持BPS极限与非洛伦兹几何之间的广泛联系，并为AdS/CFT的非微扰区域提供了可处理且显式的量子力学描述。

Abstract: BPS decoupling limits of string and M-theory have led to profound insights into gravity and non-perturbative quantum theory, most notably with the AdS/CFT correspondence. In this paper, we initiate the study of twisted BPS decoupling limits, in which the relevant BPS states carry momentum along circle fibers geometrically twisted over a base space. Prime examples are found in type IIB string theory on AdS$_5\times S^5$, for which the twisted limits involve the BPS states of the global symmetry algebra PSU$(2,2|4)$. In general, string fluctuations in a twisted BPS decoupling limit are described by non-relativistic sigma-models on emergent non-Lorentzian target spaces with a natural symplectic structure. This further supports the broader connection between BPS limits and non-Lorentzian geometry. We introduce analogous limits of D-branes as well, probing the same symplectic structure as the string. For AdS$_5\times S^5$, twisted BPS decoupling limits are dual to Spin Matrix theory limits of $\mathcal{N}=4$ supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory, providing tractable and explicit quantum-mechanical descriptions of non-perturbative regimes of AdS/CFT.

</details>


### [268] [Gaugino condensates in $\mathcal{N}=1$ super-Yang-Mills Theory on Eguchi-Hanson space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00486)
*Mohamed M. Anber*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文用瞬子方法研究 Eguchi–Hanson 空间上 N=1 SU(N) 超杨–米尔斯理论的 gaugino 凝聚，考察分数拓扑荷与边界谱不对称性对瞬子计算的影响，并计算 SU(2) 及 SU(4) 情形的凝聚值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弯曲（Eguchi–Hanson）背景上，分数拓扑荷与 ℂP³/RP³ 边界的谱不对称性会改变瞬子微积分，从而可能影响 gaugino 双线性凝聚的取值。作者希望厘清这些效应如何修正平坦空间结果，并探讨零模是否足以决定凝聚。

Method: 采用瞬子微积分：识别 charge-1/2 瞬子仅带两个伴随 gaugino 零模并可直接贡献双线性；将 Eguchi–Hanson 路径积分视为 RP³ 上的边界态，构造边界扇区投影振幅；利用超对称 Ward 恒等式证明手征关联函数与插入点及分辨率参数无关；对 SU(2) 中心 holonomy 情形完成完整集体坐标积分，对 SU(N>2) 单块嵌入的非中心 holonomy 情形引入连续行列式以保证重整化群不变性；并分析 charge-1 SU(4) 双块鞍点（16 个玻色模、8 个伴随零模）的集体坐标积分。

Result: SU(2) 中心 holonomy 情形，对指定边界投影得 ⟨tr(λλ)⟩=16π²Λ³，与平坦空间值一致。SU(N>2) 单块嵌入的非中心 holonomy 需连续行列式，零模本身不足以确定双线性。charge-1 SU(4) 双块鞍点得 ⟨∏tr(λλ)(x_i)⟩=(1/6)(16π²Λ³)⁴，在位置无关性与聚类假设下确定相应边界扇区双线性凝聚的四次方；因同扇区无独立双线性计算，因子 1/6 本身不构成 clustering puzzle。

Conclusion: Eguchi–Hanson 空间上的分数荷瞬子可自洽地重现平坦空间 gaugino 凝聚；边界扇区投影与 holonomy 结构决定零模是否足够。SU(4) 四点函数给出凝聚四次方的确定值，但 1/6 因子不构成 clustering 矛盾，因为缺少同扇区独立双线性结果进行对照。

Abstract: We study gaugino condensates in $\mathcal{N}=1$ $SU(N)$ super-Yang--Mills theory on Eguchi--Hanson space using instanton methods. Fractional topological charge and the spectral asymmetry of the asymptotic $\mathbb{RP}^3$ boundary modify the instanton calculus. A charge-$\frac12$ instanton carries only two adjoint gaugino zero modes and can therefore contribute directly to the bilinear. Because the Eguchi--Hanson path integral prepares a boundary state on $\mathbb{RP}^3$, the observables are boundary-sector-projected amplitudes. Moreover, supersymmetric Ward identities ensure that chiral correlators are independent of insertion points and the Eguchi--Hanson resolution parameter. For $SU(2)$ instanton, with central holonomy, the full collective-coordinate integral gives $\langle\operatorname{tr}(λλ)\rangle=16π^2Λ^3$ for a specified boundary projection, reproducing the flat-space value. For a single $SU(2)$-block embedding in $SU(N>2)$, the noncentral holonomy requires a continuum determinant for renormalization-group invariance, so zero modes alone do not determine the bilinear. We also study a charge-$1$ $SU(4)$ two-block saddle with central holonomy, $16$ bosonic moduli, and $8$ adjoint zero modes. Its collective-coordinate integral gives $\langle\prod_{i=1}^4\operatorname{tr}(λλ)(x_i)\rangle=\frac16(16π^2Λ^3)^4$. Under the assumptions required for position independence and clustering, this result determines the fourth power of the bilinear condensate in the corresponding boundary sector. Since no independent bilinear calculation is available in the same sector, the factor $1/6$ does not by itself constitute a clustering puzzle.

</details>


### [269] [Monogamous quantum corrections to holographic mutual information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00670)
*César A. Agón,Javier Molina-Vilaplana,Javier Moreno*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了反德西特时空中的体相互信息（MI）模型，用其描述特定全息理论MI的领头量子修正，并证明体MI的可扩展性足以使量子修正后的边界熵向量满足MI的单调性及全息熵锥不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解全息理论中相互信息（MI）的量子修正，并探究体MI的性质（尤其是可扩展性）如何决定边界熵向量能否满足单调性及全息熵锥不等式。

Method: 基于Faulkner–Lewkowycz–Maldacena处方建立体MI与边界MI的等价性；构建由AdS中协变守恒的流-流核组成的模型，并用自由大质量Dirac费米子显式实现；在AdS₃共形不动点处对不相交半圆盘进行精确计算。

Result: 体MI的可扩展性足以使量子修正后的边界熵向量满足MI的单调性和所有全息熵锥不等式；模型对应的边界长距离MI与费米子广义自由场（GFF）一致，并重现GFF预期的短距离发散结构；流-流关联函数沿重整化群流为正值且单调递减；在AdS₃共形不动点处给出了不相交半圆盘的精确结果。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个可显式实现的体MI模型，将体可扩展性与边界熵不等式联系起来，并通过自由大质量Dirac费米子验证了GFF的长/短距离行为及流-流核的正性与单调性。

Abstract: We construct an extensive model of bulk mutual information (MI) in anti-de Sitter spacetime and use it to describe the leading quantum corrections to the MI of certain holographic theories. The equivalence between bulk and boundary MI follows from the Faulkner--Lewkowycz--Maldacena prescription. We show that the extensivity of the bulk MI is sufficient for the quantum-corrected boundary entropy vector to satisfy monogamy of MI and all other holographic entropy cone inequalities. Our model is built from a covariantly conserved current-current kernel in AdS and is realized explicitly by a free massive Dirac fermion, whose boundary MI at long distances is that of a fermionic generalized free field (GFF). The construction also reproduces the expected short-distance divergence structure of a GFF, and the current-current correlator defines a positive and monotonically decreasing function along the renormalization-group flow. We illustrate this explicitly for disjoint half-disks at the conformal fixed point of the model in AdS$_3$, where we present the exact result.

</details>


### [270] [Mock Modularity, Resurgence, and Dual False Theta Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00516)
*Mrunmay Jagadale*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文证明对偶伪theta函数的模刻画与 resurgence 刻画等价，并构造出满足Pell方程条件的正整数的对偶伪theta函数家族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 负定plumbed三维流形的$\hat{Z}$不变量已有较好理解，但对取向反转流形，已知公式不能产生$q$-级数，如何理解$q \rightarrow q^{-1}$是核心开放问题。Seifert流形的$\hat{Z}$不变量由伪theta函数构成，是自然试验场。

Method: 研究取向反向下应替代伪theta函数的"对偶伪theta函数"，比较文献中两种刻画：基于带指定shadow的mock模性的模刻画，与基于Borel–Mordell积分和transseries的resurgent刻画，证明二者等价；构造由Zwegers不定theta函数构成的新家族。

Result: 证明两种刻画等价；构造出一族对偶伪theta函数，其参数$p$为满足Pell方程条件的正整数，成员由Zwegers不定theta函数构成，系数为整数，有效中心荷$c_{\text{eff}} \leq 1$；并证明Li与Schwagenscheidt的mock theta函数满足这些刻画，且来自发散乘积$\eta^{3}(\tau)\psi_{p,a}(-\tau)$的自然正则化。

Conclusion: 对偶伪theta函数的模与resurgent两种刻画等价，并给出新的构造与既有mock theta函数的自然来源，为理解$\hat{Z}$不变量的取向反转与$q \rightarrow q^{-1}$问题提供进展。

Abstract: The $\hat{Z}$ invariant of a three-manifold $M_{3}$, a half-index of the $3d$ $\mathcal{N}=2$ theory $T[M_{3}]$, is well understood for negative-definite plumbed three-manifolds. For orientation-reversed manifolds, however, the known formulas fail to produce $q$-series, and making sense of the operation $q \rightarrow q^{-1}$ remains a central open problem. Seifert manifolds, whose $\hat{Z}$ invariants are built from false theta functions, offer a natural testing ground. We study ``dual false theta functions'', the objects that should replace false theta functions under orientation reversal. Two characterizations of these duals exist in the literature: a modular one, based on mock modularity with a prescribed shadow, and a resurgent one, based on Borel--Mordell integrals and transseries. We prove that the two characterizations are equivalent. We construct a new family of dual false theta functions for values of positive integer $p$ satisfying a Pell-equation condition. Its members are built from Zwegers' indefinite theta functions, have integer coefficients, and have effective central charge $c_{\text{eff}} \leq 1$. We also show that the mock theta functions of Li and Schwagenscheidt satisfy these characterizations and arise from a natural regularization of the divergent product $η^{3}(τ)ψ_{p, a}(- τ)$.

</details>


### [271] [Hamiltonian formalism for a 5-D brane cosmolgy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00556)
*José Socorro García,Juan Luis Pérez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在五维时空中从哈密顿视角（De Donder–Weyl形式）研究膜系统的动力学解，导出尺度因子的动力学方程与广义Friedmann型方程，给出尺度因子随额外坐标y的函数解，并发展膜的ADM形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从哈密顿（De Donder–Weyl）视角系统处理五维时空膜系统的动力学，弥补通常拉格朗日/场方程视角的不足，并建立额外坐标演化与膜上物质-能量成分之间的联系。

Method: 采用De Donder–Weyl哈密顿形式主义研究五维时空中膜系统；推导尺度因子的动力学方程，得到一般形式的Friedmann型方程；求解尺度因子随额外坐标y的依赖关系；并发展膜视角下的ADM形式，将三维空间切片嵌入五维流形。

Result: 获得尺度因子的动力学方程与一般形式的Friedmann型方程；得到尺度因子作为额外坐标y函数的解；表明几何与膜内物质-能量成分相关；建立膜视角下的ADM形式（三维空间切片嵌入五维流形）。

Conclusion: 五维膜系统的动力学可在De Donder–Weyl哈密顿框架下一致描述，几何演化由膜上物质-能量成分决定；膜的ADM形式为该系统的正则/初值问题分析提供了可行框架。

Abstract: In this work we explores the dynamic solutions of a brane system in a five dimensional space-time under the Hamiltonian perspective, using the De Donner Weyl formalism. We obtain the dynamic equations for the scale factors, and obtain the Friedmann-like equations in general way. We present the solutions of the scale factors as a function of the extra coordinate, $y$, we show that the geometry is related to the matter-energy component in the brane. Finally,
  we develop the ADM formalism in the brane point of view with the foliation of the 3-dimensional space which is embedded in a 5-dimensional manifold.

</details>


### [272] [Absence of Continuous Spin Particles in Superstring Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00745)
*Arwa Alabbasi,Fernando Quevedo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文论证连续自旋粒子在微扰超弦理论中不存在，将arXiv:1302.4771的非超对称结论推广至超对称情形，构成所有微扰超弦构造的一般低能预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续自旋粒子是相对论与量子力学的一般低能推论，近年来因其潜在实验特征而受到关注；已有工作（arXiv:1302.4771）表明其在（非超对称）微扰弦理论中不出现，本文意在检验该结论在超对称情形下是否依然成立。

Method: 在微扰超弦理论的框架下进行推广性分析，将原非超对称推导扩展至超对称情形，考察连续自旋态在超弦谱中是否出现。

Result: 连续自旋粒子在微扰超弦理论中同样不存在，原结论成功推广到超对称情况。

Conclusion: "微扰超弦理论中不存在连续自旋粒子"可作为所有微扰超弦构造的普适低能预言。

Abstract: The study of continuous spin particles, as general low-energy implications of relativity and quantum mechanics, has intensified in recent years with potential experimental signatures. In this note we show that these particles are absent in perturbative super-string theories, generalising a result in arXiv:1302.4771 to the supersymmetric case. This stands out as a general low-energy prediction of all perturbative superstring constructions.

</details>


### [273] [Antipodal Self-Duality for Generalized Fishnets from Integrability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00803)
*Lance J. Dixon,Claude Duhr,Francesca C. Fernandes,Victor Mishnyakov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究方形鱼网积分在反对跖自对偶变换下的多项式性质，利用梯子积分行列式与对称多项式理论，构造出大量新的ASD量，并给出方形鱼网积分满足ASD的替代证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反对跖自对偶（ASD）是振幅在适当运动学映射与符号字母反转下与自身相关的显著性质，但此前仅在平面N=4超杨-米尔斯理论中的一个过程被观测到，其物理起源未知。近期在强形变平面N=4的方形鱼网积分中也发现了ASD，这促使作者在更一般的语境下搜索ASD现象。

Method: 以梯子积分构成的特定矩阵行列式为基本构建块，这些行列式由分割或杨图标记并自动满足Steinmann关系。作者采用基于可积性的表示（本质上是矩阵模型积分）以及对称多项式理论，分析这些行列式在保持方形鱼网不变的ASD变换下的行为。

Result: 发现了大量新的ASD量，即行列式的线性组合，其系数由分割的组合数学表达；同时作为副产品，给出了方形鱼网积分满足ASD的一个替代证明。

Conclusion: ASD性质可推广至更广泛的由梯子积分行列式线性组合构成的量，其结构由分割组合数学刻画，表明ASD并非孤立现象，而可能具有更普遍的结构性根源。

Abstract: Antipodal self-duality (ASD) is a remarkable property that relates an amplitude to itself, after performing a suitable kinematic map and reversing the letters of the symbol (more precisely, acting with the antipode of the Hopf algebra of multiple polylogarithms). ASD has been seen in only one process in planar $\mathcal{N}=4$ super-Yang-Mills theory, and its physical origin is unknown, motivating a search for ASD in other contexts. Recently, ASD was found to hold for square fishnet integrals in a strongly-deformed version of planar $\mathcal{N}=4$. In this paper, we investigate the properties of polynomials in ladder integrals under the ASD transformation that leaves the square fishnet invariant. Our basic building blocks are determinants of certain matrices of ladder integrals, that are labeled by partitions or Young diagrams, and automatically obey the Steinmann relations. We employ integrability-based representations of such determinants, which are essentially matrix-model integrals, and the theory of symmetric polynomials to find many more ASD quantities -- linear combinations of determinants whose coefficients are expressed using the combinatorics of partitions. As a byproduct, we provide an alternate proof that square fishnet integrals obey ASD.

</details>


### [274] [Complexity study of the Hartle-Hawking state in JT gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00844)
*Ritam Basu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究Jackiw-Teitelboim引力中Hartle-Hawking态在长度基下随时间演化时Wigner函数负性的增长：盘图阶负性恒为1+o(1)，晚时趋于上界√(2/π)·√d_eff(β)，且无谱形状因子的斜坡结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wigner函数负性可作为量子态经典模拟复杂度的操作性度量，作者希望借助它刻画JT引力中混沌量子动力学的半经典对偶描述，并检验长度基是否适合作为对偶有效描述的基础。

Method: 在Jackiw-Teitelboim引力的长度基下计算Hartle-Hawking态的Wigner函数及其负性随时间的演化，取盘图阶与半经典参数β领头阶，结合时间反演对称性、自由演化的Clifford剪切性质、精确存活振幅Z(β+it)/Z(β)与传播复杂度分析，并比较高阶（双边界虫洞）贡献的量级。

Result: 盘图阶领头阶下Hartle-Hawking态是静止在Liouville墙转折点、中心x=2log(2β/π)、⟨p²⟩=π²/8β的最小不确定度高斯波包，Wigner函数为正，负性对所有次指数时间均为1+o(1)，关于t偶且反射波包脱离墙后被完全冻结。晚时负性饱和至上界√(2/π)·√d_eff(β)，其中d_eff(β)=Z(β)²/Z(2β)，该值依赖离散谱。负性不显示谱形状因子的斜坡：双边界虫洞贡献相对盘图项被e^{-2S₀}压低，而该盘图项不像谱形状因子那样衰减。精确存活振幅给出传播复杂度按σ_E²t²增长，种子归一化负性在盘图阶等于精确存活概率的倒数。

Conclusion: 结果表明长度基非常适合在大e^{S₀}、次指数时间尺度上为混沌量子动力学提供半经典对偶有效描述，负性可作为区分不同谱结构（盘图与虫洞贡献）的有效诊断量。

Abstract: The negativity of the Wigner function gives an operationally meaningful measure of the complexity of simulating a quantum state on a classical computer. We study its growth for the Hartle-Hawking state under time evolution in Jackiw-Teitelboim gravity, in the length basis. At the disk level, and to leading order in the semiclassical parameter $β$, the Hartle-Hawking state is a minimum-uncertainty Gaussian wavepacket at rest at the turning point of the Liouville wall, centred at $x=2\log(2β/π)$ with $\langle p^2\rangle=π^2/8β$. Its Wigner function is positive, so its negativity is $1+o(1)$ for all sub-exponential times; it is even in $t$ by time-reversal symmetry, and exactly frozen once the reflected packet decouples from the wall, because free evolution is a Clifford shear. At late times the negativity saturates close to its upper bound, at $\sqrt{2/π}\sqrt{d_{\rm eff}(β)}$ with $d_{\rm eff}(β)=Z(β)^2/Z(2β)$, a value that requires the discrete spectrum. Unlike the spectral form factor, the negativity displays no ramp: the two-boundary wormhole contribution is suppressed by $e^{-2S_0}$ relative to a disk term that, unlike that of the spectral form factor, does not decay. The exact survival amplitude $Z(β+it)/Z(β)$ gives the spread complexity growing as $σ_E^2t^2$, and the seed-normalised negativity is, at the disk level, the inverse of the exact survival probability. We take this as evidence that the length basis is ideally suited for a dual, semi-classical effective description of chaotic quantum dynamics for large $e^{S_0}$ at sub-exponential times.

</details>


### [275] [Analytic Dual Resonance and the String Bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01101)
*Qu Cao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 提出解析双重共振（ADR）原理，要求整数共振处完整领头极点求和在任意三角剖分下具有绝对收敛的局部围道积分表示。该原理导出任意三角剖分中留数之间的一维配对关系，结合整数平面谱、联合交叉-自旋约束以及允许无减除重构的复Regge衰减，可唯一确定 n≥5 的标量弦振幅，并在五点、六点检验高维夹点关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双重共振通过不同通道的极点展开重构散射振幅，但弦振幅的相互作用形式是否唯一、超越已知低点情形如何被约束尚不清楚。作者希望从双重共振的解析结构出发，寻找能够唯一确定弦振幅的附加原理。

Method: 引入解析双重共振（ADR）条件：整数共振处完整领头极点求和需存在绝对收敛的局部围道积分。由此推导任意三角剖分下留数的一维配对关系；再结合整数平面谱、联合交叉-自旋界、以及允许无减除重构的复Regge衰减作为额外约束。并对五点、六点振幅考察由ADR预言的高维夹点关系。

Result: ADR给出的配对关系，连同整数平面谱、联合交叉-自旋界和复Regge衰减，能够唯一固定每个 n≥5 的标量弦振幅；同时ADR预言的高维夹点关系在五点和六点得到检验。

Conclusion: 该工作将双重共振的解析结构与弦相互作用的唯一性联系起来，表明ADR可作为确定高点数弦振幅的有效解析原理。

Abstract: Dual resonance reconstructs a scattering amplitude from pole expansions in different channels. We propose analytic dual resonance (ADR), requiring that the complete leading pole sum at an integer resonance admit an absolutely convergent local contour integral. ADR yields one-dimensional pairing relations between residues in arbitrary triangulations. Together with an integer planar spectrum, a joint crossing-spin bound, and complex Regge decay permitting unsubtracted reconstruction, these relations uniquely fix the scalar string amplitude for every $n\ge5$. ADR also predicts higher-dimensional pinch relations, which we examine at five and six points. These results connect the analytic structure of dual resonance to the uniqueness of string interactions.

</details>


### [276] [The Love for Three Logarithms: Universal Running of Dynamical Love Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01152)
*Sebastian Garcia-Saenz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模型无关的框架，用以描述动力学层面潮汐Love数的对数跑动，基于近区频率展开与空间无穷远处的单值性，并推导主递推公式，给出对Reissner-Nordström、Hayward及Bardeen黑洞等的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论中黑洞静态潮汐Love数为零，但在更一般理论或超越静态极限时通常非零并随尺度对数跑动；需要一个不依赖具体模型的统一框架来刻画这种跑动及其重整化群结构。

Method: 基于近区按频率ω的幂次展开以及空间无穷远处的单值性(monodromy)，构造模型无关框架；用主递推公式将单值性生成元确定到ω²的所有阶，其三个独立分量完整编码Love数的跑动，并讨论方案的依赖性与重整化群方程结构。

Result: 证明重整化群方程的二次性质对所有阶、所有类型Love数成立；澄清Mano-Suzuki-Takasugi形式中"重整化角动量"即潮汐场反常指数是方案不变的，可在任意时空直接计算而无需显式解；导出Reissner-Nordström黑洞轴向Love数主导动力学跑动的闭式公式并证明其在极端极限下消失；证明超越广义相对论的普遍黑洞中动力学跑动探测的度规形变低阶多极比静态情形更低；对Hayward和Bardeen正则黑洞给出若干精确结果。

Conclusion: 该单值性框架统一刻画了潮汐Love数的动力学跑动，揭示其重整化群结构与方案依赖性，提供无需显式解即可计算反常指数的途径，并推广至多种黑洞情形，为检验超越广义相对论的理论提供新探针。

Abstract: The static tidal Love numbers of black holes vanish in general relativity (GR), but are generically non-zero, and in fact 'run' logarithmically with scale, in more general theories or beyond the static limit. We expose a model-agnostic framework for the running of Love numbers at the dynamical level, valid for any tidal perturbation on any static, spherically symmetric, asymptotically flat background, based on the near-zone expansion in powers of the frequency $ω$ and on the monodromy at spatial infinity. A master recurrence formula determines the monodromy generator to all orders in $ω^2$, whose three independent entries fully encode the running of the Love numbers. This structure explains the quadratic nature of the renormalization group equation, here proved to all orders and for any type of Love number. We discuss the scheme dependence of the logarithmic coefficients, which is made transparent by the monodromy form. In particular, this clarifies that the anomalous exponents of the tidal field, the so-called 'renormalized angular momentum' of the Mano-Suzuki-Takasugi formalism, are scheme-invariant and directly computable on any spacetime, without need of explicit solutions. We discuss several applications to black holes, including the derivation of a closed-form formula for the leading dynamical running of axial Love numbers of Reissner-Nordström black holes, proving in particular that it vanishes in the extremal limit. For generic black holes beyond GR, we prove that dynamical running probes deformations of the metric at lower multipoles than the static one, and we illustrate the power of the method by deriving some exact results for the Hayward and Bardeen regular black holes.

</details>


### [277] [DSSYK, open ASEP/TASEP and 2d dilaton gravity at strong coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01790)
*Alexander Gorsky,Elizaveta Kovalenko,Sergei Nechaev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文讨论了KPZ普适类随机生长模型、DSSYK模型与低维膨胀子引力之间的三重对偶，并给出ASEP转移矩阵预解式的精确表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示KPZ普适类随机生长模型、DSSYK模型与低维膨胀子引力之间隐藏的对偶联系，并借助随机行走、堆叠生长等表示深化对强耦合引力的理解。

Method: 将TASEP视为二维量子引力的强耦合极限；证明DSSYK哈密顿量与ASEP转移矩阵仅相差常数，并求解有限区间ASEP转移矩阵的预解式；利用累积量与交叉图Tutte多项式T(1,q)的关系，以及二维流形上离散/连续随机行走实现各模型希尔伯特空间；发展ASEP、q-TASEP与堆模型间的新对偶。

Result: 建立了TASEP、DSSYK与低维膨胀子引力的对偶关系；得到ASEP转移矩阵预解式的精确表达式；发现累积量与交叉图Tutte多项式T(1,q)的关联，暗示与引力耦合的Q=0 Potts模型的关系；构造了各模型基于二维流形随机行走的希尔伯特空间实现及ASEP/q-TASEP/堆模型的新对偶。

Conclusion: TASEP作为堆生长模型的表示赋予强耦合引力新的洞见，暗示其可能是涌现现象；这些对偶为相关模型提供了有用的生长解释与统一框架。

Abstract: We discuss the triality between stochastic growth models in the KPZ universality class, the DSSYK model, and low dimensional dilaton gravity. The TASEP model, which will be our primary focus, is identified with the strong-coupling limit of two-dimensional quantum gravity. The Hamiltonian of DSSYK and the transfer matrix of ASEP coincide up to an additive constant, and we derive the exact expression for the resolvent of the corresponding transfer matrix in ASEP on a finite interval. We also mention the relation of cumulants with the Tutte polynomial $T(1,q)$ of the crossing graph dual to the chord diagram, which implies the relationship of the models considered with the $Q=0$ Potts model coupled with gravity. We further exploit the realization of all these models in terms of different versions of discrete and continuous random walks on two-dimensional manifolds, which parameterize the Hilbert spaces of the respective theories. The representation of TASEP as the growth model of heaps provides new insight into strong-coupling gravity, suggesting its interpretation as an emerging phenomenon. The new dualities between ASEP and q-TASEP models and heap models providing a useful growth interpretation have been developed.

</details>


### [278] [Gravity and generalised geometry from a Lie 2-algebroid perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01292)
*Athanasios Chatzistavrakidis,Chris Hull,Larisa Jonke,Sylvain Lavau,Peter Schupp*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文基于dg辛流形（Courant algebroid）重构广义连接的自然挠率与曲率张量，消除既有方法的结构循环与歧义，证明唯一相容广义连接及Lie 2-algebroid的L∞拟同构等价性，并从新定义的广义Ricci张量导出闭弦无质量模有效作用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前文献在定义广义连接的挠率与曲率时存在结构循环性和歧义问题，难以唯一地从广义几何重构闭弦无质量模的有效作用量。

Method: 将Courant algebroid描述为微分分次（dg）辛流形；对分裂dg流形建立系统程序，从中提炼出作为更大Lie 2-algebroid结构一部分的广义Lie括号；据此无歧义地定义挠率、Riemann曲率张量及其Bianchi恒等式；引入协变广义度量相容广义连接并固定（余）挠率，结合普通分裂连接；通过L∞拟同构比较不同Lie 2-algebroid。

Result: 对任意分裂dg流形的普通连接选择，存在唯一的广义度量相容广义连接（固定（余）挠率）；所有由此得到的Lie 2-algebroid在L∞拟同构意义下等价；在给定Lie 2-algebroid上，唯一相容广义连接配合规范挠率选择，可从基于新广义Ricci张量的几何作用量复现闭弦有效作用量。

Conclusion: 以dg辛流形与Lie 2-algebroid为框架，可无歧义、无循环地定义广义挠率与曲率，并唯一确定相容广义连接；该框架在L∞拟同构下自洽，且能几何地重构闭弦无质量模有效作用量。

Abstract: We revisit the problem of defining natural torsion and curvature tensors for generalised connections and of using them to reconstruct the effective action for the massless modes of the closed string from generalised geometry. The starting point is the description of a Courant algebroid as a differential graded (dg) symplectic manifold. A systematic procedure for split dg manifolds singles out a generalised Lie bracket as part of a larger Lie 2-algebroid structure, with the right properties to unambiguously define torsion and Riemann curvature tensors together with their corresponding Bianchi identities. This framework eliminates the structural circularity and ambiguity problems of previous approaches in the literature. We show that a unique generalised metric compatible generalised connection with fixed (con)torsion exists for any choice of ordinary connection that splits the dg manifold, while at the same time all Lie 2-algebroids obtained in this way are equivalent up to $L_{\infty}$ quasi-isomorphisms. For a given Lie 2-algebroid we demonstrate that the unique compatible generalised connection reproduces the effective closed string action from a geometric action based on the new generalised Ricci tensor upon making a canonical choice of torsion.

</details>


### [279] [A celestial viewpoint on infrared divergences in QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01366)
*Lorenzo Magnea,Enrico Zunino*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文综述了红外QCD现象与天球二维共轭场论之间的对偶联系，重点讨论软胶子流的软共线因子化、共形场论描述以及多项发射下的算子乘积展开结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外辐射既是QCD唯象学的实用要素，也是理解规范理论长程结构的窗口。近十年来出现了新视角：软流来自天球上的渐近对称性，软色关联来自天球上的二维共形场论。作者旨在梳理该方法的现状。

Method: 首先从体物理图像出发，给出无质量散射振幅的红外因子化，说明普适红外信息如何映射到天球上的标记点；随后研究软胶子流，用体结果约束自由场之外的体球理论结构；（1）单次发射：偶极型圈贡献中的所有对数可吸收进强耦合的标度选择；（2）多次发射：分析混合螺旋度的天球算符乘积展开，及其能量分数加权。

Result: 软色偶极奇异项被天球上自由玻色子理论中的顶点算子关联函数在全阶上重现；多次发射时，新出现的混合螺旋度天球OPE必须由能量分数加权，从而破坏全纯因子化，并在连续共线极限下阻碍结合性。

Conclusion: 这些结果展示了红外QCD的熟悉物理与天球上二维理论动力学之间引人注目的联系，为非阿贝尔红外辐射提供了全新的场论描述框架。

Abstract: Non-abelian infrared radiation can be seen as both a practical ingredient for QCD phenomenology and a window on the long-distance structure of gauge theories. During the last decade, a novel viewpoint was developed on infrared phenomena: soft currents are seen as arising from asymptotic symmetries realised on the celestial sphere, and soft colour correlations similarly emerge from a two-dimensional conformal field theory on the sphere. In this contribution, we briefly review the status of this approach. We begin with the standard bulk picture, presenting the infrared factorisation of massless scattering amplitudes, and then we show how universal infrared information gets mapped to marked points on the celestial sphere. In particular, soft colour-dipole singularities are reproduced to all orders by a correlator of vertex operators in a free-boson theory on the sphere. We then proceed to study soft-gluon currents, and show how bulk results constrain the structure of the celestial theory beyond the free-field limit. For single emission, we note that all logarithms in dipole-like loop contributions can be absorbed into the choice of scale for the strong coupling. For multiple emission, we show how the emerging mixed-helicity celestial operator product expansion must be weighted by energy fractions, breaking holomorphic factorisation and obstructing associativity under successive collinear limits. These results illustrate a remarkable connection between the familiar physics of infrared QCD and the dynamics of a two-dimensional theory on the celestial sphere.

</details>


### [280] [From Modal Memory to Topological Relaxation in a Driven Holographic Superfluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01455)
*Yan Peng,Bo-Xuan Ge*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 研究全息超流体中驱动历史对非线性拓扑弛豫的影响：即使到达相同当前驱动条件并遵循相同未来协议，晚期动力学仍强烈依赖历史，倒数第二绕数扇区的停留时间可变化约3.7倍，该变化由进入共同放大阶段的模态不平衡所组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究拓扑弛豫是否仅由当前驱动条件决定，还是存在对驱动历史的非平凡记忆效应，即历史依赖性。

Method: 构造不同驱动历史使其达到相同的当前驱动条件，随后施加相同的未来驱动协议；比较其晚期弛豫动力学，并以模态不平衡（不同模式的激发比例）为变量分析弛豫时间。

Result: 尽管最终都弛豫到相同的末态绕数扇区，晚期动力学仍强烈依赖历史：倒数第二绕数扇区的停留时间变化约3.7倍；该差异由进入共同放大阶段的模态不平衡组织；不同形状但模态不平衡匹配的历史产生几乎相同的弛豫时间；第二对竞争模式呈现相同定性行为。

Conclusion: 非线性拓扑弛豫具有显著历史依赖性，其晚期时间尺度由拓扑放大前携带的模态不平衡所控制，而非单纯由当前驱动条件或最终拓扑扇区决定。

Abstract: We study how driving history affects the nonlinear topological relaxation of a holographic superfluid. Different histories are constructed to reach the same present driving conditions and then follow the same future protocol. Although they relax to the same final winding sector, their late-time dynamics remain strongly history-dependent: the residence time of the penultimate winding sector changes by about a factor of \(3.7\). This variation is organised by the modal imbalance carried into the common amplification stage. Histories with different shapes but matched modal imbalance produce nearly identical relaxation times. The same qualitative behaviour is found for a second pair of competing modes.

</details>


### [281] [Coherent Release Fronts in Krylov Chains and Thermal AdS$_2$ Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01862)
*Mohsen Alishahiha*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 研究有限串联Krylov扇区向具有渐近线性Lanczos系数的共形尾部的相干释放，推导精确有限截断占据核及其局部均匀移动截断极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究有限串联Krylov扇区与共形尾部耦合时的相干释放动力学，理解移动截断极限下占据核的结构及尾部与有限扇区对释放剖面的共同影响。

Method: 推导精确有限截断占据核，取局部均匀移动截断极限，以其导数为滤波边界发射幅度的模平方；利用连分数极点、滤波核与对数渡越尺度刻画释放剖面；并构造热AdS$_2$实现，在弱耦合极限下分析修饰振子测度。

Result: 获得精确有限截断占据核及移动截断极限；释放剖面早期边缘记录发射前串联步数，晚期边缘反映慢共振、干涉及合并极点带来的对数增强；构造出共享受主导连分数极点但剖面不等价的一站点与两站点族；热AdS$_2$弱耦合下修饰振子测度趋于两站点浅扇区加共形尾部，主导复极点可复现至二阶。

Conclusion: 共形尾部决定滤波核与对数渡越尺度，而释放剖面还依赖有限扇区的结构；主导极点不足以唯一确定释放动力学，需结合有限扇区信息；热AdS$_2$的弱耦合极限为上述机制提供了具体实现。

Abstract: We study coherent release from a finite serial Krylov sector into an exact conformal tail with asymptotically linear Lanczos coefficients. We derive an exact finite-cut occupation kernel and its locally uniform moving-cut limit, whose derivative is the squared modulus of a filtered boundary-emission amplitude. The tail sets the filtering kernel and logarithmic transit scale, while the release profile also depends on the finite sector. Its early edge records the number of serial steps before emission, whereas its late edge reflects slow resonances, interference, and logarithmic enhancements from merged poles. A one- and two-site family can share the same leading continued-fraction pole while exhibiting inequivalent profiles. We further consider a thermal AdS$_2$ realization in which, in the weak-coupling limit, the dressed oscillator measure approaches a two-site shallow sector followed by the conformal tail, with the leading complex pole reproduced through second order.

</details>


### [282] [On-shell renormalization of sine-Gordon by the quantum inverse scattering method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01571)
*Francesco Beccarini,Claudio Conti*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在量子逆散射框架下求解格点sine-Gordon模型，发现经由盒长重整化与在壳条件可定义一个重整化方案：截断依赖全部吸收进乘在盒长上的因子Z_L，场与耦合β不重整化，且该方案可推广到具有质量谱的超局域可积理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 格点上求解sine-Gordon模型时，其连续极限与无穷体积极限只有在盒长被重标度后才一致，作者希望把这一重标度操作提升为一个自洽的重整化方案，从而显式揭示可积性与重整化之间的相互作用。

Method: 在量子逆散射方法下于格点求解sine-Gordon模型；用乘以盒长的因子Z_L吸收全部截断依赖，其指数由顶点算子的标度维数确定；不重整化场与耦合β，不引入减除标度，仅对质量参数作有限重整化，并要求最轻呼吸子质量等于线性化理论中玻色子的质量；孤子与呼吸子质量取自单值化算符本征值，作为不跑动参数的函数。

Result: 孤子与呼吸子质量由单值化算符本征值给出，并随β→0退化为经典与半经典结果；质量比可直接由正则化构造得到，而格点参数与物理标度间的关系被Z_L吸收、无需计算。

Conclusion: 该重整化方案是对理论的一种重参数化，显式体现了可积性与重整化之间的相互作用；将其与Coleman正规序及共形微扰论进行比较，并论证它可推广到具有质量谱的超局域可积理论，可能包括允许超局域格点正则化的渐近自由模型。

Abstract: In the quantum inverse scattering method, the sine-Gordon model is solved on a lattice, and its continuum and infinite-volume limits become uniform only after the length of the box is rescaled. We show that this rescaling, supplemented by an on-shell condition, defines a renormalization scheme. The whole dependence on the cut-off is absorbed into a factor $Z_L$ multiplying the length of the box, with an exponent fixed by the scaling dimension of the vertex operator; neither the field nor the coupling $β$ is renormalized, no subtraction scale is introduced, and the mass parameter receives a finite renormalization, fixed by requiring that the lightest breather has the mass of the boson in the linearized theory. The soliton and breather masses follow from the eigenvalues of the monodromy operator as functions of parameters that do not run, and reduce to the classical and semiclassical results as $β\rightarrow0$. In particular, the mass ratios are produced directly from the regularized construction, while the relation between the lattice parameters and the physical scale is absorbed into $Z_L$ and never needs to be computed. The scheme is a reparameterization of the theory which makes explicit the interplay between integrability and renormalization. We compare it with Coleman's normal ordering and with conformal perturbation theory, and we argue that it extends to ultralocal integrable theories with a massive spectrum, possibly including asymptotically free models that admit an ultralocal lattice regularization.

</details>


### [283] [Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet quintessential inflation: From super-inflation to emergent warm inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01665)
*Omar Chahboun,Rachid Ahl Laamara,El Hassan Saidi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力中耦合三个标量场，用单一势与耦合函数统一描述暴胀与晚期宇宙；Gauss-Bonnet耦合依赖场内容导致两阶段暴胀，瀑布场凝聚振荡提供辐射，两阶段耗散保留GB贡献，四分之一势在暖暴胀下可行且$n_s$落在Planck $1\sigma$内、$r$被压低，第三标量在物质-辐射等时附近释放，暴胀子晚期充当暗能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个贯穿宇宙历史的统一宇宙学模型，避免为暴胀与晚期加速分别引入不同的势与耦合；同时检验此前在冷暴胀场景中被排除的势形式（如四分之一势）在包含高阶曲率与耗散效应时是否重新可行。

Method: 在Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力中耦合三个标量场，全历史共用单一势与耦合函数；令Gauss-Bonnet耦合依赖场内容而非人为指定，从而产生两阶段暴胀：高阶曲率项先主导、后转为次主导。超暴胀阶段因瀑布场的几何诱导稳定化失效而动态终止，终止场值由$(n-1)Q$组合决定。采用$\Gamma_\phi\propto T^3$的两阶段耗散机制，在保留Gauss-Bonnet贡献的条件下推导标量与张量谱。

Result: 得到两阶段暴胀图像：初始超暴胀阶段结束于几何诱导的瀑布场稳定化失效处。瀑布凝聚的相干振荡替代常规再加热，充当初始辐射源并建立后续暖相所需的热浴。标量与张量谱显示暴胀子涨落增长受热浴剪切黏度调控，这对模型可行性至关重要。此前在冷场景被排除的四分之一势变得可行，$n_s$位于Planck $1\sigma$带内，$r$被压低；谱指数符合是普遍性的，而内部自洽性把模型限制在$(n-1)Q$的窄带内。第三标量在物质-辐射等时附近释放，以与跷跷板机制相反的逻辑把极小质量尺度转化为极大耦合；晚期暴胀子持续充当暗能量。

Conclusion: 该框架在Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力下以单一场内容统一了暴胀与晚期加速：高阶曲率驱动两阶段暴胀并由瀑布场动力学自然终止，凝聚振荡与暖耗散共同给出可行的扰动谱，使四分之一势重新符合观测，并将暗能量识别为残余暴胀子；模型的内部自洽将其参数限制于$(n-1)Q$的窄区间。

Abstract: This work presents a unified inflationary and late-time cosmology, developed within Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet gravity coupled to three scalar fields, governed by a single potential and coupling function throughout cosmic history. Permitting the Gauss-Bonnet coupling to depend on the field content, rather than assigning it arbitrarily, produces a two-phase inflationary scenario: the higher-curvature term initially drives expansion and later becomes a subdominant. The initial super-inflationary stage ends dynamically when the geometrically induced stabilization of the waterfall field fails, at a field value determined by the combination $(n-1)Q$. Coherent oscillations of the waterfall condensate serve as the initial radiation source, replacing conventional reheating and establishing the thermal bath necessary for the subsequent warm phase. Utilizing a two-stage dissipation mechanism with $Γ_φ\propto T^3$, the scalar and tensor spectra are derived while retaining Gauss-Bonnet contributions, demonstrating that the growth of inflaton fluctuations is regulated by the shear viscosity of the bath, which is essential for model viability. A quartic potential, previously excluded in the cold scenario, becomes viable, with $n_s$ within the Planck $1σ$ band and $r$ suppressed. Agreement with the spectral index is generic, while internal consistency confines the model to a narrow band in $(n-1)Q$. Finally, the release of the third scalar near matter-radiation equality transforms a very small mass scale into a very large coupling by a logic inverse to the seesaw mechanism. At late times, the inflaton persists as dark energy.

</details>


### [284] [Tree-Level Interaction Vertices in the Linear Sigma Model with Quarks under Global Rotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01760)
*Luis A. Hernández,Eduardo Lazcano,R. Zamora*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文推导了全局旋转下含夸克线性σ模型的树级相互作用顶点，揭示旋转通过角动量选择定则与贝塞尔函数径向形状因子改变顶点的动量空间结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为旋转环境中相互作用物质的微扰计算提供量子场论基础，建立适应旋转体系对称性与有限几何的相互作用规则。

Method: 从旋转背景下的标量场与费米子场解出发，构造相应的谱表示，并用其计算介子自相互作用与Yukawa耦合顶点。

Result: 旋转修改了顶点的常规动量空间结构：纵向动量守恒伴随角动量选择定则，横向动力学由含贝塞尔函数乘积的旋转诱导径向形状因子描述。

Conclusion: 所得标量自相互作用与标量-费米子耦合结构可推广到更广泛的旋转量子场论，为旋转环境中的微扰计算提供构建模块。

Abstract: We derive the tree-level interaction vertices of the linear sigma model with quarks in the presence of global rotation. Starting from the scalar and fermionic field solutions in a rotating background, we construct the corresponding spectral representations and use them to evaluate the mesonic self-interactions and Yukawa couplings of the model. We show that rotation modifies the usual momentum-space structure of the interaction vertices: conservation of longitudinal momentum is accompanied by angular-momentum selection rules, while the transverse dynamics is encoded in rotation-induced radial form factors involving products of Bessel functions. The resulting expressions provide a set of interaction rules adapted to the symmetries and finite geometry of the rotating system. Although derived within the linear sigma model with quarks, the structures obtained for scalar self-interactions and scalar-fermion couplings can be extended to a broader class of quantum field theories under global rotation. These results provide the building blocks for perturbative calculations and contribute toward a microscopic quantum-field-theoretical description of interacting matter in rotating environments.

</details>


### [285] [An Algorithm for Generating All Berglund-Hubsch-Type Calabi-Yau Orbifolds and Their Mirrors. Classification of Maximal Admissible Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01957)
*Sergei Aleshin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 提出构造任意Berglund-Hübsch型模型对应的Calabi-Yau orbifold及其镜像对的算法并证明正确性，给出定义orbifold的群的显式生成元，提出极大容许群分类方法，并以Fermat型Calabi-Yau三维流形为例完成分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Berglund-Hübsch型模型对应的Calabi-Yau orbifold及其镜像对的构造与分类缺乏系统性算法，需要一种可证明正确的通用构造方法。

Method: 提出构造与任意Berglund-Hübsch型模型相关的所有Calabi-Yau orbifold及其镜像对的算法，并给出正确性证明；同时给出定义相应orbifold的群的显式生成元，并提出极大容许群的分类方法；将该分类方法应用于任意Fermat型Calabi-Yau流形。

Result: 算法可构造出与任意Berglund-Hübsch型模型相关的全部Calabi-Yau orbifold及镜像对，并给出定义群的显式生成元；分类方法适用于任意Fermat型Calabi-Yau流形；作为示例，完成Fermat型Calabi-Yau三维流形所有极大容许群的分类。

Conclusion: 该工作为Berglund-Hübsch型模型的orbifold与镜像构造提供了通用且可证明正确的算法，并建立了极大容许群的分类框架，可用于Fermat型Calabi-Yau流形的系统分类。

Abstract: In this paper, we present an algorithm for constructing all Calabi-Yau orbifolds and their mirror pairs associated with an arbitrary Berglund-Hubsch-type model, and prove its correctness. In particular, the proposed method yields explicit generators for the groups defining the corresponding orbifolds. The method for classifying maximal admissible groups is proposed. We illustrate the application of the classification approach to an arbitrary Fermat-type Calabi-Yau manifold. In addition, as an illustrative example, we classify all maximal admissible groups of Fermat-type Calabi-Yau threefolds.

</details>


### [286] [From Hidden Zeros to Exact Splittings and New Zeros](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01775)
*Yang Li,Laurentiu Rodina*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 将隐藏零点提升为显式分裂公式，系统组织完整振幅并发现新的零点族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐藏零点对散射振幅施加了超越通常因子化性质的约束，需探索其在更一般运动学下的结构并寻找新零点。

Method: 提出精确分裂结构 A=Σ_α R_α J_{α,L} J_{α,R} J_{α,out}，适用于任意隐藏零点；各前因子 R_α 为隐藏零点轨迹条件，其余因子为低点振幅或流。

Result: 有序 Tr(φ^3) 振幅、NLSM 振幅与宇宙学波函数共享该分裂结构；系统地找到常规零点之外的新零点轨迹，包括五个新的全重数零点族，以及系数可连续变化的连续零点族。

Conclusion: 分裂表示逐项显式体现隐藏消失，为系统寻找新零点轨迹提供框架。

Abstract: Hidden zeros impose constraints on scattering amplitudes beyond their familiar factorization properties. We explore their structure in two related directions. We first promote hidden zeros to explicit splitting formulas that organize full amplitudes at generic kinematics. Ordered $\mathrm{Tr}(φ^3)$ amplitudes, nonlinear sigma model (NLSM) amplitudes, and cosmological wavefunctions share the same defining exact splitting structure $\mathcal A=\sum_αR_α\,
  {\mathcal J}_{α,L}{\mathcal J}_{α,R}
  {\mathcal J}_{α,\mathrm{out}}$, adapted to any hidden zero. Each prefactor $R_α$ is a condition of the hidden zero locus, so this decomposition manifests the hidden vanishing term by term. The remaining factors are lower point amplitudes or currents. Using these representations, we can systematically find new zero loci beyond the usual ones. In particular, we obtain five new all-multiplicity zero families. We also find continuous families of zeros: the coefficients of their defining equations can vary continuously while the amplitude remains zero.

</details>


### [287] [Super Liouville theory on the torus: bootstrap and the super Virasoro minimal string](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01909)
*Scott Collier,Beatrix Mühlmann,Mukund Rangamani,Ioannis Tsiares,Jianming Zheng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新研究了环面上 $\mathcal{N}=1$ 超Liouville理论，修正了环面单点超Virasoro块的递推表示，识别出奇自旋结构中的一个新块，并利用bootstrap构造了四个自旋结构扇区的模交核，最后应用于超Virasoro极小弦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受低维世界面弦构造应用的驱动，需要更精确地理解环面上的 $\mathcal{N}=1$ 超Liouville理论及其在超Virasoro极小弦中的应用。

Method: 重新审查并修正文献中环面单点超Virasoro块的递推表示；识别奇自旋结构中新块；利用bootstrap论证构造四个自旋结构扇区的模交核；结合GSO投影与世界面超荷选择分析四种超Virasoro极小弦理论。

Result: 修正了环面单点超Virasoro块递推表示的若干成分；发现奇自旋结构中与特权超后代相关的新块；证明类空与类时超Liouville结构常数满足模交；构造了中心荷 $c \in \mathbb{C}\setminus(-\infty,\tfrac{3}{2}]$ 下四个扇区的模交核；NS扇区球面三点与环面单点振幅与对偶矩阵积分谱曲线上的拓扑递归一致；发现 $\mathrm{0A}^{-}$ 理论中例外零动量Ramond插入并匹配混合球面三点振幅。

Conclusion: 该工作完善了环面超Liouville理论的红外与模性质描述，为超Virasoro极小弦的对偶矩阵积分提供了一致性验证，并揭示了奇自旋结构和Ramond插入的关键作用。

Abstract: Motivated by applications to low-dimensional worldsheet string constructions, we analyze $\mathcal{N} = 1$ super Liouville theory on the torus. Specifically, we re-examine the recursive representations of the torus one-point super Virasoro blocks that have been constructed in the literature and correct several of their ingredients. In the process we identify a new block in the odd spin structure, associated with a privileged superdescendant whose torus one-point function transforms like that of a Virasoro primary. We then demonstrate that the structure constants for both spacelike and timelike super Liouville theory satisfy modular crossing. Furthermore, we use a bootstrap argument to construct modular crossing kernels for all four spin structure sectors, valid for central charges $c \in \mathbb{C}\setminus(-\infty,\tfrac{3}{2}]$, and verify that they correctly implement the crossing transformations of the blocks. As a concrete application of the technology, we analyze the super Virasoro minimal string. There are four such theories, $\mathrm{0A}^{\pm}$ and $\mathrm{0B}^{\pm}$, which are distinguished by the GSO projection together with the choice of worldsheet supercharge. The resulting NS-sector sphere three-point and torus one-point amplitudes are shown to agree with topological recursion on the spectral curves of the dual matrix integrals, which were recently derived from 3d supergravity. Notably, the one-point function of the privileged superdescendant plays an essential role in the contribution of the odd spin structure to the torus amplitude via a careful treatment of the picture changing operator. We also identify an exceptional zero-momentum Ramond insertion in the $\mathrm{0A}^{-}$ theory that we interpret geometrically as a Ramond puncture, and match its mixed sphere three-point amplitude to the corresponding intersection theory prediction.

</details>


### [288] [Trinity of Carrollian Gravity: Dynamically Equivalent Formulations of Magnetic Carrollian Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01976)
*Mehdi Ahmadi-Jahmani,Aliasghar Parvizi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将洛伦兹"引力三位一体"范式推广至Carroll几何，构建磁Carroll引力的动力学等价理论框架，证明其曲率可换取挠率与非度规性的动能密度，并建立向张量规范理论、电磁学与膨胀子理论的几何词典。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 洛伦兹三位一体（几何 Trinity）已成功将引力映射为等价替代引力框架，但Carroll几何下的类似结构尚缺乏系统构建。作者希望利用退化度规上仿射联络的内在自由度，探索磁Carroll引力的等价表述，并检验Carroll情形是否能超越"引力到引力"的映射、实现时空几何到非引力理论的转化。

Method: 以Carroll几何为平台，将三位一体范式推广至退化度规；利用仿射联络的歧义与自由度，系统放松刚性磁扇区的度规相容性与零挠率约束；对修改后的联络施加全局平坦性条件，证明磁Carroll宇宙的曲率可动力学地转化为挠率与非度规性的孤立动能密度；进一步构造具有最小非度规性的对称远平行Carroll联络。

Result: 证明曲率可换取挠率/非度规性动能项；内在挠率恢复电Carroll引力，作为磁Carroll引力的远平行等价；纯空间挠率重现类标量荷Fracton规范理论磁哈密顿量的动能项；时间挠率扇区分别对应U(1) Maxwell电场与Carroll-Weyl共形膨胀子理论；对称远平行Carroll联络下，磁Carroll拉氏量动力学等价于超局域、各向同性的电Carroll引力。

Conclusion: 建立了将Carroll引力动力学翻译为张量规范理论、电磁学与膨胀子理论的几何词典，表明Carrollian等价可超越洛伦兹情形，实现时空几何与非引力理论之间的映射，为退化几何下的引力等价表述提供了统一框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we extend the paradigm of the Geometrical Trinity of Gravity to the Carrollian geometry to construct a comprehensive framework of dynamically equivalent theories for Magnetic Carrollian gravity. Utilizing the inherent ambiguities and freedoms of affine connections on degenerate metrics, we systematically relax the metric-compatibility and zero-torsion constraints of the rigid magnetic sector. By imposing global flatness on these modified connections, we prove that the curvature of a Magnetic Carrollian universe can be dynamically traded for isolated kinetic energy densities of torsion and non-metricity. Unlike the Lorentzian Trinity which maps gravity to alternative gravitational frameworks, the Carrollian equivalences can also map spacetime geometry to non-gravitational theories. Specifically, we demonstrate that intrinsic torsion recovers Electric Carrollian gravity as the Teleparallel equivalent of Magnetic Carroll gravity; purely spatial torsion reproduces kinetic terms stracturally similar to the magnetic Hamiltonian of scalar-charge Fracton gauge theory; and temporal torsion sectors map independently to $U(1)$ Maxwell electric fields and Carroll-Weyl conformal dilaton theories. Finally, by formulating a Symmetric Teleparallel Carroll connection with minimal non-metricity, we show that the Magnetic Carrollian Lagrangian is dynamically equivalent to an ultralocal, isotropic Electric Carrollian gravity upon exploiting the present freedoms in connection. These results establish a geometric dictionary translating Carrollian gravitational dynamics into the language of tensor gauge theories, electromagnetism, and dilaton theory.

</details>


### [289] [Rotation and Anisotropic Scaling in Axionic Holographic Superconductors: AdS$_4$ versus Lifshitz$_5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02063)
*Moisés Bravo-Gaete*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文比较两种精确旋转轴子黑洞的探针超导扇区，用统一稳态形式导出主标量方程，指出旋转通过共转势进入，使规范场时间与空间分量不可分离；数值上旋转关联形变降低两背景的凝聚振幅，固定J下增大动力学指数增强Lifshitz凝聚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全息超导中的一个悬而未决问题是旋转与非相对论标度对称性如何竞争并改变凝聚的发生。作者希望通过对比两个精确旋转轴子黑洞的探针超导扇区来回答该问题。

Method: 选取两个精确旋转轴子黑洞背景：四维渐近AdS且带非最小耦合标量的解，以及五维渐近Lifshitz且z∈(2,3)的解。将两度规写成统一稳态形式，得到主标量方程，其中旋转通过共转势A_t−N^x A_x进入，从而说明探针规范场的时间与空间分量不能独立处理。

Result: 文献[1,2]报告的数值剖面表明：与旋转相关的关联形变在两个背景中都降低凝聚振幅；而在固定J时增大动力学指数会增强Lifshitz凝聚。

Conclusion: 由于参数α和J也会改变支撑相应精确几何的物质场，这些趋势应被解释为完整关联族（correlated families）的性质，而非单纯机械旋转的普适效应。

Abstract: An outstanding question in holographic superconductivity is how rotation and non-relativistic scaling symmetries compete to modify the onset of condensation. We address this by comparing the probe superconductor sectors of two exact rotating axionic black holes: a four-dimensional asymptotically anti-de Sitter solution dressed by a non-minimally coupled scalar, and a five-dimensional asymptotically Lifshitz solution with $z \in (2,3)$. Writing both metrics in a common stationary form yields a master scalar equation in which rotation enters through the co-rotating potential $A_t-N^xA_x$. This equation explains why the temporal and spatial components of the probe gauge field cannot be treated independently. The numerical profiles reported in [1,2] indicate that the correlated deformation associated with rotation reduces the condensate amplitude in both backgrounds, whereas increasing the dynamical exponent at fixed $J$ enhances the Lifshitz condensate. Since the parameters $α$ and $J$ also modify the matter fields supporting the corresponding exact geometries, these trends should be interpreted as properties of complete correlated families rather than as universal effects of mechanical rotation alone.

</details>


### [290] [Infrared Consistency and the Uniqueness of String Amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02124)
*Yu-tin Huang,Shi-Lin Wan,Zhuo-Hui Wang,Shuang-Yong Zhou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在最大超对称下，利用六点共线极限与标量宇称投影，将四点有效场论振幅的约束推广至低能展开的所有阶，求解主方程并证明Veneziano与Virasoro–Shapiro振幅的指数形式；同时给出由正规前向极限重构完整振幅的映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究表明，最大超对称结合六点标量宇称条件会对四点有效场论振幅施加非线性约束，数值bootstrap给出Veneziano振幅与Virasoro–Shapiro振幅（含无穷自旋替代）的证据。本文旨在将这些约束推广至低能展开的所有阶并予以解析证明。

Method: 采用共线极限，使三个三粒子因子化道（引力中为六个）同时上壳；通过标量宇称投影与单个离散R对称性消除未知的六点余项，得到完全由四点因子化决定的三项主方程；在显式解析与正色散假设下，分析固定反射偶部与前向谱测度的关系。

Result: 求解主方程证明了所猜想的指数形式，并给出由正规前向极限重构完整振幅的映射。规范理论中，指数中固定的反射偶部决定前向谱测度并唯一选出Veneziano振幅，有限自旋留数作为结果自然出现；引力中，指数不决定前向测度，支撑在单一质量层级的测度会重构出该质量处交换无穷多自旋的振幅。

Conclusion: 在所述紫外框架内，要选出Virasoro–Shapiro振幅，最低质量极点处的有限自旋支撑是一个额外必要假设；引力情形下无穷自旋的替代解无法仅由指数约束排除。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that maximal supersymmetry, combined with a particular scalar-parity condition at six points, imposes nonlinear constraints on four-point effective field theory amplitudes. Numerical bootstrap studies incorporating unitarity provide evidence for the Veneziano amplitude in maximally supersymmetric Yang--Mills theory and for the Virasoro--Shapiro amplitude, together with infinite-spin alternatives, in supergravity. In this paper, we establish these constraints to all orders in the low-energy expansion. The derivation uses a collinear limit in which three three-particle factorization channels, or six in gravity, simultaneously go on shell. A scalar-parity projection and a single discrete R-symmetry eliminate the unknown six-point remainder, leaving three-term master equations determined entirely by four-point factorization. Solving these equations proves the conjectured exponential forms and provides a map that reconstructs the full amplitude from its regular forward limit. Under explicit analytic and positive dispersive assumptions, the fixed reflection-even part of the gauge-theory exponential determines the forward spectral measure and uniquely selects the Veneziano amplitude, with finite-spin residues emerging as a consequence. In gravity, the exponential leaves the forward measure undetermined: a measure supported at a single massive level reconstructs an amplitude with infinitely many exchanged spins at that mass. Finite-spin support at the lowest massive pole is therefore an additional assumption required to select the Virasoro--Shapiro amplitude within the stated ultraviolet framework.

</details>


### [291] [A baby universe from a large family: booklet cosmology states and quantum error correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02168)
*Jingshu Dai,Binye Dong,Cheng Peng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文将AS²的宇宙态构造推广至三个及以上全息CFT，通过多路接合条件粘合各AdS页，定义"booklet宇宙态"；用循环复高斯随机张量建模三页插入，得到三分Haar态，并证明在每页输出维数b下，任何两臂可近似恢复维度为K的编码，当K/b→0时误差消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将AS²双页宇宙态构造推广到多页（三页及以上）情形，探索多边界CFT对应的体几何与宇宙学态，并研究其编码/恢复性质。

Method: 在多个AdS页的渐近边界上各放置一个CFT，通过施加多路接合条件沿公共界面粘合所有页；态由带多线性插入的欧氏演化制备。在适当重插入极限下，体几何中心形成闭合宇宙，称为booklet宇宙态。推广AS²的有效高斯假设，用循环复高斯随机张量建模三页插入，在平坦能量窗内得到三分Haar态；每臂为对应边界CFT的网络分支。

Result: 在三页且三个输出Hilbert空间维数均为b的最简例子中，任意两臂可近似恢复维度为K的指定编码，当K/b→0时误差消失且成功概率高。

Conclusion: 多页宇宙态（booklet）构造可行，其高斯模型给出三分Haar态，并具备从任意两臂近似恢复编码的信息论性质，推广了AS²的结果。

Abstract: We propose an extension of the cosmological-state construction of Antonini, Sasieta and Swingle (AS$^2$) to three or more holographic CFTs. Each CFT resides on the asymptotic boundary of an AdS page, and all pages are glued along a common interface by imposing the multiway junction conditions. The associated states are prepared by Euclidean evolution with a multilinear insertion. In appropriate heavy insertion limit the bulk geometry develop a closed universe in the center and we refer the state in this limit as a booklet cosmological state. Extending the effective Gaussian assumption used in AS$^2$, we model the three-page insertion by a circular complex Gaussian random tensor, yielding a tripartite Haar state in flat energy windows. In this Gaussian model of the cosmology-to-boundary map, each arm is a network branch associated with one boundary CFT. In the simplest example of three pages with three equal output Hilbert-space dimension $b$, we show that a prescribed code of dimension $K$ is approximately recoverable from any two arms, with vanishing error and high probability as $K/b\to0$.

</details>


### [292] [Leading gravitational dressing of operators and states in de Sitter space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02209)
*Steven B. Giddings,Zi-Yue Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在de Sitter空间中，通过求解与约束对易的条件，构造了引力"穿衣"可观测量（领头阶），要求底层场论观测量必须是dS不变的；以双粒子标量算符为例，其穿衣后关联函数再现了线性化Schwarzschild-dS解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在dS空间中，引力约束使得局域规范不变观测量难以定义，需要构造引力穿衣（gravitationally dressed）可观测量；本文旨在建立这类可观测量的构造条件与方法。

Method: 在牛顿常数领头非平凡阶展开；证明可观测量需dS不变才可穿衣；通过令穿衣观测量与约束对易来求解，用特定Green函数构造穿衣，并给出位于时间对称切片上观测量所需Green函数的构造；以双粒子标量态算符为例，在大质量极限下显式计算其位置与穿衣。

Result: 可穿衣的观测量必须dS不变；大质量极限下双粒子呈对跖（antipodal）分布，其穿衣可显式构造；涉及该态的关联函数再现了线性化Schwarzschild-dS解；此类dS不变观测量可视为关系型（relational），并可推广至含"观测者"性质的系统；与alpha vacua存在联系。

Conclusion: 引力穿衣可观测量为dS空间中规范不变量的构造提供了途径，其要求dS不变性，并可通过Green函数系统实现；结果暗示可推广至观测者系统与alpha vacua，但更完整的构造及其作用仍有待进一步研究。

Abstract: We describe construction of gravitationally dressed observables in de Sitter (dS) space, to leading non-trivial order in Newton's constant. We show that for an underlying field theory observable to be "gravitationally dressable," it must be dS invariant. If it satisfies this condition, its gravitational dressing can be constructed in terms of certain Green functions, by solving the condition that the dressed observable commute with the constraints. We provide a construction of the Green functions relevant for dressing observables lying on a time-symmetric slice. A simple example of a dS invariant observable is an operator creating a two-particle state of scalars. In the large mass limit, the particles are shown to be antipodally located, and the dressing of this operator (or corresponding state) can be explicitly constructed. Its behavior is diagnosed by certain correlators involving this state, and we check explicitly that these correlators then reproduce a linearized version of the Schwarzschild-dS solution. The underlying dS-invariant observables may be thought of as relational, and we argue can be generalized to include systems with properties of observers, which then could be likewise gravitationally dressed. We also briefly describe how such dressed observables can be related to alpha vacua, and discuss other questions regarding a more complete construction and role of such operators.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [293] [Self-Orthogonal $τ$-Tilting Modules and Tilting Modules III: Finitistic Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00105)
*Xiaojin Zhang,Panyue Zhou*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 论文研究τ-tilting模与经典1-tilting模的联系，证明在小有限维数条件与自扩张消失有限范围下τ-tilting模即为经典1-tilting模；并建立自正交τ-tilting猜想与有限维数猜想之间的蕴含关系，给出radical cube为零情形下的验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: τ-tilting理论是倾斜理论的重要推广，但其与经典倾斜模的关系、以及自正交τ-tilting模何时为经典倾斜模尚不清楚；同时有限维数猜想是表示论核心问题，需要建立它与自正交τ-tilting猜想之间的联系。

Method: 设A为Artin代数，T为τ-tilting右A模，令C=A/Ann_A(T)。利用C^op的小有限维数有限性以及T的自扩张消失的有限范围，进行同调代数与τ-tilting理论的论证。

Result: 若C^op的小有限维数有限且T的自扩张消失范围有限，则T为经典1-tilting模；若有限维数猜想对所有Artin代数成立，则自正交τ-tilting猜想对所有Artin代数成立；自正交τ-tilting猜想的反例可给出有限维数猜想的反例；在radical cube为零的Artin代数上自正交τ-tilting猜想成立。

Conclusion: 建立了τ-tilting模与经典1-tilting模的判别条件，并揭示了自正交τ-tilting猜想与有限维数猜想之间的深刻联系，验证了一类特殊代数上的结论。

Abstract: Let $A$ be an Artin algebra and let $T$ be a $τ$-tilting right $A$-module. Put $C=A/{\rm Ann}_A(T)$. We prove that finite little finitistic dimension of $C^{\rm op}$, together with a finite range of self-extension vanishing for $T$, forces $T$ to be a classical $1$-tilting module. We also show that if the finitistic conjecture holds for all Artin algebras then the self-orthogonal $τ$-tilting conjecture holds for all Artin algebras. Moreover, a counterexample to the self-orthogonal $τ$-tilting conjecture will give a counterexample to the finitistic dimension conjecture. As an application, the self-orthogonal $τ$-tilting conjecture holds for Artin algebras with radical cube zero.

</details>


### [294] [A characterization of triangulated structures induced by premutation triples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00135)
*Panyue Zhou*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明预变异三元组(S,Z,V)的相容条件(MT4)是商范畴Z/[I]上Iitsuka典范预三角结构成为三角结构的充要条件，并刻画同心双余挠对情形下Nakaoka相容条件与(MT4)的等价性，最后构造出反例否定Iitsuka的一个问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Iitsuka引入预变异三元组作为各类变异构造的统一框架，并证明商范畴Z/[I]具有典范预三角结构，但其成为三角结构的条件尚不清晰；同时需要厘清与Nakaoka三角商构造中所用相容条件的关系，并回答Iitsuka提出的可约三元组是否为变异三元组的问题。

Method: 在外三角范畴中研究预变异三元组(S,Z,V)，令I=S∩Z=Z∩V；通过分析(MT4)相容条件在三角结构公理中的必要性，建立其充要性；对同心双余挠对比较Nakaoka相容条件与(MT4)；并构造具体的可约三元组实例进行验证。

Result: (MT4)不仅是充分的也是必要的，从而精确刻画了Iitsuka典范预三角结构何时成为三角结构；对同心双余挠对，Nakaoka构造三角商所用的相容条件等价于(MT4)；并给出一个可约三元组不是变异三元组的显式例子。

Conclusion: (MT4)是判定商范畴Z/[I]三角结构的精确判据，统一了与Nakaoka相容条件的关系，并通过反例否定回答了Iitsuka的问题。

Abstract: Let $(\mathcal{S},\mathcal{Z},\mathcal{V})$ be a premutation triple in an extriangulated category. Set $\mathcal{I}=\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{Z}=\mathcal{Z}\cap\mathcal{V}$. Iitsuka introduced premutation triples as a unified framework for various mutation constructions and showed that the quotient category $\mathcal{Z}/[\mathcal{I}]$ admits a canonical pretriangulated structure. In this paper, we prove that the compatibility condition $(\mathrm{MT4})$ is not only sufficient but also necessary for this structure to be triangulated. Therefore, $(\mathrm{MT4})$ precisely characterizes when Iitsuka's canonical pretriangulated structure becomes triangulated. Furthermore, we prove that, for concentric twin cotorsion pairs, the compatibility condition used by Nakaoka in the construction of triangulated quotients is equivalent to $(\mathrm{MT4})$. Finally, we construct an explicit reducible triple which is not a mutation triple. This gives a negative answer to a question posed by Iitsuka.

</details>


### [295] [Explicit Transgression Primitives on Flag Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00300)
*Greg Weiler*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文在相对Weil代数与旗流形K/T上构造了特征形式p_j(F_t)的相对移调原语α_j，给出对所有j≥1的通用闭式，并揭示j=4时的结构跃迁，最后用Koszul DGA消解恢复K/T的Poincaré多项式与Euler特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Borel定理以余不变代数刻画旗流形上同调并断言特征形式p_j(F_t)=tr(F_t^j)消失，但该结论是非构造性的；从Weil代数W(k)投影得到的闭原语在K上无法下降到K/T上，因此需要构造可下降的显式相对原语，并给出无矩阵秩歧义的系统方法。

Method: 引入相对移调原语α_j∈(Λ^{2j-1}m^∨)^T≅^KΩ^{2j-1}(K/T)，满足d_m α_j=-p_j(F_t)；通过连接θ_s=θ_t+sθ_m将曲率分解为F_s=(1-s)(F_t-sθ_m^2)，利用非交换字和展开与Euler Beta积分导出所有j≥1的通用闭式；在A_2根格(𝔰𝔩_3(ℂ))上将T-不变上链解释为闭环，并用极小Koszul DGA消解K_•(p_2,…,p_k)处理余不变代数。

Result: 得到所有j≥1的通用闭式原语α_j；发现结构跃迁：α_2=-⅓tr(θ_m^3)无曲率项，α_3关于F_t线性，而j=4时[F_t,θ_m^2]≠0使循环迹对称性破裂为独立项链，产生不可约不变量tr(θ_m∧F_t∧θ_m^2∧F_t)；在A_2上α_2=Ω_-−Ω_+为三角形有向差，d_m充当边缘碎裂算子，见证c_2(E)的正合性；Koszul DGA消解恢复Poincaré多项式P_{K/T}(t)=∏_{j=2}^k(1-t^{2j})/(1-t^2)与Euler特征χ(K/T)=k!，避免了特征理论在k=4时Specht分解中出现的重数障碍。

Conclusion: 通过相对移调原语与Koszul DGA消解，本文以构造性方式实现了Borel定理所断言的特征形式消失，给出了显式闭式原语及j=4的结构跃迁刻画，并在无矩阵秩歧义的意义下恢复了K/T的上同调不变量，为旗流形上同调提供了新的组合-代数框架。

Abstract: Borel's theorem presents the flag manifold cohomology as the coinvariant algebra $H^*(K/T; \mathbb{C}) \cong S(\mathfrak{h}^\vee)/I_W$, establishing the vanishing of characteristic forms $p_j(F_\mathfrak{t}) = \mathrm{tr}(F_\mathfrak{t}^j)$ non-constructively; projection from the Weil algebra $W(\mathfrak{k})$ yields closed primitives on $K$ failing to descend.
  We construct relative transgression primitives $α_j \in (Λ^{2j-1}\mathfrak{m}^\vee)^T \cong {}^KΩ^{2j-1}(K/T)$ satisfying $d_\mathfrak{m} α_j = -p_j(F_\mathfrak{t})$ on $K/T$. Via connections $θ_s = θ_\mathfrak{t} + sθ_\mathfrak{m}$, the curvature factors as $F_s = (1-s)(F_\mathfrak{t} - sθ_\mathfrak{m}^2)$; expanding via non-commutative word sums and Euler's Beta integral yields a universal closed formula for all $j \ge 1$.
  These primitives exhibit a structural transition: $α_2 = -\frac{1}{3}\mathrm{tr}(θ_\mathfrak{m}^3)$ is curvature-free, $α_3$ is linear in $F_\mathfrak{t}$, while at $j = 4$, $[F_\mathfrak{t}, θ_\mathfrak{m}^2] \neq 0$ shatters cyclic trace symmetry into independent necklaces, producing the irreducible invariant $\mathrm{tr}(θ_\mathfrak{m} \wedge F_\mathfrak{t} \wedge θ_\mathfrak{m}^2 \wedge F_\mathfrak{t})$.
  On the $A_2$ root lattice ($\mathfrak{sl}_3(\mathbb{C})$), $T$-invariant cochains correspond to closed loops, $α_2 = Ω_- - Ω_+$ is the oriented difference of triangles, and $d_\mathfrak{m}$ acts as an edge-fracturing operator witnessing the exactness of $c_2(E)$. While character theory encounters a multiplicity barrier at $k=4$ in Specht decompositions, a minimal Koszul DGA resolution $K_\bullet(p_2, \dots, p_k)$ resolves the coinvariant algebra, recovering the Poincaré polynomial $P_{K/T}(t) = \prod_{j=2}^k \frac{1-t^{2j}}{1-t^2}$ and Euler characteristic $χ(K/T) = k!$ without matrix-rank ambiguities.

</details>


### [296] [Magnitude of module categories and special biserial algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00413)
*Haruhisa Enomoto*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Børve–Horiatakis–Kalck关于表示有限代数模范畴magnitude的猜想：在代数闭域上，该magnitude至少为单模个数，且取等号当且仅当代数为special biserial。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Børve、Horiatakis和Kalck将表示有限代数模范畴的magnitude定义为不可分解模之间Hom维数矩阵的逆的所有元素之和，并证明它是一个Euler示性数（即Auslander–Reiten箭图的顶点、箭头和mesh的交替计数），进而猜想该magnitude至少等于单模个数，等号成立正好对应special biserial代数。本文旨在证明这一猜想。

Method: 先在代数闭域上处理表示有向代数：建立模范畴、去掉给定单composition factor的子范畴以及其理想商三者的magnitude之间的关系；借助Ringel与Vossieck的hammock理论，将该商等价于有限偏序集的表示范畴，并计算其magnitude。一般情形通过由标准形（standard form）分级所得的代数化归到表示有向情形。

Result: 证明了该猜想在代数闭域上成立：表示有限代数的模范畴magnitude不小于其单模个数，且等号成立当且仅当该代数为special biserial代数。同时给出了相关子范畴与理想商magnitude的计算结果。

Conclusion: Børve–Horiatakis–Kalck猜想得证，建立了模范畴magnitude与单模个数之间的下界关系，并刻画了取等号的代数类；所发展的表示有向代数与hammock理论方法为计算此类magnitude提供了工具。

Abstract: Børve, Horiatakis, and Kalck defined the magnitude of the module category of a representation-finite algebra as the sum of the entries of the inverse of the matrix of Hom dimensions between indecomposable modules. They showed it is an Euler characteristic, the alternating count of vertices, arrows, and meshes of the Auslander--Reiten quiver, and conjectured that it is at least the number of simple modules, with equality precisely for special biserial algebras. We prove this conjecture over an algebraically closed field. For a representation-directed algebra, we relate the magnitudes of the module category, the subcategory of modules without a given simple composition factor, and the ideal quotient by it. By the theory of hammocks of Ringel and Vossieck, this quotient is equivalent to a category of representations of a finite poset, whose magnitude we compute. The general case reduces to the representation-directed case through algebras obtained by grading a standard form.

</details>


### [297] [Big Finitistic Dimensions of Radical-Cube-Zero Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00433)
*Liang Chen*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 构造了一个十维代数 $A$（$(\operatorname{rad}A)^3=0$），满足 $\operatorname{findim}A=2$，$\operatorname{Findim}A=\infty$，$\operatorname{Findim}(A^{\mathrm{op}})=0$，从而否证了大有限生成投射维数猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大有限生成投射维数猜想（big finitistic dimension conjecture）长期未决。本文旨在构造反例，并系统研究此类小维数代数的投射维数结构。

Method: 通过显式逆syzygy构造，得到每一正有限投射维数的可数生成右模；逐步确定完整投射覆盖核，用非零 Ext 检测精确分解长度。对投射维数不超过一且嵌入投射模根中的模，用可逆线性算子分类；计算投射维数二模的维数向量；对一般 Artin 代数给出通过根零化理想与角的递归充分判据；并对双顶点各带一个环的初等根立方零代数证明大有限生成投射维数有限性及乘法映射判据；最后用精确表示嵌入证明投射维数二类的野性。

Result: 在任意域上构造出十维反例代数，推翻大有限生成投射维数猜想；分类了嵌入根中的投射维数至多一的模；确定所有投射维数二模的维数向量及达到尖锐维数界的模（含全部十四维投射维数二模）；对双顶点单环初等根立方零代数证明大有限生成投射维数有限，并给出界为一或二的乘法映射判据；证明投射维数二类野性，且在任意域上该类存在超分解纯内射模。

Conclusion: 该工作否证了大有限生成投射维数猜想，并系统刻画了根立方零代数的投射维数与模结构。

Abstract: We construct, over any field, a ten-dimensional algebra $A$ with $(\operatorname{rad}A)^3=0$ such that $\operatorname{findim}A=2$, $\operatorname{Findim}A=\infty$, and $\operatorname{Findim}(A^{\mathrm{op}})=0$. An explicit inverse-syzygy construction gives countably generated right modules of every positive finite projective dimension, disproving the big finitistic dimension conjecture. At each step we identify the full projective-cover kernel, and nonzero Ext detects the exact resolution length. For this algebra, we classify the modules of projective dimension at most one that embed in radicals of projective modules, with no generation restriction, in terms of invertible linear operators. We determine all dimension vectors of finite-dimensional modules of projective dimension two and classify those attaining the sharp dimension bounds, including all fourteen-dimensional modules of projective dimension two. For arbitrary Artin algebras, we give recursive sufficient criteria that control finite projective resolutions through radical-annihilated ideals and suitable corners. For finite-dimensional elementary radical-cube-zero algebras, we prove finiteness of the big finitistic dimension in the two-vertex case with one loop at each vertex and obtain multiplication-map criteria for bounds of one or two. Finally, an exact representation embedding yields wildness of the projective-dimension-two class and, over every field, a superdecomposable pure-injective module in that class.

</details>


### [298] [Endomorphism algebras of Gorenstein-projective modules over string algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00937)
*Yu-Zhe Liu,Zheng Xin*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究字符串代数的Gorenstein-投射支撑τ-倾斜对，通过极大好集合与有限相容图刻画它们，并在G条件下为End_A(T)构造平铺曲面模型，同时给出表示有限性的等价刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 字符串代数的τ-倾斜理论与Gorenstein-投射模是表示论中的重要课题；作者希望统一刻画Gorenstein-投射支撑τ-倾斜对，并通过曲面模型与相容图深入理解其自同态代数的结构与表示有限性。

Method: 将Gorenstein-投射支撑τ-倾斜对与一类特殊的极大好集合对应，构造有限相容图；在G条件下，对每个非零基本Gorenstein-投射模T建立End_A(T)的显式带标记平铺曲面模型。

Result: 建立了Gorenstein-投射支撑τ-倾斜对与极大好集合的对应及有限相容图；在G条件下得到End_A(T)的带标记平铺曲面模型；并证明A表示有限当且仅当对所有基本Gorenstein-投射模T，End_A(T)表示有限。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了字符串代数Gorenstein-投射τ-倾斜理论的组合与几何刻画，并将A的表示有限性与各End_A(T)的表示有限性等价起来，为后续研究提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Let $A$ be a string algebra. We identify its Gorenstein-projective support $τ$-tilting pairs with a distinguished class of maximal good collections and give a finite compatibility graph for them. Under the G-condition, we construct an explicit labelled tiled-surface model for $\mathrm{End}_A(T)$ for every nonzero basic Gorenstein-projective module $T$. Under the same condition, we show that $A$ is representation-finite if and only if $\End_A(T)$ is representation-finite for every basic Gorenstein-projective module $T$.

</details>


### [299] [Homology and higher-order intermediate categories for proper abelian subcategories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01155)
*Zheng-Gang Wu,Xianhui Fu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 在无需t-结构的情况下，作者在有限扩张窗口（含ℓ+1层扩张封闭真阿贝尔子范畴）上构造自然长正合同调序列，条件为负自扩张在1至ℓ+1度消失，并证明该界是统一尖锐的；在Krull-Schmidt情形下，用相容序列分类对取纯同调层封闭的中间子范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的长正合同调序列通常依赖t-结构（如导出范畴的截断），在仅有扩张封闭子范畴构成的有限层窗口时缺乏一般理论；同时，层数上界是否最优、以及如何分类保持同调层结构的中间子范畴，均需澄清。

Method: 放弃t-结构假设，直接在由ℓ+1层扩张封闭真阿贝尔子范畴构成的有限扩张窗口上构造长正合同调序列；以负自扩张在1,…,ℓ+1度的消失为唯一条件，并论证该层数界无法改进（统一尖锐性）；在Krull-Schmidt范畴中引入相容序列，用以分类对纯同调层取稳的中间子范畴。

Result: 在所述消失条件下得到自然的长正合同调序列；证明ℓ+1层的界是统一尖锐的（不可再降低）；在Krull-Schmidt情形下，给出了保纯同调层的中间子范畴的完全分类，即由相容序列参数化。

Conclusion: 该工作将长正合同调序列的构造从t-结构框架推广到更弱的扩张窗口设定，且所给层数界为最优；Krull-Schmidt下的分类结果刻画了保持层结构的中间子范畴，统一并强化了相关同调分解理论。
