Abstract: Without needing a $t$-structure, we establish natural long exact homology sequences on finite extension windows with $\ell+1$ layers of extension-closed proper abelian subcategories, assuming vanishing of negative self-extensions in degrees $1,\ldots,\ell+1$, and prove uniform sharpness of this bound. In the Krull-Schmidt setting, we classify intermediate subcategories stable under taking pure homology layers by compatible sequences.

</details>


### [300] [Central algebraic cohomology of Galois gerbes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01159)
*Taeyeoup Kang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究特征为零局部域及数域上 Galois gerbe 的中心代数上同调，系数取值于连通约化群与代数中心子群。对可由 Kaletha 刚 gerbe 出发的比较态射刻画的 gerbe，给出上同调阿贝尔化的纤维积公式；在数域上通过笛卡尔方块由上同调阿贝尔化与实局部化恢复完整上同调集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Galois gerbe 的中心代数上同调在自守型/局部朗兰兹对应中扮演关键角色，但其完整上同调集通常难以直接计算。作者希望借助阿贝尔化与局部化等工具，在一般系数（中心子群未必有限或连通）情形下建立可计算的结构性描述。

Method: 利用 Kaletha 刚性 gerbe 的比较态射，对满足条件的 gerbe 推导阿贝尔化上同调的纤维积公式，将其表达为有限中心商代数基本群与从 band 到中心子群的同态。对数域情形，不依赖比较假设，构造由阿贝尔化和实局部化构成的笛卡尔方块；并将公式特化到 Kottwitz 局部与整体 gerbe。

Result: 当 band 为有限型时比较态射存在；数域上完整上同调集可由其阿贝尔化及实局部化通过笛卡尔方块恢复；在非阿基米德局部域与全虚数域上，阿贝尔化映射为双射。

Conclusion: 中心代数上同调的结构可通过阿贝尔化与局部化数据完全刻画，并适用于 Kaletha 与 Kottwitz 的 gerbe 框架，为该类上同调的计算提供了统一且可操作的途径。

Abstract: We study central algebraic cohomology of Galois gerbes over local fields of characteristic zero and number fields, with coefficients in a connected reductive group and an algebraic central subgroup, not necessarily finite or connected. For gerbes admitting a comparison morphism from Kaletha's rigid gerbe, we obtain fiber product formulas for abelianized cohomology in terms of algebraic fundamental groups of finite central quotients and homomorphisms from the band to the central subgroup. Such comparisons exist whenever the band is of finite type. Over number fields, independently of the comparison hypothesis, the full cohomology set is recovered from its abelianization and its real localizations through a cartesian square. Over non-Archimedean local fields and totally imaginary number fields, abelianization is bijective. We also specialize the formulas to Kottwitz's local and global gerbes.

</details>


### [301] [Harmonic Norms on Quiver Representations over Non-Archimedean Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01694)
*Oren Ben-Bassat*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 证明任意完备非阿基米德域上有限箭图的每个多稳定表示在HKKP意义下均存在分裂调和范数，回答HKKP预印本中的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HKKP在《Towards Categorical Kähler Geometry》中提出：多稳定表示是否具有分裂调和范数。该问题此前受值群与箭图限制，缺乏一般性结论，本文旨在完全去掉这些限制。

Method: 利用Moret-Bailly闭像定理获得与失稳子对象的均匀分离；对范数权重作有限估计得到稳定表示的线性矫顽力界，从而在全体范数的完备CAT(0)空间上取到扩展HKKP势的最小值；通过直接一阶变分计算将分裂极小元等同于HKKP调和范数；用严格支撑铰链把分裂可达性归约为带任意实半径标号的箭图半稳定归约；微小扰动提升分裂使每个反向扩张非分裂，并用有限斜率—秩下降证明终止性。

Result: 证明了任意完备非阿基米德域上任意箭图的每个多稳定表示都存在分裂调和范数；完备范数空间中的每个极小值都由具有相同箭头奇异剖面的分裂范数达到，且对值群和箭图无任何限制；非分裂极小元在一、二半径修正中展示分裂可达性，双参数Berkovich解析族分离了多稳定性、半稳定性与所选分裂范数的调和性。

Conclusion: 本文彻底解决了HKKP提出的分裂调和范数存在性问题，给出不依赖值群与箭图类型的普适性结论，并借助Berkovich解析族刻画了多稳定、半稳定与调和范数之间的关系。

Abstract: Let $K$ be any complete non-Archimedean field, not necessarily spherically complete. We prove that every polystable representation of a finite quiver over $K$ admits a split harmonic norm in the sense of Haiden--Katzarkov--Kontsevich--Pandit (HKKP), answering a question from their preprint \emph{Towards Categorical Kähler Geometry}. Moret-Bailly's closed-image theorem gives a uniform separation from representations with destabilizing subobjects. A finite estimate on norm weights then yields a linear coercivity bound for stable representations and hence a minimum of the extended HKKP potential on the complete CAT(0) space of all norms. A direct first-variation computation identifies split minimizers with HKKP harmonic norms. Strict supporting hinges reduce split attainment to semistable reduction for quivers with arbitrary real radius labels. Small changes of the lifted splitting make each reversed extension nonsplit; a finite slope--rank descent proves termination. Consequently, every minimum in the completed norm space is attained by a split norm with the same arrow singular profiles, without restrictions on the value group or the quiver. Nonsplit minimizers illustrate split attainment in one and two radius modifications, and a two-parameter Berkovich analytic family separates polystability, semistability, and harmonicity of a chosen split norm.

</details>


### [302] [Rotation fixed points of undecorated braid varieties and fusion rings of affine Lie algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02034)
*Yuma Mizuno*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了辫子簇上Zamolodchikov变换的不动点栈的粗模空间同构于相应仿射Lie代数融合环的谱，并将结果推广到twisted Coxeter元素，从而以统一方式实现所有仿射Lie代数的融合环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单连通半单代数群G的（未装饰）辫子簇上，由词的循环旋转诱导出一个有限阶自等价，称为Zamolodchikov变换。作者希望刻画该自等价的不动点栈，并揭示其与仿射Lie代数融合环之间的深层联系。

Method: 设G为单连通半单代数群，考虑与Coxeter元素幂相伴的辫子簇，利用词循环旋转诱导Zamolodchikov变换这一有限阶自等价；通过研究该自等价的不动点栈，计算其粗模空间，并与相应仿射Lie代数的融合环谱进行同构比较；进一步将构造推广至twisted Coxeter元素的情形。

Result: Zamolodchikov变换的不动点栈的粗模空间同构于相应仿射Lie代数融合环的谱；该结果可推广到twisted Coxeter元素，并且所有仿射Lie代数的融合环均可由此方式得到。

Conclusion: 辫子簇上的Zamolodchikov变换为仿射Lie代数融合环提供了一个统一的几何实现，涵盖非twisted与twisted Coxeter元素，表明辫子簇的几何与仿射融合环之间存在自然的同构对应。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a simply connected semisimple algebraic group. On the (undecorated) braid variety associated with a power of a Coxeter element, there is a finite-order autoequivalence, called the Zamolodchikov transformation, induced by cyclic rotation of words. In this paper, we show that the coarse moduli space of the fixed-point stack of this autoequivalence is isomorphic to the spectrum of the fusion ring of the corresponding affine Lie algebra. The above results also extend to twisted Coxeter elements, and the fusion rings of all affine Lie algebras are obtained in this way.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [303] [Detecting Einstein metrics on products of spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00066)
*Anna Siffert*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在 \(S^5\times S^2,\ S^5\times S^3,\ S^5\times S^4,\ S^7\times S^2\) 上构造了无穷多个两两非等距的实解析 Einstein 度量；除有限个外，它们均非 Riemann 乘积或经典双和 Böhm 度量，且不属于同伦缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望在乘积流形上寻找超越已知 Riemann 乘积与经典 Böhm 度量的新型 Einstein 度量。关键在于处理由奇异轨道出发、经尺度放大后进入 Ricci-平坦圆锥极限所产生的振荡闭合条件，从而证明解的存在性与无穷多性。

Method: 构造具有 \(dt^2+a(t)^2g_{S^p}+b(t)^2g_{S^p}+c(t)^2g_{S^q}\) 形式的共形/纤维型 ansatz，参数满足 \(p,q\ge2,\ 2p+q\le8\)。从一侧奇异轨道的两个非坍缩半径足够小的解族出发，放大该区域使 Einstein 方程趋于 Ricci-平坦问题，其解以衰减振荡方式趋近圆锥。不追踪完整且振荡加剧的 shooting map，而是从 Ricci-平坦极限提取两个响应量：其一确定公共振荡相位；去除该相位后，另一闭合条件化为标量符号变化。利用统一的内-外匹配论证，将极限符号变化传递到精确 Einstein 方程。随初始尺度趋于零，相位可任意多圈，闭合条件被满足无穷多次。

Result: 在上述四个乘积流形上得到无穷多个两两非等距的实解析 Einstein 度量；舍去有限个后，这些度量既不等距也不与 Riemann 乘积或经典双和 Böhm 度量同伦。闭合条件随尺度趋于零而反复满足，产生无穷多解。

Conclusion: 结果给出了一类新的、与已知乘积及 Böhm 度量本质不同的 Einstein 度量，并表明振荡性闭合条件可通过 Ricci-平坦极限中的相位与符号响应有效计数，从而系统性地产生无穷多个非等距解。

Abstract: On \[ S^5\times S^2,\qquad S^5\times S^3,\qquad S^5\times S^4,\qquad S^7\times S^2, \] we construct infinitely many pairwise non-isometric real-analytic Einstein metrics. After discarding finitely many members, none is isometric or homothetic to a Riemannian product or to a classical two-summand Böhm metric. The metrics have the form \[ dt^2+a(t)^2g_{S^p}+b(t)^2g_{S^p}+c(t)^2g_{S^q}, \qquad p,q\ge2,\qquad 2p+q\le8. \]
  The construction starts with solutions for which the two non-collapsing radii at one singular orbit are very small. After magnifying this region, the Einstein equations approach a Ricci-flat problem whose solutions tend to a cone by a decaying oscillation. The main point is to determine exactly which part of this oscillation can still be seen by the closing conditions at the other end of the manifold. Rather than following the full, increasingly oscillatory shooting map, we extract two response quantities from the Ricci-flat limit. One determines the common oscillatory phase; after that phase has been removed, the second closing condition is reduced to a scalar sign change. A uniform inner--outer matching argument transfers this limiting sign change to the exact Einstein equations. As the initial scale tends to zero, the phase makes arbitrarily many turns, and the closing conditions are therefore met infinitely many times.

</details>


### [304] [Gradient Ricci Solitons on PTR-Submanifolds of Complex Space Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00077)
*Jae Won Lee*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究复空间形式中部分全实（PTR）子流形上的梯度Ricci孤子，通过Gauss-孤子方程重建切向Kähler算子平方，给出谱编码、λ上界、刚性及非存在性等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复空间形式的PTR子流形上刻画梯度Ricci孤子的内蕴-外蕴耦合结构，将孤子数据与第二基本形式联系起来，从而用内蕴孤子量编码PTR切向谱。

Method: 建立Gauss-孤子方程：在非零全纯截面曲率下，由Hessian自同态与外蕴Ricci算子重建切向Kähler算子平方P²；结合相对零化条件、无迹各向异性恒等式与平行数据，并构造平坦多级Gaussian模型与非平坦Segre CR积作为验证。

Result: 紧凑极小PTR孤子在全纯截面曲率为正时满足λ ≤ c(n-1)/4，相对零化条件下为严格不等式；得到紧凑平凡性判据、精确的无迹各向异性恒等式、平行数据刚性结果及紧凑实超曲面的非存在性定理。

Conclusion: PTR切向谱可由内蕴孤子数据与第二基本形式共同编码，所得估计与刚性刻画揭示了重建公式的极限情形，并由显式模型加以校验。

Abstract: We study gradient Ricci solitons on partially totally real (PTR) submanifolds of complex space forms. In nonzero ambient holomorphic sectional curvature, the Gauss--soliton equation reconstructs the square $P^2$ of the tangential Kähler operator from the Hessian endomorphism and an extrinsic Ricci operator. Consequently, the PTR tangency spectrum is encoded by intrinsic soliton data together with the second fundamental form. For compact minimal PTR solitons with positive ambient curvature we obtain $λ\le c(n-1)/4$, with a strict version under a relative-nullity condition. We also prove compact triviality criteria, an exact trace-free anisotropy identity, a parallel-data rigidity result, and a nonexistence theorem for compact real hypersurfaces. A flat multi-level Gaussian model and a nonflat Segre $CR$-product provide explicit consistency checks and identify the limiting cases of the reconstruction formulas.

</details>


### [305] [A Quantitative Reeb-Form Obstruction for Real Hypersurfaces of Strict Nearly Kähler Six-Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00078)
*Jae Won Lee,Chul Woo Lee*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明六维严格近Kähler流形的五维定向实超曲面处处满足(dη)_p≠0，从而排除dη=0的诱导近接触结构，并给出Yano半对称度量联络Ricci张量非对称的推论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六维严格近Kähler流形中超曲面的诱导近接触结构是否会退化为接触（即dη=0）是一个自然问题；作者希望给出点态阻碍并揭示其联络论与分类学后果。

Method: 局部证明：仅使用超曲面恒等式∇_Xξ=φAX−G(X,N)与六维严格近Kähler代数。假设(dη)_p=0导出φA+Aφ=2G_N与Hopf条件；在接触超平面D_p=kerη_p上考察φA+Aφ与φ可交换、而G_N与φ反交换且G_N²=−αI的矛盾。

Result: 证明(dη)_p≠0对所有p∈M成立；对标准Yano半对称度量联络∇̂_XY=∇_XY+η(Y)X−g(X,Y)ξ，五维时Skew Ric^{∇̂}=−3/2 dη，故Ric^{∇̂}处处非对称；同时排除任何定义条件含dη=0的局部诱导近接触类，并在标准齐性严格近Kähler空间上给出例证。

Conclusion: 六维严格近Kähler流形的五维实超曲面不存在dη=0的诱导接触结构，该阻碍具有联络论与分类学意义。

Abstract: Let $M^5$ be an oriented real hypersurface of a six-dimensional strict nearly Kähler manifold $(\widetilde M^6,\widetilde g,J)$, and let $ξ=-JN$ and $η=g(\,\cdot\,,ξ)$ denote the induced Reeb field and Reeb one-form. We prove the pointwise obstruction \[ (dη)_p\neq0\qquad\text{for every }p\in M. \] The proof is local and uses only the hypersurface identity \[ \nabla_Xξ=φAX-G(X,N) \] together with the six-dimensional strict nearly Kähler algebra. If $(dη)_p=0$, then \[ φA+Aφ=2G_N,\qquad G_NX=G(X,N), \] which forces the Hopf condition. On the contact hyperplane $\mathcal D_p=\kerη_p$, the endomorphism $φA+Aφ$ then commutes with $φ$, whereas $G_N$ anti-commutes with $φ$ and satisfies \[ G_N^2=-αI_{\mathcal D_p},\qquad α>0. \] This contradiction proves the obstruction. As a connection-theoretic consequence, for the standard Yano semi-symmetric metric connection associated with $ξ$, \[ \widehat\nabla_XY=\nabla_XY+η(Y)X-g(X,Y)ξ, \] one has in dimension five \[ \operatorname{Skew}\operatorname{Ric}^{\widehat\nabla}=-\frac32\,dη, \] and hence $\operatorname{Ric}^{\widehat\nabla}$ is nowhere symmetric. The obstruction also rules out any local induced almost contact class whose defining conditions include $dη=0$. We illustrate the result on the standard homogeneous strict nearly Kähler spaces.

</details>


### [306] [A counterexample to Yau's bounded-mean-curvature embedding question](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00153)
*Haoxuan Cheng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 构造了一个$\mathbb{R}^4$上的完备黎曼度量，具界Ricci曲率与正单射半径，但全曲率无界，从而给出Yau问题的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Yau提出的问题：完备、Ricci曲率有界且单射半径为正的黎曼流形是否总能等距嵌入欧氏空间并具有界平均曲率。本文给出否定答案。

Method: 在$\mathbb{R}^4$上构造由互不相交的曲率"峰"拼接而成的光滑完备度量，使Ricci收缩一致有界；借助短测地曲率估计与Jacobi方程证明单射半径有正下界。再由Gauss方程导出曲率无界迫使任何$C^2$等距浸入的平均曲率无界。

Result: 得到具界Ricci曲率、正单射半径但全曲率无界的完备度量，其任何到有限维欧氏空间的$C^2$等距浸入均具无界平均曲率；构造适用于所有维数$\geq 4$，四维时Ricci曲率可趋于零。

Conclusion: Yau关于有界Ricci曲率与正单射半径流形可具界平均曲率等距嵌入欧氏空间的问题在维数$\geq 4$时答案是否定的，反例即为所构造的度量。

Abstract: Yau asked whether every complete Riemannian manifold with bounded Ricci curvature and positive injectivity radius admits an isometric embedding into some Euclidean space with bounded mean curvature. We construct a counterexample: a smooth complete metric on $\mathbb{R}^4$ with bounded Ricci curvature, positive injectivity radius, and unbounded full curvature. The Gauss equation then forces every $C^2$ isometric immersion of this metric into a finite-dimensional Euclidean space to have unbounded mean curvature. The metric is assembled from disjoint curvature peaks whose Ricci contractions stay uniformly bounded. A short-geodesic curvature estimate and the Jacobi equation give the uniform injectivity-radius bound. The construction works in every dimension at least four, and in dimension four the Ricci curvature may be chosen to tend to zero at infinity.

</details>


### [307] [Minimality and Nonlinear Spectral Rigidity of Hypersurfaces in Real Space Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00214)
*Zihao Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究欧氏、球面与双曲空间中超曲面的非线性平均曲率谱方程，证明在谱参数 σ≥nc 时极小，σ<nc 时给出梯度点的严格平均曲率界；该框架涵盖所有幂型响应，证明欧氏余维一的 Chen 猜想及常负曲率下双调和极小性，并给出脐点模型说明谱极小范围最优，同时得到紧致非极小测地球与等半径 Clifford 超曲面的定量局部刚性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一处理三种常截面曲率空间中的非线性平均曲率谱方程，并解决 Chen 猜想与双调和极小性等经典问题，同时刻画谱参数与极小性、曲率界的临界关系。

Method: 对容许解析响应 F 与外围截面曲率 c，采用共同的实解析延拓与焦迹（focal-trace）论证，结合显式脐点模型，并进一步使用定量刚性分析处理模去外围等距的局部唯一性。

Result: 当 σ≥nc 时超曲面极小；当 σ<nc 时在梯度点得到严格平均曲率界。该类别包含所有幂 F(t)=t^{p-1}, p>1，以及满足显式宽度条件的正混合。由此证明欧氏余维一的 Chen 猜想与常负曲率下的双调和极小性，且无需完备性或全纯性假设。显式脐点模型表明无条件的谱极小范围是尖锐的。此外，证明三类几何中紧致非极小测地球及球面中真等半径 Clifford 超曲面在模去外围等距意义下的定量局部刚性（对模型振幅附近满足 F,F'>0 的光滑响应律）。

Conclusion: 非线性平均曲率谱方程在三种常截面曲率空间中具有统一的极小性与曲率界理论，谱阈值 σ=nc 是最优的；主要结果在较弱假设下蕴含 Chen 猜想与负曲率双调和极小性，并通过脐点模型确认尖锐性，同时给出测地球与 Clifford 超曲面的定量局部刚性。

Abstract: We study nonlinear mean-curvature spectral equations for hypersurfaces in Euclidean, spherical and hyperbolic space. For an admissible analytic response $F$ and ambient sectional curvature $c$, we prove minimality when the spectral parameter satisfies $σ\ge nc$, and strict mean-curvature bounds at gradient points when $σ<nc$. The class includes every power $F(t)=t^{p-1}$, $p>1$, and positive mixtures satisfying an explicit width condition. In particular, the result proves Chen's conjecture in Euclidean codimension one and biharmonic minimality in constant negative curvature, without completeness or holonomicity. A common real-analytic continuation and focal-trace argument treats all three geometries. Explicit umbilical models show that the unconditional spectral minimality range is sharp. Independently, we prove quantitative local rigidity, modulo ambient isometries, of compact nonminimal geodesic spheres in all three geometries and proper equal-radius Clifford hypersurfaces in spheres, for smooth laws with $F,F'>0$ near the model amplitude.

</details>


### [308] [Minimal equatorial fillings of low index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00247)
*Jacob Bernstein,Daniel Ketover*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究 $\mathbb{S}^3$ 中赤道极小填充的几何与拓扑限制，重点关注最低可能 Morse 指标的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极小曲面填充问题与 Morse 指标理论密切相关，但低指标情形下填充曲面的几何拓扑性质尚缺乏系统刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用极小曲面理论与 Morse 指标分析相结合的方法，对 $\mathbb{S}^3$ 中赤道边界的最低指标极小填充进行几何与拓扑限制的推导。

Result: 获得了 $\mathbb{S}^3$ 中最低 Morse 指标赤道极小填充在几何与拓扑方面的若干限制条件。

Conclusion: 最低指标的赤道极小填充受到严格的几何与拓扑约束，这为理解极小填充的分类与刚性提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We obtain restrictions on the geometry and topology of minimal equatorial fillings in $\mathbb{S}^3$ of the lowest possible Morse index.

</details>


### [309] [An exact hypergeometric Weierstrass representation for Smyth's free-boundary minimal disk in the regular tetrahedron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00434)
*Steven Finch*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文对正四面体情形给出了Smyth（1984）所证三个正交极小曲面的显式Weierstrass数据，导出了Gauss映射、超几何参数、总曲率、对称平方结构与刚性结论，并给出高精度数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Smyth（1984）证明了任意四面体内存在三个与边界正交相交的嵌入极小圆盘，但其证明是抽象的（通过斜四边形的Plateau解的共轭曲面得到），未给出显式表达。作者希望针对正（regular）四面体这一对称性最高的情形，把该构造完全显式化，以揭示其解析结构并验证已有数值结果。

Method: 在正四面体中研究斜四边形Q（边长为 c_iν_i，c_i为面面积、ν_i为外法向）上的Plateau解及其共轭曲面，显式求解相应的Weierstrass数据。通过识别Gauss映射为具有角 (π/4, π/2, πβ/2)（其中 β=arccos(-1/3)/π）的圆弧三角形的Schwarz三角形函数，并用超几何参数（关于β线性）刻画该数据。

Result: 给出了正四面体情形的显式Weierstrass数据：Gauss映射为上述Schwarz三角形函数，超几何参数线性依赖于β，且没有辅助参数、也没有自由参数；得到κ的Γ函数闭形式、g'的Wronskian闭形式、精确总曲率 2π(2β-1)、数据的对称平方结构以及刚性结论——由于 β/2 无理，射影单值化群稠密，数据不可为代数的。数值上将表示延拓到28位：Area/ℓ²=0.2172341554075574483760009126…，与Ken Brakke 2013年Surface Evolver计算一致。

Conclusion: 该工作把Smyth存在性定理在正四面体情形提升为完全显式的解析描述，表明该极小曲面族由唯一确定的超几何数据支配（无自由参数），并因其稠密单值化群而本质上是超越的，同时以高精度数值位验证了先前的数值计算。

Abstract: Smyth (1984) proved that every tetrahedron $T\subset\mathbb{R}^3$ contains three embedded minimal disks meeting $\partial T$ orthogonally, obtained as conjugate surfaces of Plateau solutions in a skew quadrilateral $Q$ whose edges are $c_iν_i$, where $\varphi_1,\varphi_2,\varphi_3,\varphi_4$ are faces of $T$, $c_i=\operatorname{Area}(\varphi_i)$ and $ν_i$ are outward normal vectors. For the $\textit{regular}$ tetrahedron we give the Weierstrass data explicitly. The Gauss map is a Schwarz triangle function for a circular-arc triangle with angles $(π/4,\ π/2,\ πβ/2)$, where $β=\arccos(-1/3)/π$; the hypergeometric parameters are linear in $β$ and there is $\textit{no accessory parameter and no free parameter}$. We record a closed form for $κ$ in $Γ$-values, a Wronskian closed form for $g'$, the exact total curvature $2π(2β-1)$, the symmetric-square structure of the data, and a rigidity statement: since $β/2$ is irrational, the projective monodromy is dense and the data $\textit{cannot}$ be algebraic. Numerically the representation extends to 28 digits -- $\operatorname{Area}/\ell^{2}=0.2172341554075574483760009126\ldots$ -- confirming a 2013 Surface Evolver computation of Ken Brakke.

</details>


### [310] [The Wasserstein space of the circle is not flat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00625)
*André Magalhães de Sá Gomes,Christian S. Rodrigues,Luiz A. B. San Martin*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文证明圆环 $S^1$ 上的 Wasserstein 空间不平坦，其截面曲率由 Fourier 系数上的辛形式控制，在 $n$ 阶 Fourier 模态张成的平面上曲率上界为 $3n^2$ 且在均匀测度处取等，因而无界；并修正了"底流形一维则 Wasserstein 空间平坦"的普遍预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实直线与区间的 Wasserstein 空间是平坦的，这导致了一个被广泛接受的预期：只要底流形是一维的，其 Wasserstein 空间就平坦。作者要检验这一预期，并以圆环 $S^1$ 作为反例，揭示曲率的来源与结构。

Method: 在由 Laplace 算子特征函数诱导的 $P^\infty(M)$ 切丛整体标架（Lott 观察到其存在）下进行计算：证明该标架是每个切空间的 Schauder 基，并在此标架中写出 Otto 度量及其 Levi-Civita 联络。对 $S^1$ 得到 $P^\infty(S^1)$ 的度量与联络系数的闭形式，表示为密度的 Fourier 系数的有限组合；同时直接从 $W_2$ 定义验证曲率。

Result: 圆的 Wasserstein 空间非平坦；截面曲率由 Fourier 系数上的辛形式给出，平面平坦当且仅当它对该辛形式是各向同性的；在 $n$ 阶 Fourier 模态张成的平面上曲率上界为 $3n^2$，等号仅在均匀测度处成立，故曲率无界。该结果说明 $P_2(S^1)$ 中均匀测度的任何邻域都不是 CAT(0)。

Conclusion: 圆环的 Wasserstein 空间不平坦，纠正了"底流形一维则 Wasserstein 空间平坦"的普遍说法；所得整体标架还被用于动力学计算，把扩张圆映射在其不变测度处推前作用的导数读作 Fourier 频率上的加权移位。

Abstract: We show that the Wasserstein space of the circle is not flat, in contrast with the flatness of the Wasserstein spaces of the real line and of an interval. Its sectional curvature is governed by a symplectic form on the Fourier coefficients, so that a plane is flat exactly when it is isotropic for that form, and on the plane spanned by the $n$-th Fourier modes the curvature is bounded above by $3n^2$, with equality exactly at the uniform measure. It is therefore unbounded. We compute it inside the formal Riemannian structure of Otto and confirm it directly from the definition of $W_2$, which also shows that no neighbourhood of the uniform measure in $P_2(S^1)$ is CAT(0). The result corrects the widely stated expectation that a Wasserstein space is flat whenever its base manifold is one-dimensional.
  The computation is carried out in the global frame of the tangent bundle of $P^\infty(M)$ induced by the eigenfunctions of the Laplacian of $M$, whose existence was observed by Lott. We prove that it is a Schauder basis of every tangent space and write Otto's metric and its Levi-Civita connection in it. On the circle this gives the metric and connection coefficients of $P^\infty(S^1)$ in closed form, as finite combinations of the Fourier coefficients of the density. A final section puts the frame to work on a computation from dynamics, reading the derivative of the push-forward action of an expanding circle map at its invariant measure as a weighted shift on the Fourier frequencies.

</details>


### [311] [The Heat Kernel of the Hodge Laplacian on an Invariant Subspace of 1-forms on the Hyperbolic Space $\mathbb{H}^{n}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00653)
*Petros Siasos*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文给出实双曲空间 $\mathbb{H}^n$ 上 Hodge Laplacian 限制在 $1$-形式不变子空间上的热核显式公式，建立跨维 Millson 型递推关系，并由此导出尖锐一致估计与长时间渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典标量热核的显式公式与界估计已较完善，但作用于 $1$-形式上的 Hodge Laplacian 热核（尤其在实双曲空间及其不变子空间上）缺乏精确闭式表达、维度间联系以及尖锐估计，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用 warped product（翘曲积）分解，将问题约化为特定子空间上的分量算子；分别对奇数维与偶数维求解，得到热核的闭式表达式；并建立连接不同维数热核的 Millson 型递推关系。

Result: 获得了奇数维与偶数维下 Hodge Laplacian 限制于 $1$-形式不变子空间热核的精确闭式公式；建立了跨维 Millson 型递推关系；基于显式表示导出了尖锐一致估计与长时间渐近行为，将经典标量热核界推广至实双曲空间上的 $1$-形式。

Conclusion: 该工作为实双曲空间上 $1$-形式热核提供了显式刻画与维数递推工具，并把标量情形的经典热核界自然推广到 $1$-形式，为后续谱几何与热核分析研究奠定基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct explicit formulas for the heat kernel of the Hodge Laplacian restricted to an invariant subspace of $1$-forms on the real hyperbolic space $\mathbb{H}^{n}$. Using warped product decompositions, the problem is reduced to component operators on specific subspaces, yielding precise closed-form expressions for both odd and even dimensions. We further establish Millson-type recurrence relations that connect heat kernels across differing dimensions. Based on these explicit representations, we derive sharp uniform estimates and long-time asymptotic behavior, extending classical scalar heat kernel bounds to $1$-forms on real hyperbolic spaces.

</details>


### [312] [Strict Log-Concavity of First Dirichlet Eigenfunctions under Symmetry in $\mathbb{CP}^n$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00716)
*Jia-Lin Hsu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了$\mathbb{CP}^n$（$n=2,3,4$）中具有凸边界的区域上，关于标准$U(1)\times U(n-1)$作用不变的Dirichlet第一正特征函数是严格对数凹的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究复射影空间中的几何分析问题，探究对称性假设下Dirichlet第一特征函数的凸性/对数凹性，此类结论与特征值估计、热核及几何不等式密切相关。

Method: 在$\mathbb{CP}^n$的光滑凸边界区域上考察第一Dirichlet特征函数在标准$U(1)\times U(n-1)$作用下的不变性，并证明其严格对数凹性；结论在维数$n=2,3,4$时成立。

Result: 对$n=2,3,4$，$\mathbb{CP}^n$中凸边界区域上$U(1)\times U(n-1)$不变的第一正Dirichlet特征函数严格对数凹。

Conclusion: 在$\mathbb{CP}^n$（$n=2,3,4$）的对称凸区域上建立了第一特征函数严格对数凹性这一几何凸性结果，为更高维情形及更一般的Kähler流形提供了参考。

Abstract: We prove that the positive first Dirichlet eigenfunction of every smooth domain with convex boundary in $\mathbb{CP}^n$ invariant under the standard $U(1)\times U(n-1)$-action is strictly log-concave for $n=2,3,4$.

</details>


### [313] [On the Bryant conjecture for minimal hypersurfaces in $S^4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00886)
*Chao Qian,Ya Tao*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明S^4中具有常数量曲率且每点三个互异主曲率的局部极小超曲面M^3必局部等距于极小Cartan等参超曲面，肯定回答Bryant猜想。核心是构造归一化局部图解的四阶jet集合在固定|A|^2≡S_0>3时的紧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bryant猜想关于S^4中具有三个互异主曲率、常数量曲率的极小超曲面的分类问题长期未解决，本文旨在给出肯定的证明，填补该几何分类问题的空白。

Method: 通过参数化方法将问题转化为归一化局部图解的研究，引入对第四阶jet的紧性分析：对固定S_0>3，证明满足|A|^2≡S_0的局部图解的四阶jet集合具有紧性，其中A为形状算子。利用紧性结合极小子流形方程与常数量曲率条件，排除非Cartan型解。

Result: 证明了所述jet集合\mathscr{G}_{S_0}是紧的；进而推出满足条件的局部极小超曲面M^3局部等距于S^4中的极小Cartan等参超曲面，从而Bryant conjecture成立。

Conclusion: 本文证实了Bryant猜想，表明S^4中满足常数量曲率与三互异主曲率条件的局部极小超曲面只能是极小Cartan等参超曲面，为超曲面分类提供了新的紧性分析工具。

Abstract: We prove that a local minimal hypersurface $M^3$ in the unit sphere $S^4$ with constant scalar curvature and three distinct principal curvatures at every point must be locally congruent to the minimal Cartan isoparametric hypersurface in $S^4$, which provides a positive answer to the Bryant conjecture \cite{ChangLocal}. The key ingredient is to prove that, for each fixed \(S_0>3\), the set $\mathscr{G}_{S_0}$ of fourth-order jets of normalized local graph solutions satisfying \(|A|^2\equiv S_0\) is compact, where \(A\) denotes the shape operator.

</details>


### [314] [Monge-Ampère degenerations and conifold contractions in Fujiki class $\mathcal{C}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00942)
*Riley Guyett,Ryan Mc Gowan*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文研究具有常标量曲率的Calabi-Yau簇在小型crepant消解下的度量退化行为，给出例外轨迹之外的统一估计，并在三维情形证明Gromov-Hausdorff收敛到正则轨迹的度量完备化，从而推广Song关于Candelas-de la Ossa猜想的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Candelas与de la Ossa曾猜想Calabi-Yau簇在crepant消解下存在度量退化与收敛现象，Song已就此取得部分进展。本文旨在将相关结果推广到更一般的带有普通双点的Q-Gorenstein正规射影Calabi-Yau簇，并给出更为一致的估计与收敛性刻画。

Method: 设Y为具有普通双点奇点的Q-Gorenstein正规射影Calabi-Yau簇，π: X→Y为小型crepant消解且X属于Fujiki类C。作者在例外轨迹之外对给定的、可能存在退化的度量建立统一估计；当Y为三维时，进一步分析其度量完备化与Gromov-Hausdorff意义下的收敛。

Result: 在例外轨迹之外获得了度量的统一估计；当Y为三维时，证明这些度量在Gromov-Hausdorff意义下收敛到正则轨迹上Y的度量完备化。

Conclusion: 本文的结果推广了Song关于Candelas-de la Ossa猜想的工作，为更一般的Q-Gorenstein Calabi-Yau簇在crepant消解下的度量退化与收敛行为提供了理论支持。

Abstract: Given a $\mathbb{Q}$-Gorenstein normal projective Calabi-Yau variety $Y$ with ordinary double points as singularities and a small crepant resolution $π\colon X\to Y$ with $X$ in the Fujiki class $\mathcal{C}$, we establish uniform estimates away from the exceptional locus where the given metrics may degenerate. When $Y$ is a threefold, we prove that these metrics also converge in a Gromov-Hausdorff sense to the metric completion of $Y$ on the regular locus. In particular, our results extend the work of Song on a conjecture of Candelas and de la Ossa.

</details>


### [315] [Parabolic Monge-Ampère equations on a family of Calabi-Yau manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01202)
*Jiyuan Han*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文证明了抛物型Monge–Ampère方程的一致$L^\infty$估计，并将其应用于穿孔圆盘上一族极化Calabi–Yau流形上的Kähler–Ricci流，得到沿流的一致Skoda估计、一致直径界及Gromov–Hausdorff预紧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Kähler–Ricci流在流形族退化情形下的正则性，需要一个关于抛物型Monge–Ampère方程的一致$L^\infty$估计作为核心工具。

Method: 在初始测度与给定测度满足一致Skoda估计的前提下，证明抛物型Monge–Ampère方程的一致$L^\infty$估计；结合插值论证将该估计应用于穿孔圆盘上极化Calabi–Yau族上的Kähler–Ricci流。

Result: 建立沿Kähler–Ricci流的一致Skoda估计，并给出该流族的一致直径界与Gromov–Hausdorff预紧性，从而得到Li–Tosatti直径估计的一个替代证明。

Conclusion: 一致$L^\infty$估计是处理退化Kähler–Ricci流族的有力工具，可统一地推出Skoda估计、直径控制与紧性结论。

Abstract: We prove a uniform $L^\infty$-estimate for the parabolic Monge--Ampère equation under uniform Skoda estimates with respect to the initial and prescribed measures. We apply this estimate to Kähler--Ricci flows on a family of polarized Calabi--Yau manifolds over the punctured disk. An interpolation argument gives uniform Skoda estimates along the flows. We also establish uniform diameter bounds and Gromov--Hausdorff precompactness for this family of flows. In particular, we obtain an alternative proof of the diameter estimate by Li-Tosatti.

</details>


### [316] [Algebraic limits of Hermitian-Yang-Mills connections on Kähler surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01221)
*Keshu Zhou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文给出在Kähler曲面上对具界能量的Hermitian-Yang-Mills联络一致施行Hörmander $L^2$-估计的几何方法，证明光滑HYM联络的Uhlenbeck收敛可实现为解析平坦族内的代数收敛，并由此得到可光滑化族的连续性定理与固定拓扑下$\mu$-半稳定层的界性结果，同时讨论其在模空间问题中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hermitian-Yang-Mills联络的收敛性与模空间构造是复几何与代数几何交叉的核心问题；Uhlenbeck收敛的解析极限与代数（层论）极限之间缺乏统一的对应框架，且固定拓扑下$\mu$-半稳定层的界性及族的连续性对模问题的紧化与构造具有关键意义，需要一个能同时处理解析与代数两方面的几何工具。

Method: 发展一种几何方法来一致地对Kähler曲面上具界能量的Hermitian-Yang-Mills联络施行Hörmander $L^2$-估计；利用该估计将光滑HYM联络的Uhlenbeck收敛嵌入一个解析平坦族，并证明其可实现为该族内部的代数收敛。

Result: 建立了具界能量HYM联络上统一一致的Hörmander $L^2$-估计；证明了Kähler曲面上光滑HYM联络的Uhlenbeck收敛可在解析平坦族内实现为代数收敛；作为应用，得到光滑化族的连续性定理，以及固定拓扑下$\mu$-半稳定层的界性定理。

Conclusion: 解析收敛（Uhlenbeck）与代数收敛在同一平坦族框架下被统一，从而为光滑化族的连续性与半稳定层的界性提供新证明，并为模空间问题（如紧化与构造）提供潜在应用方向。

Abstract: We develop a geometric way to uniformly carry out Hörmander's $L^2$-estimates for Hermitian-Yang-Mills connections with bounded energy on Kähler surfaces. We then show the Uhlenbeck convergence of smooth HYM connections on Kähler surfaces can be realized as algebraic convergence inside an analytic flat family. As applications, we prove a continuity theorem for smoothable families and a boundedness result for $μ$-semistable sheaves with fixed topology. We also discuss potential applications for the moduli problem.

</details>


### [317] [Riemannian Structures on Quotients in Elastic Diffeology via Q-Charts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01346)
*Yusuke Shiobara*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文研究Q-charts（Blohmann弹性沿其下降的子浸没）上的黎曼几何下降问题，证明了弹性微分构造（连接、度量、Levi-Civita联络、测地线等）沿Q-charts双向保持性质，并将结果应用于无理环面给出完整黎曼几何刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在微分空间（diffeological spaces）框架下，Blohmann的弹性微分学提供了黎曼几何的推广。然而对于沿子浸没的下降问题（即何时底空间上的结构可由全空间上不变结构决定），尤其是Q-charts这一类的黎曼几何下降，尚缺乏系统研究。作者希望建立一套沿Q-charts 的黎曼装置下降理论，并将其用于主作用的商空间情形。

Method: 借鉴Miyamoto的Q-charts概念，考察弹性微分沿子浸没 $φ\colon X\to Y$ 的下降。核心工具是证明迭代切丛 $T^nX$ 及其纤维积 $T_kX$ 恰为 $φ$ 下相应丛的拉回。由此建立 $Y$ 上结构与 $X$ 上在保持纤维的过渡伪群 $Ψ(φ)$ 下不变结构之间的双射，并逐项验证无挠性、有效性、平坦性、满性、Levi-Civita性质与测地线方程在双向保持。对微分离散群主作用的商，全局提升测地线，证明以 $\R$ 为曲线对象的弹性空间类在此类商下封闭，并证明完备测地流下降。

Result: 对无理环面 $\Tor_α=\R/(\Z+α\Z)$ 完全确定其黎曼几何：设 $\vartheta$ 为 $dt$ 诱导的一形式，则每个黎曼度量都是 $\vartheta^2$ 的正常数倍；垂直连接构成单参数族的平坦有效连接，其中唯一成员是每个度量的唯一Levi-Civita联络。该连接具有完备测地流，但诱导的伪距离恒为零。

Conclusion: Q-charts上弹性黎曼几何可系统下降，各类结构与性质双向保持；主作用商空间情形下测地线与完备测地流下降，以 $\R$ 为曲线对象的弹性空间类封闭。无理环面的例子表明完备测地流与伪距离非退化并不等价——存在完备测地流而伪距离恒为零的黎曼结构，揭示了微分空间黎曼几何与经典情形的本质差异。

Abstract: Following Miyamoto, we consider Q-charts, a class of subductions $φ\colon X\to Y$ of diffeological spaces along which Blohmann's elasticity descends. We show that the Riemannian apparatus of elastic diffeology---vertical, horizontal and full connections, Riemannian metrics, Levi-Civita connections and geodesics---also descends along Q-charts. More precisely, structures on $Y$ correspond bijectively to structures on $X$ that are invariant under the pseudogroup $Ψ(φ)$ of fibre-preserving transitions, and torsion-freeness, effectivity, flatness, fullness, the Levi-Civita property and the geodesic equation are preserved in both directions. The key input is that the iterated tangent bundles $T^nX$ and their fibre products $T_kX$ are the pullbacks along $φ$ of the corresponding bundles over $Y$.
  For quotients by principal actions of diffeologically discrete groups, we lift geodesics globally, prove that the class of elastic spaces on which $\R$ is a curve object is closed under such quotients, and show that complete geodesic flows descend. As an application, we determine the Riemannian geometry of the irrational tori $\Tor_α=\R/(\Z+α\Z)$ completely. Let $\vartheta$ be the one-form on $\Tor_α$ induced by $dt$ on $\R$. Every Riemannian metric is a positive constant multiple of $\vartheta^2$, and the vertical connections form a one-parameter family of flat effective connections, a single member of which is the unique Levi-Civita connection of every metric. This connection has a complete geodesic flow, yet the induced pseudo-distance vanishes identically.

</details>


### [318] [Rigidity of volume-minimizing free boundary hypersurfaces in high dimensional Riemannian manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01392)
*Sanghun Lee*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 建立了体积最小自由边界超曲面的体积估计与刚性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究$(n+1)$维黎曼流形中自由边界超曲面的体积最小性质，旨在给出其体积估计并刻画等号情形下流形的刚性。

Method: 对稳定极小自由边界超曲面，以$M$的数量曲率与边界$\partial M$的第二基本形式为参数导出体积估计；并在等号成立的情形下证明$M$的刚性结果。

Result: 得到稳定极小自由边界超曲面的体积估计，并在对应等号情形下证明了$M$的刚性结果。

Conclusion: 体积估计与刚性结果建立了自由边界极小超曲面体积与其所在流形容量的联系，等号情形刻画了流形的刚性结构。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish a volume estimate and an associated rigidity result for volume-minimizing free boundary hypersurfaces in an $(n+1)$-dimensional Riemannian manifold $M$. First, we derive a volume estimate for stable minimal free boundary hypersurfaces in terms of the scalar curvature of $M$ and the second fundamental form of the boundary $\partial M$. We then prove rigidity results of $M$ in the corresponding equality cases.

</details>


### [319] [The sphere is the only closed surface satisfying the fixed-width Archimedean property](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01550)
*Mijia Lai*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 论文证明：在$\mathbb{R}^3$中，对于某个固定宽度$h$，若连通光滑闭嵌入曲面在两个平行平面间的区域面积恒为$2\pi h$，则该曲面必为单位球。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Archimedes的球面切片定理（球体两平行截面间面积等于$2\pi h$）是著名结果，但该性质是否唯一刻画单位球此前未被确认。论文旨在回答这一逆问题：对单个固定宽度，该面积性质能否作为单位球的特征。

Method: 考虑$\mathbb{R}^3$中连通、光滑、闭、嵌入的曲面，要求存在固定宽度$h$，使得曲面介于两个与该曲面相交、间距为$h$的平行平面之间的区域面积恒为$2\pi h$，利用这一面积约束并结合几何分析论证。

Result: 证明了该性质在单个固定宽度$h$下即可唯一刻画单位球：满足条件的连通光滑闭嵌入曲面只能是单位球。

Conclusion: Archimedes的切片面积性质对单个固定宽度即构成单位球的刚性特征，给出该经典定理的一个逆定理式刻画。

Abstract: Among Archimedes' many celebrated discoveries is a striking fact: the region of the unit sphere between two parallel planes, both meeting the sphere and separated by a distance $h$, has area $2πh$. We prove that, among connected smooth closed embedded surfaces in $\mathbb R^3$, this property characterizes the unit sphere for a single fixed width.

</details>


### [320] [Solvable Algebraic Generalized Ricci Solitons in low dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01608)
*Lorenzo Mucciante*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了可解李代数上代数广义Ricci孤立子的结构性质，证明了非平凡情形必为扩张型，并在三维及四维可解幺模李代数上完成了分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义Ricci孤立子是Ricci孤立子的推广，理解其代数化（李代数层面）的结构与存在性，有助于刻画相应几何结构并给出低维分类。

Method: 在可解李代数框架下研究代数广义Ricci孤立子，利用结构分析证明其扩张性，并对阿贝尔李代数的一维扩张给出存在性必要条件。

Result: 证明所有非平凡可解代数广义Ricci孤立子必为扩张型；给出阿贝尔李代数一维扩张上存在此类孤立子的必要条件。

Conclusion: 在等距与尺度等价意义下，分类了三维李代数上的代数广义Ricci孤立子，以及四维可解幺模李代数上的代数广义Ricci孤立子。

Abstract: We prove new structural results for algebraic generalized Ricci solitons on solvable Lie algebras and we discuss the existence of examples in low dimensions. We establish that all non-trivial solvable algebraic generalized Ricci solitons must be expanding and give necessary conditions for the existence of such solitons on one-dimensional extensions of abelian Lie algebras. Finally, we classify algebraic generalized Ricci solitons on three-dimensional Lie algebras, and algebraic generalized Ricci solitons on four-dimensional solvable unimodular Lie algebras, up to isometry and scaling.

</details>


### [321] [Finite Time Singularities of the Kähler Ricci Flow on Compact Kähler Surfaces are of Type I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01643)
*Yeyun Xu,Linfeng Zhou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在紧Kähler曲面上、无对称性假设且初始Kähler类不必为有理的情形下，为有限时间体积坍缩的Kähler–Ricci流建立了Type I曲率界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kähler–Ricci流在有限时间发生体积坍缩时的曲率行为一直缺乏有效刻画，而既有结果多依赖对称性假设或初始Kähler类为有理的限制，本文旨在去除这些限制并给出Type I曲率界。

Method: 证明结合局部辛拓扑与梯度Kähler–Ricci收缩孤子分类，并借助Bamler的紧性定理与结构理论获得论证所需的收缩极限。

Result: 在紧Kähler曲面上，对有限时间体积坍缩的Kähler–Ricci流、在无对称性假设且初始Kähler类非有理的情况下，建立了Type I曲率界。

Conclusion: 该结果推广了先前受对称性和有理Kähler类限制的结论，表明体积坍缩情形下曲率仍受Type I控制，所采用的辛拓扑与孤子分类方法具有进一步应用价值。

Abstract: We establish a Type I curvature bound for finite-time volume-collapsing Kähler--Ricci flows on compact Kähler surfaces. No symmetry assumption is imposed, and the initial Kähler class need not be rational. The proof combines local symplectic topology with the classification of gradient Kähler--Ricci shrinking solitons. Bamler's compactness and structure theory provides the shrinking limits used in the argument.

</details>


### [322] [Classification of quadratically pinched translators in higher codimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01713)
*Debora Impera,Michele Rimoldi,Francesco Ruatta*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究任意余维平均曲率流平移孤子的二次拼挤问题，在Andrews-Baker阈值处证明余维数约化定理，并进一步给出刚性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平移孤子的分类与刚性是平均曲率流的重要问题。已有的余维数约化结果多来自古平均曲率流，其拼挤范围在高余维下不够优，尤其在低维情形存在改进空间，且常需熵假设。本文旨在去除熵假设并获得更优的拼挤常数。

Method: 纯椭圆方法：将平移孤子视为加权极小子流形，结合漂移Laplace算子的Simons型恒等式、精细化的梯度估计以及Omori-Yau极大值原理。

Result: 在|B|^2 ≤ (4/(3n)−ε0)|H|^2条件下，证明完备连通平移孤子要么是仿射n-平面，要么|H|>0处处成立且包含于(n+1)维仿射子空间；在更强拼挤|B|^2 ≤ (c_n−ε0)|H|^2（c_n=min{4/(3n),1/(n−2)}，n≥3；n=2时c_n=2/3）下，得到刚性：平移孤子为仿射平面或包含于(n+1)维仿射子空间的bowl孤子。

Conclusion: 该结果在低维改进了由古平均曲率流余维数约化所得拼挤范围，且无需任何熵假设，为平移孤子的分类提供新的刚性刻画。

Abstract: We study complete translating solitons for the mean curvature flow in arbitrary codimension under quadratic pinching of the second fundamental form. We prove a codimension reduction theorem at the Andrews-Baker threshold: if a complete $n$-dimensional connected translator isometrically immersed in $(\mathbb{R}^{n+p},\langle\cdot,\cdot\rangle)$ satisfies \[ |B|^2\leq \left(\frac{4}{3n}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)|H|^2 \] for some $\varepsilon_{0}>0$, then either it is an affine $n$-plane or $|H|>0$ everywhere and the translator is contained in an $(n+1)$-dimensional affine subspace. In particular, this improves in low dimensions the pinching range previously obtained from codimension reduction results for ancient mean curvature flows. Our proof is purely elliptic: viewing translators as weighted minimal submanifolds, we combine Simons-type identities for the drift Laplacian with refined gradient estimates and an Omori-Yau maximum principle. As a consequence, under the stronger pinching condition \[ |B|^2\leq (c_n-\varepsilon_{0})|H|^2,\qquad c_n=\min\left\{\frac{4}{3n},\frac{1}{n-2}\right\} \] for $n\geq3$, with $c_n=2/3$ when $n=2$, we obtain a rigidity theorem: the translator is either an affine plane or a bowl soliton contained in an $(n+1)$-dimensional affine subspace. No entropy assumption is required.

</details>


### [323] [Hessian manifolds of positive constant Hessian sectional curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01737)
*Hakobi Sakamoto*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在正曲率情形下分类了常Hessian截面曲率Hessian流形的标准模型，方法是在Kähler流形上引入Hessian结构并通过阿贝尔Lie群Hamilton作用的商流形实现这些模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hessian流形（即信息几何中的对偶平坦结构）被视为Kähler几何的实类比，其Hessian截面曲率对应Kähler几何中的全纯截面曲率。Furuhata与Kurose（2013）已分类非正曲率情形下的标准模型，但正曲率情形尚待解决，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在配备适当阿贝尔Lie群Hamilton作用的Kähler流形之商流形上引入Hessian结构，并将标准模型实现为此类商流形，从而对正曲率情形下的标准模型进行分类。

Result: 对常Hessian截面曲率Hessian流形在正曲率情形下的标准模型完成了分类，并通过Kähler流形的商流形实现了这些标准模型。

Conclusion: 本文补全了Hessian流形标准模型的分类（正曲率情形），所引入的Kähler商流形方法为研究Hessian几何与Kähler几何的对应关系提供了新途径。

Abstract: Hessian structure on a manifold is a geometric structure consisting of a pair $(\nabla,g)$ of an affine connection $\nabla$ and a Riemannian metric $g$ satisfying certain compatibility conditions. It is also known as a dually flat structure in information geometry. Hessian geometry is considered to be a real analogue of Kähler geometry; in particular, Hessian sectional curvature corresponds to holomorphic sectional curvature in Kähler geometry. The standard models of Hessian manifolds with constant Hessian sectional curvature were classified by Furuhata and Kurose (2013) in the nonpositive curvature case, whereas the positive curvature case is open. In this paper, we classify the standard models in the positive curvature case. For this end, we introduce Hessian structure on quotient manifolds of Kähler manifolds equipped with suitable Hamiltonian actions of abelian Lie groups and realizing the standard models as such quotient manifolds.

</details>


### [324] [Quasi-contact metric structures from Sasaki-Einstein metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02073)
*Beniamino Cappelletti-Montano,Antonio De Nicola*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文首次构造了非接触度量的准接触度量结构，并研究了Killing特征向量场下的Ricci曲率判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准接触度量结构是接触度量结构的自然推广，但其与接触度量结构的本质区别此前缺乏具体例子，尤其在奇维数下的存在性问题有待解决。

Method: 在奇维数≥5时构造非接触度量的准接触度量结构实例；在5维情形下，通过形变Sasaki-Einstein SU(2)-结构，构造带Killing特征向量场的单参数族实例；在任意维数下，研究带Killing特征向量场的准接触度量结构，并推导使其成为K-接触的Ricci曲率判据。

Result: 在一切奇维数≥5中给出了首个非接触度量的准接触度量结构例子；在5维中得到了带Killing特征向量场的单参数族实例，并在S^5上给出紧致齐性例子；在任意维数下建立了使结构成为K-接触的Ricci曲率判据。

Conclusion: 准接触度量结构与接触度量结构确实存在本质差异，该差异在奇维数≥5中普遍存在，并可通过Ricci曲率条件刻画Killing特征向量场情形下二者的联系。

Abstract: We construct the first examples of quasi-contact metric structures that are not contact metric in every odd dimension greater than or equal to $5$. In dimension $5$, we construct a one-parameter family of such examples with a Killing characteristic vector field by deforming Sasaki-Einstein $\mathrm{SU}(2)$-structures. This yields compact, homogeneous examples on $S^5$. In arbitrary dimensions, we investigate quasi-contact metric structures with a Killing characteristic vector field and derive a Ricci curvature criterion for the structure to be K-contact.

</details>


### [325] [The Morse index of prismatic free boundary minimal surfaces in the unit ball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02078)
*Hung Tran*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文精确计算了一类球内嵌入自由边界极小曲面的Morse指标为2b、零度为3，并确定了指标形式的负空间在对称群下的表示结构。所用主要工具是等变意义下面积与能量之间的比较，其中Teichmüller方向借助圆域按对称类型计数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 除赤道圆盘与临界悬链面外，球内自由边界极小曲面的Morse指标此前鲜有精确结果。本文旨在对具有棱柱对称性、亏格为零且含b个边界分支的一大类FBMS精确确定其指标、零度及负空间的表示结构，并给出旋转或多元体对称曲面指标的下界。

Method: 假设曲面Σ⊂Ball为嵌入FBMS、亏格零、含b个边界分支，在阶为4b的棱柱群下不变，其中阶b的旋转循环置换边界分支，赤道面反射保持每个分支；通过有限群表示论进行等变比较，将面积与能量相联，并用圆域按对称类型统计Teichmüller方向，进而确定指标形式的负空间表示。

Result: 证明了Σ的Morse指标为2b，零度为3；并确定指标形式的负空间作为对称群表示的结构。该结果涵盖Karpukhin、Kusner、McGrath、Stern的b-noid，以及大b情形下Folha、Pacard、Zolotareva的亏格零曲面；同时给出具有旋转或多元体对称曲面的指标下界。

Conclusion: 除赤道圆盘与临界悬链面外，这些是该类球内FBMS中首批指标被精确知晓的例子；所发展的等变比较方法将面积-能量比较与表示论结合，为对称极小曲面的指标计算提供了新途径。

Abstract: For integer $b\ge3$, let $\Sig\subset\Ball$ be an embedded free boundary minimal surface (FBMS) of genus zero with $b$ boundary components. Suppose that $\Sig$ is invariant under the prismatic group of order $4b$, the rotation of order $b$ permutes the boundary components cyclically, and the reflection in the equatorial plane preserves each of them. We show that the Morse index of $\Sig$ is $2b$ and its nullity is three; moreover, we determine the negative space of the index form as a representation of the symmetry group. Examples include the $b$-noids of Karpukhin, Kusner, McGrath, and Stern and, for large $b$, the genus-zero surfaces of Folha, Pacard, and Zolotareva. To our knowledge, apart from the equatorial disc and the critical catenoid, these are the first FBMS in the ball whose Morse indices are known precisely. There are also lower bounds for the index of surfaces with rotational or polyhedral symmetry. The main new ingredient is an equivariant comparison, using the representation theory of finite groups, between area and energy, in which the Teichmüller directions are counted by symmetry type using circle domains.

</details>


### [326] [Uniqueness of the soliton vector field of a shrinking gradient Kähler-Ricci soliton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02164)
*Ronan J. Conlon,Alix Deruelle*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了完备收缩梯度Kähler-Ricci孤子的孤子向量场在双全纯映射拉回意义下唯一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 收缩梯度Kähler-Ricci孤子的孤子向量场刻画了度量的刚性结构，其是否由度量本身（在双全纯等价意义下）唯一决定，是理解孤子几何与分类的关键问题。

Method: 对完备收缩梯度Kähler-Ricci孤子进行分析，比较不同孤子向量场，通过对双全纯映射的推前（pushforward）建立向量场之间的等价关系。

Result: 证明了孤子向量场在推前于双全纯映射意义下唯一。

Conclusion: 完备收缩梯度Kähler-Ricci孤子的孤子向量场由孤子结构唯一确定，至多相差一个双全纯映射的推前，从而为孤子的分类与刚性研究提供了基础。

Abstract: We establish the uniqueness of the soliton vector field of a complete shrinking gradient Kähler-Ricci soliton up to pushforward by biholomorphism.

</details>


### [327] [Ahlfors--Weill Extension, Asymptotically Conformal Curves and Epstein--Poincaré Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02171)
*Ming Hong Tee*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文构造了一类局部拟反射，修正Ahlfors的经典估计，证明了渐近共形曲线所围区域的双曲度量与拟双曲度量渐近等价，并借助Epstein曲面给出几何解释，证明互补区域的Epstein-Poincaré曲面在曲线附近任意接近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ahlfors-Weill构造可将Schwarzian范数小于1/2的单叶函数延拓为Riemann球上的拟共形同胚，并可用Epstein曲面几何理解；对渐近共形曲线，单值化映射可延拓到更大的圆盘，且可用Loewner理论验证其拟共形性。作者希望进一步构造相应的局部拟反射，并用它来精细化度量估计与曲面逼近问题。

Method: 1）构造具有上述延拓性质的局部拟反射；2）利用该局部拟反射修正Ahlfors的经典估计；3）结合Epstein曲面对延拓的几何重释，分析互补区域Epstein-Poincaré曲面的接近性。

Result: 由渐近共形曲线所围的区域具有渐近相似的双曲度量与拟双曲度量；其互补区域的Epstein-Poincaré曲面在曲线附近任意接近。

Conclusion: 通过局部拟反射与Ahlfors估计的修正，建立了渐近共形曲线区域中双曲与拟双曲度量的渐近等价性，并从Epstein曲面角度证明了互补区域曲面的局部逼近性，深化了对Ahlfors-Weill构造几何意义的理解。

Abstract: The Ahlfors--Weill construction explicitly extends a univalent function $f$ with Schwarzian norm less than $1/2$ to a quasiconformal homeomorphism of the Riemann sphere, an extension that can be understood geometrically through Epstein surfaces. For asymptotically conformal curves, the uniformization map can be extended to a larger disk, and Loewner theory can be used to verify that this extension is quasiconformal. In this paper, we construct a local quasireflection with such an extension. Using this local quasireflection, we modify classical estimates of Ahlfors and show that domains bounded by asymptotically conformal curves have asymptotically similar hyperbolic and quasihyperbolic metrics. Furthermore, by combining these estimates with a geometric reinterpretation of the extension through Epstein surfaces, we show that the Epstein-Poincaré surfaces of the complementary domains are arbitrarily close to one another near the curve.

</details>
